`backend/smt_solver.py`:

```py
"""Bounded SMT-LIB solving and portable angr constraint snapshots."""
from pathlib import Path
import os
import tempfile
import time

MAX_SCRIPT_BYTES = 4 * 1024 * 1024
ALLOWED_COMMANDS = {
    'set-logic', 'set-info', 'declare-const', 'declare-fun', 'declare-sort',
    'define-fun', 'define-sort', 'assert', 'check-sat', 'get-model',
}


def validate_script(script):
    """Accept declarative SMT-LIB only; resource options belong to the runner.

    The scanner handles strings, quoted symbols and comments, while Z3 performs
    syntax/type validation. Commands such as reset, pop and set-option cannot
    alter the assertion set or bypass the worker's limits.
    """
    if not isinstance(script, str) or not script.strip():
        raise ValueError('Provide nonempty SMT-LIB declarations and assertions')
    if len(script.encode('utf-8')) > MAX_SCRIPT_BYTES:
        raise ValueError('SMT-LIB exceeds 4 MiB')
    depth, index, commands = 0, 0, []
    while index < len(script):
        char = script[index]
        if char == ';':
            end = script.find('\n', index)
            index = len(script) if end < 0 else end + 1
            continue
        if char in ('"', '|'):
            if depth == 0:
                raise ValueError('Expected a parenthesized SMT-LIB command')
            quote = char
            index += 1
            while index < len(script):
                if script[index] == quote:
                    if quote == '"' and index + 1 < len(script) and script[index + 1] == '"':
                        index += 2
                        continue
                    break
                index += 1
            if index == len(script):
                raise ValueError('Unterminated SMT-LIB string or quoted symbol')
        elif char == '(':
            if depth == 0:
                start = index + 1
                while start < len(script) and script[start].isspace():
                    start += 1
                end = start
                while end < len(script) and not script[end].isspace() and script[end] not in '();':
                    end += 1
                command = script[start:end]
                if command not in ALLOWED_COMMANDS:
                    raise ValueError('Unsupported SMT-LIB command: ' + command)
                commands.append(command)
            depth += 1
        elif char == ')':
            depth -= 1
            if depth < 0:
                raise ValueError('Unbalanced SMT-LIB parentheses')
        elif depth == 0 and not char.isspace():
            raise ValueError('Expected a parenthesized SMT-LIB command')
        index += 1
    if depth:
        raise ValueError('Unbalanced SMT-LIB parentheses')
    return tuple(commands)


def scalar_symbols(assertions):
    """Collect scalar terms and reserve all free symbol names for trackers."""
    import z3
    variables, declarations, reserved, seen, todo = {}, {}, set(), set(), list(assertions)
    while todo:
        node = todo.pop()
        if node.get_id() in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(node.get_id())
        if z3.is_app(node) and node.decl().kind() == z3.Z3_OP_UNINTERPRETED:
            name = str(node.decl().name())
            if name in declarations and not declarations[name].eq(node.decl()):
                raise ValueError('Overloaded SMT symbol %r cannot be displayed unambiguously; use distinct names' % name)
            declarations[name] = node.decl()
            reserved.add(name)
        if z3.is_const(node) and node.decl().kind() == z3.Z3_OP_UNINTERPRETED:
            if node.sort().kind() in (z3.Z3_BV_SORT, z3.Z3_BOOL_SORT, z3.Z3_INT_SORT):
                variables[str(node)] = node
        todo.extend(node.children())
    if len(variables) > 16384:
        raise ValueError('SMT model has more than 16384 scalar variables')
    return variables, reserved


def model_values(model, variables, excluded=()):
    """Render one joint model, preserving full bit-vector width and byte order."""
    import z3
    values, other_values = {}, {}
    for name, variable in sorted(variables.items()):
        value = model.eval(variable, model_completion=True)
        item = {'value': str(value)}
        if z3.is_bv_value(value):
            bits = value.size()
            item.update(bits=bits, unsigned=str(value.as_long()),
                        hex=format(value.as_long(), '0%dx' % ((bits + 3) // 4)))
        values[name] = item
    for declaration in model.decls():
        name = str(declaration.name())
        if name not in variables and name not in excluded:
            value = str(model[declaration])
            other_values[name] = {'value': value[:16384], 'truncated': len(value) > 16384}
    return values, other_values


def check_predicate(script, assertions, expression, started, timeout):
    """Prove a Boolean condition under explicit assumptions without vacuity."""
    import z3
    if not isinstance(expression, str) or not expression.strip() or len(expression.encode('utf-8')) > 65536:
        raise ValueError('predicate must be a nonempty SMT-LIB Boolean expression up to 64 KiB')
    if validate_script('(assert ' + expression + ')') != ('assert',):
        raise ValueError('predicate must contain exactly one Boolean expression')
    augmented = script + '\n(assert ' + expression + ')\n'
    validate_script(augmented)
    parsed = z3.parse_smt2_string(augmented)
    if len(parsed) != len(assertions) + 1:
        raise ValueError('predicate must contain exactly one Boolean expression')
    predicate = parsed[-1]
    variables, _ = scalar_symbols([*assertions, predicate])
    solver = z3.Solver()
    solver.add(assertions)

    def check(condition=None):
        remaining = int((timeout - (time.monotonic() - started)) * 1000)
        if remaining <= 0:
            return {'status': 'unknown', 'reason_unknown': 'shared time budget exhausted'}
        solver.set(timeout=max(1, remaining))
        solver.push()
        try:
            if condition is not None:
                solver.add(condition)
            answer = solver.check()
            outcome = {'status': str(answer)}
            if answer == z3.unknown:
                outcome['reason_unknown'] = solver.reason_unknown()
            elif answer == z3.sat and condition is not None:
                values, other = model_values(solver.model(), variables)
                outcome.update(witness=values, other_model_values=other)
            return outcome
        finally:
            solver.pop()

    checks = {'assumptions': check(), 'when_true': {'status': 'not_run'},
              'when_false': {'status': 'not_run'}}
    classification = 'inconclusive'
    if checks['assumptions']['status'] == 'unsat':
        classification = 'unreachable'
    elif checks['assumptions']['status'] == 'sat':
        checks['when_true'] = check(predicate)
        checks['when_false'] = check(z3.Not(predicate))
        statuses = (checks['when_true']['status'], checks['when_false']['status'])
        classification = {('sat', 'unsat'): 'always_true', ('unsat', 'sat'): 'always_false',
                          ('sat', 'sat'): 'both_feasible'}.get(statuses, 'inconclusive')
    return {'status': 'unknown' if classification == 'inconclusive' else classification,
            'analysis': 'predicate', 'classification': classification, 'checks': checks,
            'predicate': expression, 'assertions': len(assertions),
            'scalar_variables': len(variables), 'elapsed': round(time.monotonic() - started, 3),
            'z3_version': z3.get_version_string(),
            'warnings': ['Verdict applies only to the supplied SMT-LIB assumptions and expression.',
                         'Native branch equivalence requires correct widths, signedness and modeled state.']}


def solve_smt(req):
    import z3
    script = req.get('smtlib', '')
    validate_script(script)
    timeout = int(req.get('timeout', 60))
    maximum = int(req.get('max_models', 1))
    if not 1 <= timeout <= 86400 or not 1 <= maximum <= 16:
        raise ValueError('timeout must be 1..86400 seconds; max_models must be 1..16')
    started = time.monotonic()
    assertions = z3.parse_smt2_string(script)
    if 'predicate' in req:
        return check_predicate(script, assertions, req['predicate'], started, timeout)
    solver = z3.Solver()
    variables, reserved = scalar_symbols(assertions)
    tags = {}
    for index, assertion in enumerate(assertions):
        # FreshBool's counter does not account for names created by the parser.
        # Tracking labels are assumptions, so a collision would change semantics.
        tag = z3.FreshBool('arsenal_assertion')
        while str(tag) in reserved:
            tag = z3.FreshBool('arsenal_assertion')
        reserved.add(str(tag))
        tags[str(tag)] = index
        solver.assert_and_track(assertion, tag)
    models, other_models, status, reason = [], [], 'unknown', ''
    for _ in range(maximum):
        remaining = int((timeout - (time.monotonic() - started)) * 1000)
        if remaining <= 0:
            status, reason = 'unknown', 'time budget exhausted'
            break
        solver.set(timeout=max(1, remaining))
        answer = solver.check()
        status = str(answer)
        if answer == z3.unknown:
            reason = solver.reason_unknown()
            break
        if answer == z3.unsat:
            break
        model = solver.model()
        values, other_values = model_values(model, variables, tags)
        blockers = [variable != model.eval(variable, model_completion=True) for variable in variables.values()]
        models.append(values)
        other_models.append(other_values)
        if not blockers:
            break
        solver.add(z3.Or(blockers))
    result = {
        'status': 'sat' if models else status, 'models': models,
        'other_model_values': other_models,
        'enumeration_status': status, 'reason_unknown': reason,
        'assertions': len(assertions), 'scalar_variables': len(variables),
        'elapsed': round(time.monotonic() - started, 3), 'z3_version': z3.get_version_string(),
        'statistics': {key: solver.statistics().get_key_value(key) for key in solver.statistics().keys()},
        'warnings': ['Models satisfy the supplied assertions; native behavior requires concrete verification.',
                     'Only Boolean, integer and bit-vector variables occurring in assertions are enumerated. Other model values are shown separately.'],
    }
    if not models and status == 'unsat':
        result['unsat_core_assertions'] = sorted(tags[str(tag)] for tag in solver.unsat_core())
        result['warnings'].append('The unsat core is sufficient for a contradiction, not necessarily minimal.')
    return result


def export_state(state, symbols, specs, destination, reached_goal=False):
    """Export one exact path's constraints; a frontier snapshot is not a goal."""
    path = Path(destination)
    if path.suffix != '.smt2':
        raise ValueError('Constraint snapshot path must end in .smt2')
    if path.exists():
        with path.open('rb') as existing:
            if not existing.read(20).startswith(b'; Arsenal snapshot:'):
                raise ValueError('Refusing to overwrite a file that is not an Arsenal snapshot')
    try:
        from angr import claripy
    except ImportError:
        import claripy
    constraints = list(state.solver.constraints)
    if len(constraints) > 20000:
        raise ValueError('Constraint snapshot exceeds 20000 assertions')
    solver = claripy.Solver()
    solver.add(constraints)
    aliases = []
    for index, (symbol, spec) in enumerate(zip(symbols, specs)):
        alias = claripy.BVS('arsenal_export_buffer_%d' % index, symbol.size())
        solver.add(alias == symbol)
        aliases.append({'name': spec['name'], 'symbol': alias.args[0], 'length': spec['length']})
    if hasattr(solver, 'to_smt2'):
        script = solver.to_smt2()
    else:
        import z3
        converted = [claripy.backends.z3.convert(c) for c in solver.constraints]
        native = z3.Solver(ctx=converted[0].ctx if converted else None)
        native.add(converted)
        script = native.to_smt2()
    kind = 'reached goal' if reached_goal else 'partial path; goal NOT asserted'
    script = '; Arsenal snapshot: ' + kind + '\n' + script
    if len(script.encode('utf-8')) > MAX_SCRIPT_BYTES:
        raise ValueError('Constraint snapshot exceeds 4 MiB')
    fd, temporary = tempfile.mkstemp(prefix=path.name + '.', dir=path.parent)
    try:
        with os.fdopen(fd, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as stream:
            stream.write(script)
        os.replace(temporary, path)
    finally:
        if os.path.exists(temporary):
            os.unlink(temporary)
    return {'path': str(path), 'reached_goal': reached_goal, 'address': hex(state.addr),
            'assertions': len(constraints), 'inputs': aliases,
            'scope': 'One path only; other active paths are not included.'}

```

`backend/solve.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Isolated, bounded angr worker. Usage: solve.py REQUEST.json RESULT.json.

Addresses are absolute in the requested base_addr coordinate system. No binary is
executed natively. Parent watchdog terminates the worker even during solver calls.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import re
from pathlib import Path
import signal
import subprocess
import sys
import time
import traceback
from collections import Counter


def integer(value):
    return int(value, 0) if isinstance(value, str) else int(value)


def bounded_int(req, key, default, low, high):
    value = integer(req.get(key, default))
    if not low <= value <= high:
        raise ValueError(f'{key} must be between {low} and {high}')
    return value


def input_specs(req):
    """Validate named inputs before loading a binary; legacy requests keep defaults."""
    if 'inputs' not in req:
        return [dict(name='input1', length=bounded_int(req, 'length', 16, 1, 65536),
                     printable=req.get('printable', True), prefix=req.get('prefix', ''),
                     suffix=req.get('suffix', ''), byte_constraints=req.get('byte_constraints', []))]
    raw = req['inputs']
    if not isinstance(raw, list) or not 1 <= len(raw) <= 32:
        raise ValueError('inputs must be a nonempty list with at most 32 entries')
    specs, names, total = [], set(), 0
    for item in raw:
        if not isinstance(item, dict):
            raise ValueError('Each input must be an object')
        name = item.get('name')
        if not isinstance(name, str) or not re.fullmatch(r'[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]{0,63}', name):
            raise ValueError('Input name must be an identifier of at most 64 characters')
        if name in names:
            raise ValueError(f'Duplicate input name: {name}')
        names.add(name)
        if 'length' not in item or isinstance(item['length'], bool) or not isinstance(item['length'], (str, int)):
            raise ValueError(f'{name}: length must be an integer')
        length = bounded_int(item, 'length', 16, 1, 65536)
        total += length
        if total > 65536:
            raise ValueError('Combined input length must not exceed 65536 bytes')
        printable = item.get('printable', req.get('printable', True))
        if not isinstance(printable, bool):
            raise ValueError(f'{name}: printable must be a boolean')
        spec = dict(name=name, length=length, printable=printable)
        for field in ('prefix', 'suffix'):
            value = item.get(field, '')
            if not isinstance(value, str) or len(value.encode('utf-8')) > length:
                raise ValueError(f'{name}: {field} must be text fitting the input length')
            spec[field] = value
        constraints = item.get('byte_constraints', [])
        if not isinstance(constraints, list) or any(not isinstance(c, dict) for c in constraints):
            raise ValueError(f'{name}: byte_constraints must be a list of objects')
        for constraint in constraints:
            offset = constraint.get('offset')
            if isinstance(offset, bool) or not isinstance(offset, (int, str)) or not 0 <= integer(offset) < length:
                raise ValueError(f'{name}: byte_constraints offset outside input')
            for key in ('value', 'min', 'max'):
                if key in constraint:
                    value = constraint[key]
                    if isinstance(value, bool) or not isinstance(value, (int, str)) or not 0 <= integer(value) <= 255:
                        raise ValueError(f'{name}: byte constraint value must be 0..255')
        spec['byte_constraints'] = constraints
        specs.append(spec)
    return specs


def filesystem_specs(req):
    """Validate concrete in-memory files for challenge assets read at runtime."""
    raw = req.get('files', [])
    if not isinstance(raw, list) or len(raw) > 32:
        raise ValueError('files must be a list with at most 32 entries')
    files, paths, total = [], set(), 0
    for item in raw:
        if not isinstance(item, dict):
            raise ValueError('Each file mapping must be an object')
        path, encoded = item.get('path'), item.get('hex')
        if not isinstance(path, str) or not path.startswith('/') or '\0' in path or len(path) > 4096:
            raise ValueError('Mapped file path must be an absolute path of at most 4096 characters')
        if path in paths:
            raise ValueError(f'Duplicate mapped file path: {path}')
        paths.add(path)
        if not isinstance(encoded, str) or len(encoded) > 32 * 1024 * 1024:
            raise ValueError(f'{path}: hex content must be a string of at most 16 MiB')
        try:
            content = bytes.fromhex(encoded)
        except ValueError as exc:
            raise ValueError(f'{path}: invalid hex file content') from exc
        total += len(content)
        if total > 64 * 1024 * 1024:
            raise ValueError('Combined mapped file content must not exceed 64 MiB')
        files.append((path, content))
    return files


def symbolic_input(claripy, spec, name, argv=False):
    """Use narrow symbolic bits for constrained byte ranges.

    This is equivalent to the existing byte constraints, but avoids carrying
    irrelevant high bits when a challenge input has a limited alphabet.
    """
    length = spec['length']
    domains = [[0, 255] for _ in range(length)]
    if spec['printable']:
        domains = [[0x20, 0x7e] for _ in range(length)]

    for field, at_end in (('prefix', False), ('suffix', True)):
        encoded = spec[field].encode('utf-8')
        offset = length - len(encoded) if at_end else 0
        for index, value in enumerate(encoded, offset):
            domains[index] = [value, value]

    for constraint in spec['byte_constraints']:
        index = integer(constraint['offset'])
        low, high = domains[index]
        if 'value' in constraint:
            low = max(low, integer(constraint['value']))
            high = min(high, integer(constraint['value']))
        if 'min' in constraint:
            low = max(low, integer(constraint['min']))
        if 'max' in constraint:
            high = min(high, integer(constraint['max']))
        domains[index] = [low, high]

    if argv:
        for domain in domains:
            domain[0] = max(domain[0], 1)
    byte_values = []
    for index, (low, high) in enumerate(domains):
        if low > high:
            # Preserve the original constraints so the normal unsat result
            # explains conflicting prefixes, alphabets, and byte bounds.
            return claripy.BVS(name, length * 8)
        if low == high:
            byte_values.append(claripy.BVV(low, 8))
        elif low < 0x80 <= high:
            # Claripy's simplifier can narrow comparisons against an offset
            # expression across the signed-byte boundary. Keep that case as a
            # full byte; the explicit unsigned constraints below define it.
            byte_values.append(claripy.BVS(f'{name}_byte_{index}', 8))
        else:
            width = max(1, (high - low).bit_length())
            offset = claripy.BVS(f'{name}_byte_{index}', width)
            offset = claripy.ZeroExt(8 - width, offset) if width < 8 else offset
            byte_values.append(offset if low == 0 else offset + claripy.BVV(low, 8))
    return claripy.Concat(*byte_values)


def call_input_name(arg, specs):
    name = arg.get('input')
    if name is None:
        if len(specs) != 1:
            raise ValueError('Buffer call argument requires an input name with multiple inputs')
        return specs[0]['name']
    if not isinstance(name, str) or name not in {item['name'] for item in specs}:
        raise ValueError(f'Unknown buffer input selector: {name!r}')
    return name


def save(path, result):
    path = Path(path)
    temp = path.with_name(path.name + f'.{os.getpid()}.tmp')
    temp.write_text(json.dumps(result, indent=2), encoding='utf-8')
    os.replace(temp, path)


def concrete_stdout(state):
    """Literal stdout goals only: never select an arbitrary symbolic output model."""
    output = bytearray()
    for data, size in state.posix.stdout.content:
        if not state.solver.unique(size):
            raise ValueError("stdout goal has symbolic output length; use find/avoid addresses instead")
        count = state.solver.eval(size)
        if not count:
            continue
        payload = data[data.size() - 1:data.size() - count * 8]
        if not state.solver.unique(payload):
            raise ValueError("stdout goal depends on symbolic output; use find/avoid addresses instead")
        output.extend(state.solver.eval(payload, cast_to=bytes))
    return bytes(output)


def install_common_models(project, claripy):
    """Replace unresolved comparison stubs with constraint-preserving models.

    angr has a SimProcedure for memcmp on many versions, but bcmp is often
    left as an unconstrained extern when libraries are disabled.  That is
    dangerous for path solving: a symbolic return lets both sides of every
    comparison survive and the solver loses the program's actual predicate.
    """
    import angr
    from angr.procedures.libc.strlen import strlen as OriginalStrlen
    from angr.procedures.libc.strcmp import strcmp as OriginalStrcmp
    from angr.procedures.libc.strncmp import strncmp as OriginalStrncmp
    from angr.procedures.posix.mmap import mmap as OriginalMmap
    from cle.backends.externs.simdata.io_file import io_file_data_for_arch
    from angr.storage.memory_mixins.address_concretization_mixin import MultiwriteAnnotation
    try:
        from .libc_models import GlibcRandomState, glibc_random_import, linux_anonymous_mmap_supported
    except ImportError:
        try:
            from backend.libc_models import GlibcRandomState, glibc_random_import, linux_anonymous_mmap_supported
        except ImportError:
            from libc_models import GlibcRandomState, glibc_random_import, linux_anonymous_mmap_supported

    def known_argv_length(state, address):
        lengths = state.globals.get("arsenal_argv_lengths", {})
        if not state.solver.unique(address):
            return None
        pointer = state.solver.eval(address)
        if pointer not in lengths:
            return None
        expected = lengths[pointer]
        terminator = state.memory.load(pointer + expected, 1)
        contents = state.memory.load(pointer, expected) if expected else None
        nonzero = [byte != 0 for byte in contents.chop(8)] if contents is not None else []
        intact = claripy.And(terminator == 0, *nonzero)
        if state.solver.satisfiable(extra_constraints=[claripy.Not(intact)]):
            return None
        return expected

    def concrete_string_length(state, address, budget):
        if not state.solver.unique(address):
            return None
        pointer = state.solver.eval(address)
        for offset in range(budget):
            byte = state.memory.load(pointer + offset, 1)
            if not state.solver.unique(byte):
                return None
            if state.solver.eval(byte) == 0:
                return offset
        return None

    class BoundedStrcmp(angr.SimProcedure):
        def run(self, a_addr, b_addr, wchar=False, ignore_case=False):
            if wchar or ignore_case:
                return OriginalStrcmp.run(self, a_addr, b_addr, wchar=wchar,
                                          ignore_case=ignore_case)
            budget = 4096
            a_len = known_argv_length(self.state, a_addr)
            b_len = known_argv_length(self.state, b_addr)
            if a_len is None:
                a_len = concrete_string_length(self.state, a_addr, budget)
            if b_len is None:
                b_len = concrete_string_length(self.state, b_addr, budget)
            if a_len is None or b_len is None or max(a_len, b_len) + 1 > budget:
                return OriginalStrcmp.run(self, a_addr, b_addr, wchar=False,
                                          ignore_case=False)
            return _string_compare(self.state, claripy, a_addr, b_addr,
                                   max(a_len, b_len) + 1)

    class BoundedStrncmp(angr.SimProcedure):
        def run(self, a_addr, b_addr, limit, a_len=None, b_len=None,
                wchar=False, ignore_case=False):
            if wchar or ignore_case or not self.state.solver.unique(limit):
                return OriginalStrncmp.run(self, a_addr, b_addr, limit,
                                           a_len=a_len, b_len=b_len, wchar=wchar,
                                           ignore_case=ignore_case)
            count = self.state.solver.eval(limit)
            if count > 4096:
                return OriginalStrncmp.run(self, a_addr, b_addr, limit,
                                           a_len=a_len, b_len=b_len, wchar=False,
                                           ignore_case=False)
            if count == 0:
                return claripy.BVV(0, self.state.arch.sizeof['int'])
            return _string_compare(self.state, claripy, a_addr, b_addr, count)

    class ByteCompare(angr.SimProcedure):
        def run(self, left, right, count):
            solver = self.state.solver
            if not solver.unique(count):
                raise angr.errors.SimProcedureError("bcmp requires a uniquely bounded length")
            size = solver.eval(count)
            if size < 0 or size > 65536:
                raise angr.errors.SimProcedureError("bcmp length exceeds model budget")
            if size == 0:
                return claripy.BVV(0, self.state.arch.bits)
            lhs = self.state.memory.load(left, size, endness="Iend_BE")
            rhs = self.state.memory.load(right, size, endness="Iend_BE")
            equal = lhs == rhs
            return claripy.If(equal, claripy.BVV(0, self.state.arch.bits),
                              claripy.BVV(1, self.state.arch.bits))

    class KnownArgvStrlen(angr.SimProcedure):
        def run(self, string):
            lengths = self.state.globals.get("arsenal_argv_lengths", {})
            if not self.state.solver.symbolic(string):
                address = self.state.solver.eval(string)
                if address in lengths:
                    expected = lengths[address]
                    # Only use the shortcut while the modeled bytes still
                    # have their original NUL terminator.  Program code may
                    # mutate argv in place before asking for its length.
                    terminator = self.state.memory.load(address + expected, 1)
                    bytes_before = self.state.memory.load(address, expected)
                    nonzero = [byte != 0 for byte in bytes_before.chop(8)]
                    intact = claripy.And(terminator == 0, *nonzero)
                    if not self.state.solver.satisfiable(extra_constraints=[claripy.Not(intact)]):
                        return claripy.BVV(expected, self.state.arch.bits)
            # Delegate unrelated strings to angr's normal bounded model.  An
            # unconstrained fallback here would manufacture path branches in
            # runtime code such as getenv and locale initialization.
            return OriginalStrlen.run(self, string)

    class TrackedFgets(angr.SimProcedure):
        def read_count(self, dst, count, file_ptr):
            """Consume through the first newline without constraining later bytes."""
            fd_offset = io_file_data_for_arch(self.state.arch)['fd']
            descriptor = self.state.mem[file_ptr + fd_offset:].int.resolved
            simfd = self.state.posix.get_fd(descriptor)
            if simfd is None:
                return -1
            storage = simfd.read_storage
            if not isinstance(storage, angr.SimFile):
                raise angr.errors.SimProcedureError(
                    'fgets requires byte-addressable file storage; packet streams are not modeled')
            if angr.options.CONCRETIZE_SYMBOLIC_FILE_READ_SIZES in self.state.options:
                raise angr.errors.SimProcedureError(
                    'fgets cannot preserve line lengths with symbolic read-size concretization enabled')
            position = simfd.read_pos
            capacity = claripy.BVV(count - 1, self.state.arch.bits)
            if storage.has_end:
                remaining = claripy.If(claripy.UGE(position, storage.size),
                                       claripy.BVV(0, self.state.arch.bits), storage.size - position)
                capacity = claripy.If(claripy.ULT(remaining, capacity), remaining, capacity)
            scan_limit = self.state.solver.max(capacity)
            if scan_limit > 4096:
                raise angr.errors.SimProcedureError('fgets read exceeds the 4096-byte model budget')
            if scan_limit == 0:
                return claripy.BVV(0, self.state.arch.bits)
            peek = storage.load(position, scan_limit)
            amount = capacity
            for offset in range(scan_limit - 1, -1, -1):
                amount = claripy.If(
                    claripy.And(claripy.ULT(claripy.BVV(offset, capacity.size()), capacity),
                                peek.get_byte(offset) == 10),
                    claripy.BVV(offset + 1, capacity.size()), amount)
            data, actual = simfd.read_data(amount)
            self.state.memory.store(dst, data, size=actual)
            end = (dst + actual).annotate(MultiwriteAnnotation())
            self.state.memory.store(end, b'\0')
            return actual

        def run(self, dst, size, file_ptr):
            # The internal reader returns a count. C fgets returns the
            # destination pointer, or NULL when no bytes could be read.
            # Reject an unknown bound instead of guessing a size or discarding
            # feasible negative/zero cases with an added constraint.
            if not self.state.solver.unique(size):
                raise angr.errors.SimProcedureError('fgets requires a uniquely bounded size')
            count = self.state.solver.eval(size) & 0xffffffff
            if count == 0 or count & 0x80000000:
                return claripy.BVV(0, self.state.arch.bits)
            if count == 1:
                self.state.memory.store(dst, b'\0')
                result = 0
            else:
                previous_first_byte = self.state.memory.load(dst, 1)
                result = self.read_count(dst, count, file_ptr)
                # If the read length is symbolic, writing its terminator also
                # touches dst on the EOF alternative. Preserve the original
                # byte on that alternative, as required by the C contract.
                if isinstance(result, int):
                    if result == 0:
                        self.state.memory.store(dst, previous_first_byte)
                else:
                    self.state.memory.store(dst, claripy.If(
                        result == 0, previous_first_byte,
                        self.state.memory.load(dst, 1)))
            if self.state.solver.unique(dst):
                pointer = self.state.solver.eval(dst)
                bound = min(count - 1, 4095)
                if isinstance(result, int):
                    if result >= 0:
                        bound = min(result, bound)
                elif self.state.solver.unique(result):
                    bound = min(self.state.solver.eval(result), bound)
                bounds = dict(self.state.globals.get('arsenal_fgets_bounds', {}))
                bounds[pointer] = bound
                self.state.globals['arsenal_fgets_bounds'] = bounds
            if count == 1:
                return dst
            if isinstance(result, int):
                return dst if result > 0 else claripy.BVV(0, self.state.arch.bits)
            return claripy.If(claripy.And(result != 0, result != -1), dst,
                              claripy.BVV(0, self.state.arch.bits))

    class BoundedStrcspn(angr.SimProcedure):
        def run(self, string, reject):
            budget = 4096
            reject_len = concrete_string_length(self.state, reject, budget)
            if reject_len is None:
                raise angr.errors.SimProcedureError('strcspn reject set must be concrete and bounded')
            reject_values = {self.state.solver.eval(self.state.memory.load(reject + i, 1))
                             for i in range(reject_len)}

            bound = None
            if self.state.solver.unique(string):
                pointer = self.state.solver.eval(string)
                tracked = self.state.globals.get('arsenal_fgets_bounds', {}).get(pointer)
                if tracked is not None:
                    bound = min(tracked + 1, budget)
            if bound is None:
                length = concrete_string_length(self.state, string, budget)
                if length is not None:
                    bound = length + 1
            if bound is None:
                bound = budget

            result = claripy.BVV(budget, self.state.arch.bits)
            active = claripy.BoolV(True)
            for offset in range(budget):
                if offset == bound and not self.state.solver.satisfiable(extra_constraints=[active]):
                    break
                byte = self.state.memory.load(string + offset, 1)
                stop = claripy.Or(byte == 0, *(byte == value for value in reject_values))
                result = claripy.If(claripy.And(active, stop),
                                    claripy.BVV(offset, result.size()), result)
                active = claripy.And(active, claripy.Not(stop))
                if claripy.is_false(active):
                    break
            # A prior fgets bound can become stale after a buffer is changed.
            # Continue up to the hard budget whenever that hint is insufficient.
            if self.state.solver.satisfiable(extra_constraints=[active]):
                raise angr.errors.SimProcedureError('strcspn exceeded the 4096-byte model budget')
            return result

    class LinuxAnonymousMmap(OriginalMmap):
        """Honor Linux's rule that fd is ignored for MAP_ANONYMOUS mappings."""
        def run(self, addr, length, prot, flags, fd, offset):
            if self.state.solver.unique(flags) and self.state.solver.eval(flags) & 0x20:
                fd = claripy.BVV(-1, self.state.arch.bits)
            return super().run(addr, length, prot, flags, fd, offset)

    class GnuSrand(angr.SimProcedure):
        def run(self, seed):
            if not self.state.has_plugin('arsenal_glibc_random'):
                self.state.register_plugin('arsenal_glibc_random', GlibcRandomState())
            self.state.get_plugin('arsenal_glibc_random').seed(seed)

    class GnuRand(angr.SimProcedure):
        def run(self):
            if not self.state.has_plugin('arsenal_glibc_random'):
                self.state.register_plugin('arsenal_glibc_random', GlibcRandomState())
            value = self.state.get_plugin('arsenal_glibc_random').draw()
            return value.zero_extend(self.arch.sizeof['int'] - value.size())

    class GnuRandom(GnuRand):
        def run(self):
            # glibc random() shares the same state but returns a C long.
            value = super().run()
            return value.zero_extend(self.arch.bits - value.size())

    class UnsupportedRandomState(angr.SimProcedure):
        def run(self, *args):
            raise angr.errors.SimProcedureError(
                'glibc initstate/setstate changes the random generator state layout; '
                'this operation is not modeled')

    for name, model in (("bcmp", ByteCompare), ("strlen", KnownArgvStrlen),
                        ("strcmp", BoundedStrcmp), ("strncmp", BoundedStrncmp),
                        ("mmap", LinuxAnonymousMmap), ("fgets", TrackedFgets),
                        ("strcspn", BoundedStrcspn), ("srand", GnuSrand),
                        ("rand", GnuRand), ("srandom", GnuSrand),
                        ("random", GnuRandom), ("initstate", UnsupportedRandomState),
                        ("setstate", UnsupportedRandomState)):
        imported = project.loader.main_object.imports.get(name)
        if name == 'mmap' and not linux_anonymous_mmap_supported(project):
            continue
        if name in ('srand', 'rand', 'srandom', 'random', 'initstate', 'setstate') and not glibc_random_import(project, imported):
            continue
        resolved = imported.resolvedby if imported is not None else None
        # A symbol with this spelling can be user code. Only replace an actual
        # unresolved external import; never a main-object implementation.
        if resolved is not None and resolved.owner is project.loader.extern_object:
            project.hook(resolved.rebased_addr, model(), replace=True)


def _string_compare(state, claripy, left, right, count):
    """Return the first unsigned-byte difference, stopping at a NUL byte."""
    int_bits = state.arch.sizeof['int']
    result = claripy.BVV(0, int_bits)
    continuing = claripy.BoolV(True)
    for offset in range(count):
        lhs = state.memory.load(left + offset, 1)
        rhs = state.memory.load(right + offset, 1)
        difference = claripy.ZeroExt(int_bits - 8, lhs) - claripy.ZeroExt(int_bits - 8, rhs)
        result = claripy.If(claripy.And(continuing, lhs != rhs), difference, result)
        continuing = claripy.And(continuing, lhs == rhs, lhs != 0)
        if state.solver.is_false(continuing):
            break
    return result


def validate_target_conditions(conditions, arch, find):
    """Reject malformed or truncating goals before any expensive exploration."""
    if not isinstance(conditions, list) or len(conditions) > 64:
        raise ValueError('find_conditions must be a list of at most 64 predicates')
    if conditions and not find:
        raise ValueError('find_conditions require find addresses')
    for item in conditions:
        if not isinstance(item, dict):
            raise ValueError('Each find condition must be an object')
        if set(item) == {'register', 'value'}:
            name = item['register']
            if not isinstance(name, str) or name not in arch.registers:
                raise ValueError('Unknown goal register: %r' % name)
            bits = arch.registers[name][1] * 8
            value = integer(item['value'])
            if not -(1 << (bits - 1)) <= value < (1 << bits):
                raise ValueError('Goal value does not fit %d-bit register %s' % (bits, name))
        elif set(item) == {'memory'}:
            spec = item['memory']
            if not isinstance(spec, dict) or set(spec) != {'address', 'hex'}:
                raise ValueError('Memory goal requires address and hex')
            address, data = integer(spec['address']), bytes.fromhex(spec['hex'])
            if not 1 <= len(data) <= 65536 or not 0 <= address < (1 << arch.bits) or address + len(data) > (1 << arch.bits):
                raise ValueError('Memory goal range or length is invalid')
        else:
            raise ValueError('Goal must specify register/value or memory/address/hex')


def target_conditions_hold(state, conditions, project, claripy):
    """Evaluate optional predicates attached to a find address."""
    constraints = []
    for item in conditions:
        if not isinstance(item, dict):
            return False
        if "register" in item:
            name = item["register"]
            if name not in project.arch.registers:
                return False
            value = integer(item.get("value", 0))
            reg = getattr(state.regs, name)
            constraints.append(reg == value)
        elif "memory" in item:
            spec = item["memory"]
            address = integer(spec["address"])
            data = bytes.fromhex(spec["hex"])
            actual = state.memory.load(address, len(data), endness="Iend_BE")
            constraints.append(actual == claripy.BVV(data))
        else:
            return False
    if not state.solver.satisfiable(extra_constraints=constraints):
        return False
    state.add_constraints(*constraints)
    return True


def native_material_assist(req, specs):
    """Derive a candidate from an explicit, strong native-analysis hint.

    This is deliberately narrow and provenance-preserving.  It is a fallback
    for cases where whole-program symbolic execution cannot model a language
    runtime; it is never mixed into an ordinary angr result.
    """
    if not req.get('hybrid_assist') or not req.get('native_hints') or len(specs) != 1:
        return None
    binary = req.get('binary')
    if (req.get('mode') != 'argv' or req.get('find') or req.get('avoid')
            or not req.get('stdout_find') or not isinstance(binary, str)
            or not Path(binary).expanduser().is_file()):
        return None
    hint = req['native_hints'][0]
    if hint.get('algorithm') != 'RC4':
        return None
    try:
        key = bytes.fromhex(hint['key_hex'])
        data = bytes.fromhex(hint['data_hex'])
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return None
    if not 1 <= len(key) <= 256 or len(data) != specs[0]['length']:
        return None
    state = list(range(256))
    j = 0
    for i in range(256):
        j = (j + state[i] + key[i % len(key)]) & 255
        state[i], state[j] = state[j], state[i]
    i = j = 0
    output = bytearray()
    for value in data:
        i = (i + 1) & 255
        j = (j + state[i]) & 255
        state[i], state[j] = state[j], state[i]
        output.append(value ^ state[(state[i] + state[j]) & 255])
    candidate = bytes(output)
    spec = specs[0]
    if spec.get('printable') and any(byte < 0x20 or byte > 0x7e for byte in candidate):
        return None
    prefix = spec.get('prefix', '').encode()
    suffix = spec.get('suffix', '').encode()
    if (prefix and not candidate.startswith(prefix)) or (suffix and not candidate.endswith(suffix)):
        return None
    for constraint in spec.get('byte_constraints', []):
        offset = integer(constraint['offset'])
        byte = candidate[offset]
        if 'value' in constraint and byte != integer(constraint['value']):
            return None
        if 'min' in constraint and byte < integer(constraint['min']):
            return None
        if 'max' in constraint and byte > integer(constraint['max']):
            return None
    source = hint.get('function')
    provenance = (' at %#x' % integer(source)) if source is not None else ''
    return {'status': 'candidate', 'method': 'native-material-assist',
            'goal_validated': False,
            'warnings': ['Unverified RC4 transform candidate%s. The requested execution goal and relationship to the input have NOT been validated.' % provenance],
            'stats': {'steps': 0, 'peak_active': 0, 'active': 0, 'avoided': 0,
                      'terminated': 0, 'errored': 0, 'unconstrained': 0, 'elapsed': 0.0},
            'inputs': [{'name': spec['name'], 'length': len(candidate),
                        'input_hex': candidate.hex(),
                        'input_text': candidate.decode('utf-8', errors='replace')}],
            'input_hex': candidate.hex(), 'input_text': candidate.decode('utf-8', errors='replace'),
            'trace': []}


def solve(req):
    """Preserve unknown feasibility for solver interrupts outside simgr.step."""
    started_ns = time.time_ns()
    try:
        return _solve(req)
    except Exception as exc:
        try:
            from angr import claripy
        except ImportError:
            import claripy
        cause, seen = exc, set()
        while cause is not None and id(cause) not in seen:
            if isinstance(cause, claripy.ClaripySolverInterruptError):
                result = {'status': 'incomplete', 'reason': 'solver_query_interrupted',
                          'error': str(cause), 'warnings': [
                              'A solver query reached a resource limit; feasibility remains unknown. '
                              'Increase solver_timeout_ms or inspect the last SMT snapshot.']}
                path = Path(req['smt_export']) if req.get('smt_export') else None
                try:
                    if path is not None and path.is_file() and path.stat().st_mtime_ns >= started_ns:
                        with path.open('r', encoding='utf-8') as stream:
                            header = stream.readline(200)
                        if header.startswith('; Arsenal snapshot:'):
                            result['smt_snapshot'] = {'path': str(path),
                                'reached_goal': header.strip() == '; Arsenal snapshot: reached goal',
                                'scope': 'Latest snapshot from this run; the interrupted query may be ahead of it.'}
                except (OSError, UnicodeError) as snapshot_error:
                    result['warnings'].append('Could not read latest SMT snapshot: %s' % snapshot_error)
                return result
            seen.add(id(cause))
            cause = cause.__cause__ or cause.__context__
        raise


def _solve(req):
    specs = input_specs(req)
    mapped_files = filesystem_specs(req)
    if req.get('mode') == 'call':
        for arg in req.get('call_args', []):
            if not isinstance(arg, dict) or arg.get('kind') not in ('buffer', 'int'):
                raise ValueError('call_args kind must be buffer or int')
            if arg['kind'] == 'buffer':
                call_input_name(arg, specs)
    import angr
    try:
        from angr import claripy
    except ImportError:
        import claripy
    try:
        from .angr_compat import install_avx512_permute_models
    except ImportError:
        try:
            from backend.angr_compat import install_avx512_permute_models
        except ImportError:
            from angr_compat import install_avx512_permute_models
    avx512_permute_models = install_avx512_permute_models()
    if avx512_permute_models:
        print('[angr] exact EVEX permutation models installed: ' +
              ', '.join(avx512_permute_models), flush=True)
    from angr.sim_type import SimTypeFunction, SimTypeInt, SimTypePointer, SimTypeChar

    started = time.monotonic()
    max_steps = bounded_int(req, 'max_steps', 10000, 1, 1000000)
    max_states = bounded_int(req, 'max_states', 256, 1, 100000)
    timeout = bounded_int(req, 'timeout', 60, 1, 86400)
    solver_timeout = (bounded_int(req, 'solver_timeout_ms', 10000, 1, 86400000)
                      if 'solver_timeout_ms' in req else None)
    mode = req.get('mode', 'stdin')
    if mode not in ('stdin', 'argv', 'call'):
        raise ValueError('mode must be stdin, argv, or call')
    binary = str(Path(req['binary']).expanduser().resolve(strict=True))
    find = {integer(x) for x in req.get('find', [])}
    avoid = {integer(x) for x in req.get('avoid', [])}
    find_conditions = req.get('find_conditions', [])
    stdout_find = req.get('stdout_find', '').encode('utf-8')
    stdout_avoid = req.get('stdout_avoid', '').encode('utf-8')
    use_return = mode == 'call' and 'return_value' in req
    if not find and not stdout_find and not use_return:
        raise ValueError('Specify find addresses, stdout_find, or a call return_value')
    if find & avoid:
        raise ValueError('An address cannot be both find and avoid')
    main_opts = {}
    if req.get('base_addr') is not None:
        main_opts['base_addr'] = integer(req['base_addr'])
    project = angr.Project(binary, auto_load_libs=False, main_opts=main_opts)
    validate_target_conditions(find_conditions, project.arch, find)
    install_common_models(project, claripy)
    obj = project.loader.main_object
    if 'base_addr' in main_opts and obj.mapped_base != main_opts['base_addr']:
        raise ValueError('Loader cannot rebase this non-position-independent binary; use its original image base')
    for addr in find | avoid:
        if not obj.contains_addr(addr):
            raise ValueError(f'Address {addr:#x} lies outside main binary; check image base')
    symbols = [symbolic_input(
        claripy, item,
        'arsenal_input_' + item['name'] if 'inputs' in req else 'arsenal_input',
        argv=(mode == 'argv')) for item in specs]
    combined = claripy.Concat(*symbols) if len(symbols) > 1 else symbols[0]
    options = {angr.options.TRACK_CONSTRAINT_ACTIONS}
    if req.get('zero_fill_unconstrained'):
        # Some language runtimes call allocator/IO stubs whose return values
        # are not modeled.  Zero-filling those values is a bounded fallback
        # for startup exploration; it is never enabled implicitly because it
        # can hide a missing model.
        options.update({angr.options.ZERO_FILL_UNCONSTRAINED_MEMORY,
                        angr.options.ZERO_FILL_UNCONSTRAINED_REGISTERS})
    # Keep unknown memory symbolic: zero-filling can manufacture false solutions.
    warnings = ['External functions use angr SimProcedures; results require concrete verification.']
    if avx512_permute_models:
        warnings.append('Exact symbolic EVEX permutation models enabled: %d VEX operations.' %
                        len(avx512_permute_models))
    if req.get('hybrid_assist'):
        warnings.append('Static crypto candidates are not solver proofs; native hints are ignored during symbolic exploration.')
    base_state = None
    sentinel = None
    if mode == 'stdin':
        state_factory = project.factory.entry_state if req.get('skip_init') else project.factory.full_init_state
        state_args = dict(
            args=[binary, *req.get('argv_before', []), *req.get('argv_after', [])],
            stdin=angr.SimFileStream(name='stdin', content=combined, has_end=True),
            add_options=options)
        if mapped_files:
            state_args['fs'] = {path: angr.SimFile(name=path, content=content)
                                for path, content in mapped_files}
        state = state_factory(**state_args)
    elif mode == 'argv':
        state_factory = project.factory.entry_state if req.get('skip_init') else project.factory.full_init_state
        state_args = dict(
            args=[binary, *req.get('argv_before', []), *symbols, *req.get('argv_after', [])],
            add_options=options)
        if mapped_files:
            state_args['fs'] = {path: angr.SimFile(name=path, content=content)
                                for path, content in mapped_files}
        state = state_factory(**state_args)
    else:
        start = integer(req['start_addr'])
        if not obj.contains_addr(start):
            raise ValueError('start_addr lies outside the main binary; check image base')
        base_state = project.factory.blank_state(add_options=options)
        for path, content in mapped_files:
            base_state.fs.insert(path, angr.SimFile(name=path, content=content))
        # Allocate from angr's modeled heap rather than assuming a free address.
        buffers = {}
        for item, sym in zip(specs, symbols):
            buf = base_state.heap.allocate(item['length'] + 1)
            base_state.memory.store(buf, sym)
            base_state.memory.store(buf + item['length'], claripy.BVV(0, 8))
            buffers[item['name']] = buf
        args, types = [], []
        defaults = [{'kind': 'buffer', 'input': item['name']} for item in specs]
        for arg in req.get('call_args', defaults):
            if arg['kind'] == 'buffer':
                args.append(buffers[call_input_name(arg, specs)])
                types.append(SimTypePointer(SimTypeChar()))
            elif arg['kind'] == 'int':
                args.append(integer(arg['value']))
                types.append(SimTypeInt())
            else:
                raise ValueError('call_args kind must be buffer or int')
        prototype = req.get('prototype') or SimTypeFunction(types, SimTypeInt())
        sentinel = project.loader.extern_object.allocate()
        state = project.factory.call_state(start, *args, base_state=base_state,
                                           prototype=prototype, ret_addr=sentinel)
        warnings.append('Call mode starts without caller context. Set prototype, registers and memory explicitly when needed.')
    if solver_timeout is not None:
        state.solver._solver.timeout = solver_timeout
    if mode == 'argv':
        # Record the concrete pointer for each modeled argument.  The custom
        # strlen model can then answer argv-length queries without branching
        # over every symbolic byte.
        argv_base = state.solver.eval(state.posix.argv)
        lengths = {}
        for index, spec in enumerate(specs, 1 + len(req.get('argv_before', []))):
            pointer = state.memory.load(argv_base + index * project.arch.bytes,
                                       project.arch.bytes,
                                       endness=project.arch.memory_endness)
            if not state.solver.symbolic(pointer):
                lengths[state.solver.eval(pointer)] = spec['length']
        state.globals['arsenal_argv_lengths'] = lengths
    for name, value in req.get('registers', {}).items():
        if name not in project.arch.registers:
            raise ValueError(f'Unknown register {name}')
        setattr(state.regs, name, integer(value))
    for item in req.get('memory', []):
        state.memory.store(integer(item['address']), bytes.fromhex(item['hex']))
    for spec, sym in zip(specs, symbols):
        length = spec['length']
        if spec['printable']:
            for byte in sym.chop(8):
                state.solver.add(claripy.UGE(byte, claripy.BVV(0x20, 8)),
                                 claripy.ULE(byte, claripy.BVV(0x7e, 8)))
        if mode == 'argv':
            for byte in sym.chop(8):
                state.solver.add(byte != 0)
        for field, at_end in [('prefix', False), ('suffix', True)]:
            data = spec[field].encode('utf-8')
            if len(data) > length:
                raise ValueError(f'{field} exceeds input length')
            if data:
                offset = length - len(data) if at_end else 0
                state.solver.add(sym.get_bytes(offset, len(data)) == claripy.BVV(data))
        for item in spec['byte_constraints']:
            offset = integer(item['offset'])
            if not 0 <= offset < length:
                raise ValueError('byte_constraints offset outside input')
            byte = sym.get_byte(offset)
            for key in ('value', 'min', 'max'):
                if key in item:
                    value = integer(item[key])
                    if not 0 <= value <= 255:
                        raise ValueError('Byte constraint value must be 0..255')
                    constant = claripy.BVV(value, byte.size())
                    if key == 'value':
                        state.solver.add(byte == constant)
                    elif key == 'min':
                        state.solver.add(claripy.UGE(byte, constant))
                    else:
                        state.solver.add(claripy.ULE(byte, constant))
    if not state.solver.satisfiable():
        return {'status': 'not_found', 'warnings': warnings, 'error': 'Input constraints are unsatisfiable'}
    manager = project.factory.simgr(state, save_unconstrained=True)
    # Veritesting merges straight-line and loop regions before stepping.  This
    # is especially useful for compiler-generated startup code and bounded
    # byte-processing loops, but it is opt-in because merging can be expensive
    # on heavily indirect or self-modifying programs.
    if req.get('veritesting'):
        from angr.exploration_techniques import Veritesting
        manager.use_technique(Veritesting())
    steps, peak, avoided, terminated = 0, 1, 0, 0
    last_progress = started
    found = None
    status = 'not_found'
    observed_stdout, detected_success, detected_failure = set(), set(), set()
    snapshot, last_snapshot = None, 0.0

    def snapshot_state(current, reached_goal=False):
        nonlocal snapshot, last_snapshot
        if not req.get('smt_export'):
            return
        try:
            try:
                from .smt_solver import export_state
            except ImportError:
                from smt_solver import export_state
            snapshot = export_state(current, symbols, specs, req['smt_export'], reached_goal)
        except Exception as exc:
            message = 'SMT snapshot unavailable: %s' % exc
            if message not in warnings:
                warnings.append(message)
        last_snapshot = time.monotonic()
    return_bits = bounded_int(req, 'return_bits', 32, 1, project.arch.bits)
    stops = find | avoid | ({sentinel} if sentinel is not None else set())
    while manager.active:
        candidates = list(manager.active) + list(manager.deadended)
        manager.stashes['deadended'] = []
        retained = []
        for current in candidates:
            addr = current.addr
            output = concrete_stdout(current) if stdout_find or stdout_avoid else b''
            if output and len(observed_stdout) < 16:
                observed_stdout.add(output[:4096].decode('utf-8', errors='replace'))
            if stdout_find and stdout_find in output:
                detected_success.add(stdout_find.decode('utf-8', errors='replace'))
            if stdout_avoid and stdout_avoid in output:
                detected_failure.add(stdout_avoid.decode('utf-8', errors='replace'))
            if addr in avoid or (stdout_avoid and stdout_avoid in output):
                avoided += 1
                continue
            success = ((addr in find and target_conditions_hold(current, find_conditions, project, claripy))
                       or bool(stdout_find and stdout_find in output))
            if sentinel is not None and addr == sentinel:
                if use_return:
                    ret = current.registers.load(project.arch.ret_offset, project.arch.bytes)
                    wanted = ret[return_bits - 1:0] == integer(req['return_value'])
                    if current.solver.satisfiable(extra_constraints=[wanted]):
                        current.add_constraints(wanted)
                        success = True
                terminated += 1
            elif current in manager.active:
                retained.append(current)
            if success:
                found, status = current, 'found'
                break
        manager.stashes['active'] = retained
        if found is not None:
            break
        if time.monotonic() - started >= timeout:
            status = 'timeout'
            break
        if steps >= max_steps or len(manager.active) > max_states:
            status = 'limit'
            break
        if not manager.active:
            break
        if time.monotonic() - last_snapshot >= 15:
            snapshot_state(manager.active[0])
        manager.step(extra_stop_points=stops)
        steps += 1
        peak = max(peak, len(manager.active))
        now = time.monotonic()
        if steps == 1 or now - last_progress >= 2:
            locations = Counter(hex(s.addr) for s in manager.active).most_common(5)
            print("[angr] steps=%d active=%d peak=%d errored=%d elapsed=%.1fs locations=%s" %
                  (steps, len(manager.active), peak, len(manager.errored), now - started, locations), flush=True)
            last_progress = now
        # Handle a final output emitted on a terminal state even with no active paths.
        if not manager.active and stdout_find:
            for current in manager.deadended:
                output = concrete_stdout(current)
                if stdout_find in output and not (stdout_avoid and stdout_avoid in output):
                    found, status = current, 'found'
                    break
    unconstrained = len(manager.unconstrained)
    interrupted = len(manager.stashes.get('interrupted', []))
    if status == 'not_found' and (manager.errored or unconstrained or interrupted):
        status = 'incomplete'
    if interrupted:
        warnings.append('%d paths were interrupted by solver resource limits; their feasibility is unknown.' % interrupted)
    if found is not None:
        snapshot_state(found, reached_goal=True)
    elif manager.active or manager.stashes.get('interrupted'):
        snapshot_state((manager.active or manager.stashes['interrupted'])[0])
    if unconstrained:
        warnings.append(f'{unconstrained} paths had unconstrained instruction pointers and were not explored.')
    result = {'status': status, 'warnings': warnings,
              'stats': {'steps': steps, 'peak_active': peak, 'active': len(manager.active),
                        'avoided': avoided, 'terminated': terminated, 'errored': len(manager.errored),
                        'unconstrained': unconstrained, 'interrupted': interrupted,
                        'solver_timeout_ms': solver_timeout,
                        'elapsed': round(time.monotonic() - started, 3),
                        'angr_version': angr.__version__, 'mapped_base': obj.mapped_base,
                        'architecture': project.arch.name},
              'errors': [str(e.error)[:400] for e in manager.errored[:5]]}
    if snapshot is not None:
        result['smt_snapshot'] = snapshot
    if observed_stdout:
        result['observed_stdout'] = sorted(observed_stdout)[:16]
    if detected_success:
        result['detected_success_messages'] = sorted(detected_success)
    if detected_failure:
        result['detected_failure_messages'] = sorted(detected_failure)
    if found is not None:
        # One joint model is essential: independent evals can break relations between inputs.
        data = found.solver.eval(combined, cast_to=bytes)
        offset, recovered = 0, []
        for item in specs:
            chunk = data[offset:offset + item['length']]
            recovered.append(dict(name=item['name'], length=item['length'], input_hex=chunk.hex(),
                                  input_text=chunk.decode('utf-8', errors='replace')))
            offset += item['length']
        result.update(inputs=recovered,
                      stdout=found.posix.dumps(1).decode('utf-8', errors='replace'),
                      trace=[int(x) for x in found.history.bbl_addrs] + [found.addr],
                      found_addr=found.addr)
        if len(specs) == 1 or mode == 'stdin':
            result.update(input_hex=data.hex(), input_text=data.decode('utf-8', errors='replace'))
        variables = set().union(*(c.variables for c in found.solver.constraints))
        extras = sorted(v for v in variables if not v.startswith('arsenal_input_'))
        if extras:
            result['warnings'].append('Solution also depends on modeled or uninitialized symbolic data: ' + ', '.join(extras[:20]))
    return result


def main():
    worker = len(sys.argv) > 1 and sys.argv[1] == '--worker'
    args = sys.argv[2:] if worker else sys.argv[1:]
    if len(args) != 2:
        print('Usage: solve.py REQUEST.json RESULT.json', file=sys.stderr)
        return 2
    request_path, result_path = map(Path, args)
    # If the GUI hard-kills the supervisor, Linux also kills its solver worker.
    if worker and sys.platform == 'linux':
        import ctypes
        parent_pid = os.getppid()
        libc = ctypes.CDLL(None, use_errno=True)
        if libc.prctl(1, signal.SIGKILL, 0, 0, 0) != 0:
            raise OSError(ctypes.get_errno(), 'prctl(PR_SET_PDEATHSIG) failed')
        if parent_pid == 1 or os.getppid() != parent_pid:
            os.kill(os.getpid(), signal.SIGKILL)
    try:
        req = json.loads(request_path.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
        timeout = bounded_int(req, 'timeout', 60, 1, 86400)
        if worker:
            # This is a resource boundary, not a security sandbox for parser bugs.
            if sys.platform == 'linux':
                import resource
                memory_mb = bounded_int(req, 'memory_mb', 8192, 512, 131072)
                resource.setrlimit(resource.RLIMIT_AS, (memory_mb * 1024**2,) * 2)
                resource.setrlimit(resource.RLIMIT_CORE, (0, 0))
            engine = req.get('engine', 'angr')
            if engine == 'z3':
                from smt_solver import solve_smt
                result = solve_smt(req)
            elif engine == 'angr':
                result = solve(req)
            else:
                raise ValueError('engine must be angr or z3')
        else:
            command = [sys.executable, str(Path(__file__).resolve()), '--worker',
                       str(request_path.resolve()), str(result_path.resolve())]
            # File output avoids PIPE deadlocks and provides auditable diagnostics.
            log_path = result_path.with_suffix(result_path.suffix + '.log')
            with log_path.open('w', encoding='utf-8') as log:
                process = subprocess.Popen(command, stdout=log, stderr=log, start_new_session=True)
                def cancel_worker(_signum, _frame):
                    try:
                        os.killpg(process.pid, signal.SIGKILL)
                    except ProcessLookupError:
                        pass
                    process.wait()
                    raise KeyboardInterrupt

                previous_handlers = {sig: signal.signal(sig, cancel_worker)
                                     for sig in (signal.SIGTERM, signal.SIGINT)}
                try:
                    code = process.wait(timeout=timeout + 5)
                except KeyboardInterrupt:
                    result = {'status': 'error', 'error': 'Cancelled by caller', 'log': str(log_path)}
                except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
                    try:
                        os.killpg(process.pid, signal.SIGKILL)
                    except ProcessLookupError:
                        pass
                    process.wait()
                    result = {'status': 'timeout', 'error': f'Worker exceeded {timeout + 5}s wall clock budget', 'log': str(log_path)}
                else:
                    if code == 0 and result_path.exists():
                        return 0
                    result = {'status': 'error', 'error': f'Worker exited with code {code}', 'log': str(log_path)}
                finally:
                    for sig, handler in previous_handlers.items():
                        signal.signal(sig, handler)
        save(result_path, result)
        return 0
    except Exception as exc:
        save(result_path, {'status': 'error', 'error': f'{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}'})
        traceback.print_exc(file=sys.stderr)
        return 0


if __name__ == '__main__':
    raise SystemExit(main())

```

`docs/CRYPTO_ANALYSIS.md`:

```md
# Native crypto analysis

## Why import- and signature-based scans miss custom RC4

Classifying recognized imported crypto APIs finds nothing in a locally implemented,
stripped or inlined cipher, and static byte signatures (FindCrypt-style) find nothing
either: RC4 initializes its permutation at runtime, so there is no AES-style table that
every compiled RC4 implementation contains. Key bytes can also be constructed or
transformed before scheduling. The scanner therefore matches the shape of the operations
rather than constants or names.

## Recognition and material recovery are separate

`plugin/crypto_patterns.py` operates on normalized expression relationships.
`plugin/crypto_scan.py` adapts Hex-Rays expressions and collects bounded evidence.
The relevant RC4 observations include byte-state permutation swaps, scheduling
with cyclic key indexing, byte-wrapped index updates, and stream XOR through a
lookup indexed by the sum of state bytes. A constant 256, a generic swap, or
random-looking data alone does not identify RC4.

Incomplete phases remain qualified candidates. Even a strong structural match
does not prove execution reachability, standard initialization in every path,
or encryption versus decryption direction. Unsupported compiler and decompiler
forms can be missed.

Key and data bytes require a relationship to the recognized operands and an
exact supported bound. Direct read-only global slices and supported constant
local initializers can be recovered. Unknown writes, escaping calls, unsupported
control flow, and unresolved aliases must cause recovery to decline rather than
return stale or nearby bytes. A decoded key is the material that reaches key
scheduling, not automatically the raw source bytes.

`plugin/crypto_ui.py` displays the algorithm evidence and material provenance.
Its reference preview applies `plugin/crypto_reference.py` to selected recovered
bytes. That preview is independent of recognition: readable output alone never
upgrades a match or proves the program's behavior.

## Generalization checks

`tests/crypto_fixtures/` is generated independently of the scanner.
Fixtures vary keys, data lengths, masks, source layouts, compiler, optimization,
PIE, and stripping. They cover combined and split routines, stack constants,
encoded keys, state copies, and near misses. Negative routines include identity
initializers, shuffles, simple XOR, unrelated modulo loops, a modified KSA, and
a stream loop with the wrong step. An initialized key mutated by an opaque call
checks that a previous constant is not returned as current key material.
The compiled matrix currently exercises x86-64; other processor modules have not
been validated by this update.

The fixture manifest is test truth only. The scanner must not read it. Detection
must not depend on the file path, function names, hashes, addresses, key
length, key bytes, ciphertext, or expected plaintext. Matrix reports record misses
and unresolved material rather than hiding them.

Reference output is checked against the published
[RFC 6229 RC4 vectors](https://www.rfc-editor.org/rfc/rfc6229#section-2) and against
independently generated fixture outputs. Only reviewed local fixtures are run
natively; analyzed targets are never executed.

## Using the feature

Restart IDA and open Arsenal with Ctrl+Alt+A. **Triage & bridges → Scan database**
includes native crypto scanning by default; inspect **Crypto** for results.
Disable **Include in Triage database scan** when only the faster call/input scan
is needed. The Crypto tab also has a direct **Scan crypto & materials** button.

Select a finding, inspect clickable algorithm operations, then inspect each
material's role, byte count, bytes, and provenance. A data operand is shown without
assuming ciphertext versus plaintext. Choose key/data material for the optional
standard RC4 preview. Export findings to retain the full coverage notes and
limitations. Scanning does not patch or execute the target.

```

`examples/z3/constant-predicate.json`:

```json
{
  "engine": "z3",
  "timeout": 10,
  "smtlib": "(declare-const x (_ BitVec 8))",
  "predicate": "(= (bvand (bvmul x (bvadd x #x01)) #x01) #x00)"
}

```

`examples/z3/contradictory-assumptions.json`:

```json
{
  "engine": "z3",
  "timeout": 10,
  "smtlib": "(declare-const x (_ BitVec 8)) (assert (= x #x00)) (assert (= x #x01))",
  "predicate": "(= x #x00)"
}

```

`examples/z3/overflow-counterexample.json`:

```json
{
  "engine": "z3",
  "timeout": 10,
  "smtlib": "(declare-const x (_ BitVec 8))",
  "predicate": "(bvugt (bvadd x #x01) x)"
}
```

`plugin/arsenal.py`:

```py
"""IDA Arsenal: bounded, asynchronous symbolic solving and analysis workbench."""
import hashlib
import json
import os
import re
from pathlib import Path
import sys
import time

from PyQt5 import QtCore, QtGui, QtWidgets
import ida_bytes
import ida_funcs
import ida_idaapi
import ida_kernwin
import ida_nalt
import ida_name
import idautils

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
JOBS = ROOT / "jobs"


def addresses(text):
    """Accept explicit hex addresses or names from this database."""
    result = []
    for token in text.replace(",", " ").split():
        ea = ida_name.get_name_ea(ida_idaapi.BADADDR, token)
        if ea == ida_idaapi.BADADDR:
            try:
                ea = int(token, 16)
            except ValueError:
                raise ValueError("Unknown address or symbol: " + token)
        if not ida_bytes.is_mapped(ea):
            raise ValueError("Address is not mapped in this database: " + token)
        result.append(ea)
    return result


def button(text, slot, layout):
    widget = QtWidgets.QPushButton(text)
    widget.clicked.connect(slot)
    layout.addWidget(widget)
    return widget


class SolverPanel(QtWidgets.QWidget):
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.process = None
        self.job = None
        self.result = None
        self.colors = {}
        self.cancelled = False
        self.crypto_provider = None
        self.log_offset = 0
        self.started_at = None
        self.progress_timer = QtCore.QTimer(self)
        self.progress_timer.setInterval(500)
        self.progress_timer.timeout.connect(self.poll_progress)
        self.deadline = QtCore.QTimer(self)
        self.deadline.setSingleShot(True)
        self.deadline.timeout.connect(self.expired)
        outer = QtWidgets.QVBoxLayout(self)
        outer.setContentsMargins(10, 10, 10, 10)
        outer.setSizeConstraint(QtWidgets.QLayout.SetMinimumSize)
        columns = QtWidgets.QSplitter(QtCore.Qt.Horizontal)
        settings = QtWidgets.QGroupBox("Inputs & exploration goal")
        results = QtWidgets.QGroupBox("Solution & execution path")
        settings.setMinimumWidth(480)
        results.setMinimumWidth(360)
        columns.addWidget(settings)
        columns.addWidget(results)
        columns.setSizes([560, 520])
        outer.addWidget(columns)
        layout = QtWidgets.QVBoxLayout(settings)
        layout.setSizeConstraint(QtWidgets.QLayout.SetMinimumSize)
        note = QtWidgets.QLabel(
            "Choose the input source and a goal. Mark find/avoid addresses from the disassembly context menu.\n"
            "Double-click a recovered path block to navigate."
        )
        note.setWordWrap(True)
        layout.addWidget(note)
        form = QtWidgets.QFormLayout()
        self.input_form = form
        self.mode = QtWidgets.QComboBox()
        self.mode.addItems(["stdin", "argv", "call"])
        form.addRow("Symbolic input", self.mode)
        self.length = QtWidgets.QSpinBox()
        self.length.setRange(1, 4096)
        self.length.setValue(16)
        form.addRow("Input bytes", self.length)
        self.printable = QtWidgets.QCheckBox("ASCII printable bytes (0x20–0x7e)")
        self.printable.setChecked(True)
        form.addRow("Domain", self.printable)
        self.prefix = QtWidgets.QLineEdit()
        self.prefix.setPlaceholderText("Optional known prefix")
        form.addRow("Prefix (UTF-8)", self.prefix)
        self.start = QtWidgets.QLineEdit()
        self.start.setPlaceholderText("Function name or hex address; used in call mode")
        form.addRow("Function entry", self.start)
        self.find = QtWidgets.QLineEdit()
        self.find.setPlaceholderText("Space-separated names or hex addresses")
        form.addRow("Find addresses", self.find)
        self.goal_registers = QtWidgets.QLineEdit()
        self.goal_registers.setPlaceholderText("Optional: eax=0 or bpl=1")
        self.goal_registers.setToolTip("Require these register values together at a find address. Useful when cmov/setcc replaces a success branch. Values accept decimal or 0x hexadecimal.")
        form.addRow("At find, require", self.goal_registers)
        self.avoid = QtWidgets.QLineEdit()
        form.addRow("Avoid addresses", self.avoid)
        self.stdout_find = QtWidgets.QLineEdit()
        self.stdout_find.setPlaceholderText("e.g. Good Job. (alternative to a target address)")
        form.addRow("Success stdout", self.stdout_find)
        discover = QtWidgets.QPushButton('Discover prompts and outcome messages')
        discover.clicked.connect(lambda: self.discover_outcomes())
        form.addRow('Message discovery', discover)
        self.outcome_box = QtWidgets.QGroupBox('Observed messages — review before choosing a goal')
        outcome_layout = QtWidgets.QVBoxLayout(self.outcome_box)
        self.outcome_choices = QtWidgets.QComboBox()
        self.outcome_choices.setSizeAdjustPolicy(QtWidgets.QComboBox.AdjustToMinimumContentsLengthWithIcon)
        self.outcome_choices.setMinimumContentsLength(35)
        self.outcome_choices.currentIndexChanged.connect(self.show_outcome)
        outcome_layout.addWidget(self.outcome_choices)
        self.outcome_details = QtWidgets.QPlainTextEdit()
        self.outcome_details.setReadOnly(True)
        self.outcome_details.setMaximumHeight(110)
        outcome_layout.addWidget(self.outcome_details)
        outcome_actions = QtWidgets.QHBoxLayout()
        self.outcome_use = button('Use selected success stdout', self.use_outcome, outcome_actions)
        button('Jump to output / reference', self.jump_outcome, outcome_actions)
        outcome_layout.addLayout(outcome_actions)
        self.outcome_box.hide()
        form.addRow(self.outcome_box)
        self.return_value = QtWidgets.QLineEdit("1")
        form.addRow("Call return value", self.return_value)
        self.timeout = QtWidgets.QSpinBox()
        self.timeout.setRange(5, 86400)
        self.timeout.setValue(60)
        form.addRow("Time limit (seconds)", self.timeout)
        self.smt_snapshots = QtWidgets.QCheckBox('Save SMT snapshots for the Z3 tab')
        self.smt_snapshots.setToolTip('Save one active path every 15 seconds and the final goal path. Partial snapshots do not assert the goal.')
        form.addRow('Constraint analysis', self.smt_snapshots)
        layout.addLayout(form)
        self.multi_enabled = QtWidgets.QCheckBox("Multiple named inputs")
        self.multi_enabled.setToolTip("Configure independent buffers, arguments, or consecutive stdin segments.")
        layout.addWidget(self.multi_enabled)
        from symbolic_inputs import SymbolicInputs
        self.input_editor = SymbolicInputs()
        layout.addWidget(self.input_editor)
        self.input_editor.hide()
        self.multi_enabled.toggled.connect(self.toggle_inputs)
        self.input_notice = QtWidgets.QLabel()
        self.input_notice.setWordWrap(True)
        self.input_notice.hide()
        layout.addWidget(self.input_notice)
        self.advanced = QtWidgets.QPlainTextEdit()
        self.advanced.setPlaceholderText(
            'Optional JSON: {"suffix":"\\n","byte_constraints":[{"offset":0,"value":65}],'
            '"call_args":[{"kind":"buffer"},{"kind":"int","value":16}],'
            '"max_states":256,"max_steps":10000}'
        )
        self.advanced.setMaximumHeight(85)
        self.advanced.setMinimumHeight(60)
        advanced_toggle = QtWidgets.QToolButton()
        advanced_toggle.setText("Advanced model options")
        advanced_toggle.setCheckable(True)
        advanced_toggle.setArrowType(QtCore.Qt.RightArrow)
        advanced_toggle.setToolButtonStyle(QtCore.Qt.ToolButtonTextBesideIcon)
        advanced_toggle.toggled.connect(self.advanced.setVisible)
```
