```py
    report['notes'].append('Bounded semantic recognition targets native RC4 byte-table implementations. SIMD, heavy obfuscation, indirect state aliases and unsupported decompiler forms may be missed.')
    return report


def _add_native_findings(report):
    from crypto_algorithms import scan_native_algorithms
    native = scan_native_algorithms(MAX_FUNCTIONS, MAX_FUNCTION_BYTES)
    report['coverage']['native'] = {key: value for key, value in native.items() if key != 'findings'}
    report['notes'].extend(native['errors'])
    if native['truncated']:
        report['notes'].append('Native instruction scan reached its function budget; coverage is incomplete.')
    report['findings'].extend(native['findings'])


def _zero_seed(analysis,event):
    lhs=event['lhs']
    if lhs[0]!='var':return False
    previous=None
    for candidate in analysis['events']:
        if candidate is event:break
        if candidate['lhs']==lhs:previous=candidate
    return bool(previous and previous['rhs']==('num',0) and previous.get('control')==event.get('control'))


def _arg_root(a, root, args):
    if isinstance(root, tuple) and root[0]=='var' and root[1] in a['arg_indices']:
        ix=a['arg_indices'].index(root[1]); return args[ix] if ix<len(args) else None
    return None


def _recover(f,a,root,length_expr,role,site,analyses,ea,ib):
    # In-routine arrays and direct caller constants only. No proximity inference.
    candidates=[(a,root,length_expr,site)]
    if isinstance(root,tuple) and root[0]=='var' and root[1] in a['arg_indices']:
        ix=a['arg_indices'].index(root[1])
        for caller, ca in analyses.items():
            for call in ca['calls']:
                if call['target']==ea and ix<len(call['args']):
                    le=_subst(length_expr,{v:call['constant_args'][j] for j,v in enumerate(a['arg_indices']) if j<len(call['constant_args'])})
                    candidates.append((ca,call['args'][ix],le,call['site']))
    for ca, cr, le, origin in candidates:
        context=next((e for e in ca['events']+ca['calls'] if e['site']==origin),None)
        # Material extents must be literal/typed constant expressions after
        # substituting literal caller arguments, never a guessed scalar state.
        n=_eval(le,{})
        if isinstance(cr,tuple) and cr[0]=='ref':cr=cr[1]
        if n is None or not 0<n<=MAX_MATERIAL: continue
        buf=None;address=None;basis=None
        if isinstance(cr,tuple) and cr[0]=='obj':
            address=cr[1]
            # Only literal mapped, non-writable static storage is accepted.
            import ida_segment
            seg=ida_segment.getseg(address)
            if seg and address+n<=seg.end_ea and not (seg.perm & ida_segment.SEGPERM_WRITE):
                buf=ib.get_bytes(address,n); basis='Exact read-only operand and proven length'
        if isinstance(cr,tuple) and cr[0]=='var':
            cells={};used=[]
            aliased=False
            for e in ca['events']:
                if e['site']>=origin:continue
                rhs=e['rhs'];lhs=e['lhs']
                if lhs[0]=='var' and lhs!=cr and (rhs==cr or rhs==('ref',cr) or (rhs[0]=='add' and any(q==cr or q==('ref',cr) for q in walk(rhs)))):aliased=True;break
            if aliased:continue
            for source in ca['local_sources']:
                if source['root']!=cr or source['end']>=origin:continue
                if context is None or not set(source['control']).issubset(context['control']):continue
                # No intervening write or escaping call may alter these bytes.
                invalid=False
                source_cells=dict(source['cells'])
                for e in ca['events']:
                    if not source['end']<e['site']<origin:continue
                    lhs=e['lhs']
                    rhs=e['rhs']
                    alias_source=(rhs==cr or rhs==('ref',cr) or (rhs[0]=='add' and any(q==cr or q==('ref',cr) for q in walk(rhs))))
                    if lhs[0]=='var' and lhs!=cr and alias_source:invalid=True;break
                    if lhs==cr:invalid=True;break
                    if lhs[0]=='load' and lhs[1]==cr:
                        later_index=_eval(lhs[2],e['values_before'])
                        if later_index is None:invalid=True;break
                        for j in range(later_index,later_index+e['lhs_width']):source_cells.pop(j,None)
                for call in ca['calls']:
                    if source['end']<call['site']<origin:invalid=True
                if not invalid:cells.update(source_cells);used.append(source)
            if all(j in cells for j in range(n)):
                buf=bytes(cells[j] for j in range(n));basis='Exact local operand from constant stores or proven zero-start, unit-step initializer; no intervening writes/escaping calls'
        if buf is not None and len(buf)==n:
            material={'role':role,'hex':buf.hex(),'length':n,'basis':basis,'site':origin}
            if address is not None:material['address']=address
            elif isinstance(cr,tuple) and cr[0]=='var':
                transforms=[source for source in used if source.get('transform')]
                if transforms:
                    material['initializer_sites']=[source['start'] for source in transforms]
                    operations=set();sources=set()
                    for source in transforms:
                        for event in source['events']:
                            for node in walk(event['rhs']):
                                if node[0] in ('xor','add','sub','band','bor','shl','ushr','mul'):operations.add(node[0])
                                if node[0]=='load' and node[1][0]=='obj':sources.add(node[1][1])
                    material['source_addresses']=sorted(sources)
                    material['transform_operations']=sorted(operations)
                    material['basis']+='; byte operations: '+', '.join(sorted(operations))
            if role=='data': material['interpretation']='RC4 XOR operand; ciphertext/plaintext direction is not proven'
            if material not in f['materials']:f['materials'].append(material)


def _subst(x,mapping):
    if not isinstance(x,tuple): return x
    if x[0]=='var' and x[1] in mapping:return mapping[x[1]]
    return tuple(_subst(q,mapping) if isinstance(q,tuple) else q for q in x)


def _eval(x,values):
    if not isinstance(x,tuple): return None
    op=x[0]
    if op=='num':return x[1]
    if op=='var':return values.get(x[1])
    if op in ('cast','ref'):return _eval(x[1],values)
    if op=='castint':
        value=_eval(x[3],values);bits=x[1]
        if value is None or not 0<bits<=128:return None
        value%=1<<bits
        return value-(1<<bits) if x[2] and value&(1<<(bits-1)) else value
    if op=='typedop':
        left,right=_eval(x[4],values),_eval(x[5],values);bits=x[2]
        if left is None or right is None or not 0<bits<=128:return None
        if x[1] in ('shl','ushr','sshr') and not 0<=right<bits:return None
        value=_eval((x[1],('num',left),('num',right)),{})
        if value is None:return None
        if x[3] and not -(1<<(bits-1))<=value<(1<<(bits-1)):return None
        return _eval(('castint',bits,x[3],('num',value)),{})
    if op=='neg':
        v=_eval(x[1],values);return -v if v is not None else None
    if len(x)!=3:return None
    l,r=_eval(x[1],values),_eval(x[2],values)
    if l is None or r is None:return None
    try:return {'add':lambda:l+r,'sub':lambda:l-r,'mul':lambda:l*r,'xor':lambda:l^r,'band':lambda:l&r,'bor':lambda:l|r,'shl':lambda:l<<r,'ushr':lambda:l>>r,'sshr':lambda:l>>r,'umod':lambda:l%r,'smod':lambda:l-((abs(l)//abs(r))*(-1 if (l<0)!=(r<0) else 1))*r,'udiv':lambda:l//r,'sdiv':lambda:(abs(l)//abs(r))*(-1 if (l<0)!=(r<0) else 1),'eq':lambda:int(l==r),'ne':lambda:int(l!=r),'ult':lambda:int(l<r),'slt':lambda:int(l<r),'ule':lambda:int(l<=r),'sle':lambda:int(l<=r),'ugt':lambda:int(l>r),'sgt':lambda:int(l>r)}[op]()
    except (KeyError,ZeroDivisionError,ValueError,OverflowError):return None


def _analyze(cf,hx,ib):
    import ida_ida
    byteorder='big' if ida_ida.inf_is_be() else 'little'
    variables=cf.get_lvars()
    local_sources=[]
    arrayvars={i for i,v in enumerate(variables) if v.type().is_array()}
    ptrvars={i for i,v in enumerate(variables) if v.type().is_ptr()}
    arg_indices=[i for i,v in enumerate(variables) if (v.is_arg_var() if callable(v.is_arg_var) else v.is_arg_var)]
    definitions={}; valuecontrols={}; events=[];calls=[];copies=[];values={};memory=defaultdict(dict); loopinfo={}
    names={getattr(hx,n):n[4:] for n in dir(hx) if n.startswith('cot_') and isinstance(getattr(hx,n),int)}
    def strip(e):
        while e.op==hx.cot_cast:e=e.x
        return e
    def flatten(e):
        if isinstance(e,tuple) and e[0]=='add':return flatten(e[1])+flatten(e[2])
        return [e]
    def plus(items):
        if not items:return ('num',0)
        out=items[0]
        for item in items[1:]:out=('add',out,item)
        return out
    def rootrank(n):
        if not isinstance(n,tuple):return 0
        if n[0]=='obj':return 5
        if n[0]=='var':return 4 if n[1] in arrayvars else 2 if n[1] in ptrvars else 1 if n[1] in arg_indices else 0
        if n[0]=='ref' and n[1][0]=='var':return 5
        return 0
    def load_address(expr):
        terms=flatten(expr)
        if not terms:return None
        ranked=sorted(enumerate(terms),key=lambda q:rootrank(q[1]),reverse=True)
        ix,root=ranked[0]
        if not rootrank(root):return None
        if root[0]=='ref':root=root[1]
        return ('load',root,plus(terms[:ix]+terms[ix+1:]))
    def norm(e,expand=True):
        e=strip(e);op=names.get(e.op,'unknown')
        if op=='num':return ('num',int(e.numval()))
        if op=='sizeof':return ('num',e.x.type.get_size())
        if op=='var':
            val=('var',e.v.idx)
            return definitions.get(e.v.idx,val) if expand else val
        if op=='obj':return ('obj',int(e.obj_ea))
        if op=='ref':
            inner=norm(e.x,False)
            if inner[0]=='load':return ('add',('ref',inner[1]),inner[2])
            return ('ref',inner)
        if op=='idx':
            addr=('add',norm(e.x,expand),norm(e.y,expand))
            result=load_address(addr)
            return result or ('idx',norm(e.x,False),norm(e.y,expand))
        if op=='ptr':return load_address(norm(e.x,expand)) or ('ptr',norm(e.x,expand))
        if op in ('postinc','preinc','postdec','predec'):return norm(e.x,False)
        if op=='call':
            target=strip(e.x)
            if target.op==hx.cot_helper and target.helper in ('LOBYTE','BYTE0') and len(e.a)==1:return norm(e.a[0],False)
            return ('call',)
        if op in ('neg','bnot','lnot'):return (op,norm(e.x,expand))
        try:return (op,norm(e.x,expand),norm(e.y,expand))
        except Exception:return (op,)
    def constant_expr(e):
        op=names.get(e.op,'unknown')
        if op=='cast':
            return ('castint',e.type.get_size()*8,bool(e.type.is_signed()),constant_expr(e.x))
        if op in ('idx','ptr'):
            result=norm(e,False)
            if result[0]!='load' or e.type.get_size()!=1:return ('unknown',)
            return ('typedload',result[1],result[2],e.type.get_size(),bool(e.type.is_signed()))
        if op=='num':
            value=int(e.numval());bits=e.type.get_size()*8
            if bits and e.type.is_signed() and value&(1<<(bits-1)):value-=1<<bits
            return ('num',value)
        if op=='obj' and not e.type.is_array() and not e.type.is_ptr():return ('static',int(e.obj_ea),e.type.get_size(),bool(e.type.is_signed()))
        if op in ('var','obj','ref','sizeof'):return norm(e,False)
        if op in ('neg','bnot','lnot'):return (op,constant_expr(e.x))
        if op in ('add','sub','xor','band','bor','mul','shl','ushr','sshr','umod','smod','udiv','sdiv'):
            return ('typedop',op,e.type.get_size()*8,bool(e.type.is_signed()),constant_expr(e.x),constant_expr(e.y))
        return ('unknown',)

    def loop_details(ins,env):
        lid=int(ins.this)
        if lid in loopinfo:return loopinfo[lid]
        obj=ins.cfor if ins.op==hx.cit_for else ins.cwhile if ins.op==hx.cit_while else ins.cdo
        cond=norm(obj.expr,False)
        bound=None
        if len(cond)==3 and cond[0] in ('ne','ult','slt','ule','sle','ugt','sgt'):
            bound=cond[2]
            if cond[0] in ('ule','sle') and bound[0]=='num':bound=('num',bound[1]+1)
        elif cond[0]=='var':bound=cond
        typed_bound=bound
        raw_condition=strip(obj.expr)
        if len(cond)==3 and raw_condition.op in (hx.cot_ne,hx.cot_ult,hx.cot_slt,hx.cot_ule,hx.cot_sle,hx.cot_ugt,hx.cot_sgt):
            typed_bound=constant_expr(raw_condition.y)
            if cond[0] in ('ule','sle'):
                typed_bound=('typedop','add',raw_condition.y.type.get_size()*8,bool(raw_condition.y.type.is_signed()),typed_bound,('num',1))
        stepvar=None; zero_unit=False; top_guard=False
        if ins.op==hx.cit_for:
            initial=strip(obj.init); step=strip(obj.step)
            if initial.op==hx.cot_asg and strip(initial.x).op==hx.cot_var and strip(initial.y).op==hx.cot_num and initial.y.numval()==0:
                v=strip(initial.x).v.idx
                if step.op in (hx.cot_preinc,hx.cot_postinc) and strip(step.x).op==hx.cot_var and strip(step.x).v.idx==v:
                    stepvar=v;zero_unit=cond[0] in ('ne','ult','slt','ule','sle') and cond[1]==('var',v)
        if ins.op in (hx.cit_while,hx.cit_do) and len(cond)==3 and cond[0] in ('ne','ult','slt','ule','sle') and cond[1][0]=='var':
            variable=cond[1][1]
            increments=[];writes=[]
            class StepVisitor(hx.ctree_visitor_t):
                def __init__(self):super().__init__(hx.CV_FAST)
                def visit_expr(self,q):
                    if q.op in (hx.cot_preinc,hx.cot_postinc) and norm(q.x,False)==('var',variable):increments.append(1)
                    if q.op==hx.cot_asg and norm(q.x,False)==('var',variable):writes.append(1)
                    return 0
            StepVisitor().apply_to(obj.body,None)
            StepVisitor().apply_to(obj.expr,None)
            if env.get(variable)==0 and len(increments)==1 and not writes:
                zero_unit=True;stepvar=variable
        if ins.op==hx.cit_for:
            step=strip(obj.step)
            if step.op==hx.cot_asg:
                left=norm(step.x,False);right=norm(step.y,False)
                if left[0]=='var' and right==('add',left,('num',1)):stepvar=left[1]
            # A top-of-loop equality break is equivalent to an upper bound only
            # when the index starts at zero and advances one each iteration.
            initial=strip(obj.init)
            if bound is None and stepvar is not None and initial.op==hx.cot_asg and norm(initial.x,False)==('var',stepvar) and norm(initial.y,False)==('num',0):
                body=obj.body
                if body.op==hx.cit_block:
                    for statement in body.cblock:
                        if statement.op!=hx.cit_if:
                            # Permit only plain scalar bookkeeping before guard;
                            # no input read, call or earlier control transfer.
                            if statement.op!=hx.cit_expr:break
                            expression=statement.cexpr
                            if expression.op!=hx.cot_asg or loads(norm(expression.y)) or any(q[0]=='call' for q in walk(norm(expression.y))):break
                            continue
                        if statement.op==hx.cit_if:
                            branch=statement.cif
                            condition=norm(branch.expr,False)
                            then=branch.ithen
                            breaks=then.op==hx.cit_break or (then.op==hx.cit_block and len(then.cblock)==1 and then.cblock[0].op==hx.cit_break)
                            if breaks and condition[0]=='eq' and condition[1]==('var',stepvar):
                                def literal(q):
                                    if q[0]=='ref' and q[1][0]=='obj':return ('num',q[1][1])
                                    return tuple(literal(t) if isinstance(t,tuple) else t for t in q)
                                bound=literal(condition[2]);typed_bound=bound;zero_unit=True;top_guard=True
                            break
        simple_body=True;breaks_seen=0;other_exits=0;nested_loops=0
        class ControlVisitor(hx.ctree_visitor_t):
            def __init__(self):super().__init__(hx.CV_FAST)
            def visit_insn(self,q):
                nonlocal simple_body,breaks_seen,other_exits,nested_loops
                if q.op==hx.cit_break:breaks_seen+=1
                if q.op in (hx.cit_continue,hx.cit_goto,hx.cit_return):other_exits+=1
                if q.op in (hx.cit_for,hx.cit_while,hx.cit_do):nested_loops+=1
                if q.op in (hx.cit_if,hx.cit_switch,hx.cit_break,hx.cit_continue,hx.cit_goto,hx.cit_return,hx.cit_for,hx.cit_while,hx.cit_do):simple_body=False
                return 0
        ControlVisitor().apply_to(obj.body,None)
        safe_extent=not other_exits and not nested_loops and (breaks_seen==0 or top_guard and breaks_seen==1)
        if top_guard and not safe_extent:bound=None;typed_bound=None;zero_unit=False
        info={'loop':lid,'simple_body':simple_body,'safe_extent':safe_extent,'bound_expr':typed_bound,'loop_bound':_eval(typed_bound,{}),'zero_unit':zero_unit,'stepvar':stepvar}
        loopinfo[lid]=info;return info
    class Visitor(hx.ctree_visitor_t):
        def __init__(self):
            super().__init__(hx.CV_PARENTS)
            self.lastcontrol=None
        def visit_expr(self,e):
            info={}
            control=[]
            parents=list(self.parents)
            for pos,parent in enumerate(parents):
                if parent is not None and parent.op in (hx.cit_if,hx.cit_switch):
                    child=parents[pos+1] if pos+1<len(parents) else e
                    control.append((int(parent.this),int(child.this)))
            if self.lastcontrol is not None and self.lastcontrol!=tuple(control):definitions.clear()
            self.lastcontrol=tuple(control)
            active_values={v:n for v,n in values.items() if set(valuecontrols.get(v,())).issubset(control)}
            for parent in reversed(list(self.parents)):
                if parent is not None and parent.op in (hx.cit_for,hx.cit_while,hx.cit_do):info=loop_details(parent.cinsn,active_values);break
            value_scope=tuple(control)+((('loop',info['loop']),) if info.get('loop') is not None else ())
            active_values={v:n for v,n in values.items() if set(valuecontrols.get(v,())).issubset(value_scope)}
            if e.op in (hx.cot_preinc,hx.cot_postinc,hx.cot_predec,hx.cot_postdec):
                lhs=norm(e.x,False)
                events.append({'lhs':lhs,'rhs':('add' if e.op in (hx.cot_preinc,hx.cot_postinc) else 'sub',lhs,('num',1)),'site':int(e.ea),'lhs_width':e.x.type.get_size(),'byte_wrap':e.x.type.get_size()==1,'lookup_byte_wrap':False,'wrapped_lookups':[],'typed_moduli':[],'signed_reads':False,'control':tuple(control),'values_before':dict(active_values),**info})
            if e.op in (hx.cot_preinc,hx.cot_postinc,hx.cot_predec,hx.cot_postdec) and norm(e.x,False)[0]=='var':
                values.pop(norm(e.x,False)[1],None);definitions.pop(norm(e.x,False)[1],None)
            if e.op==hx.cot_call:
                target=strip(e.x)
                if True:
                    args=[norm(x,False) for x in e.a]
                    calls.append({'target':int(target.obj_ea) if target.op==hx.cot_obj else None,'args':args,'constant_args':[constant_expr(x) for x in e.a],'loops':[int(p.cinsn.this) for p in parents if p is not None and p.op in (hx.cit_for,hx.cit_while,hx.cit_do)],'site':int(e.ea),'control':tuple(control),'values_before':dict(active_values)})
                    import idc
                    name=(idc.get_name(target.obj_ea) if target.op==hx.cot_obj else target.helper if target.op==hx.cot_helper else '').lower().lstrip('_')
                    if name in ('memcpy','qmemcpy','memmove') and len(args)>=3:
                        def asroot(q):return q[1] if q and q[0]=='ref' else q
                        if _eval(args[2],active_values)==256:copies.append((asroot(args[1]),asroot(args[0]),int(e.ea),tuple(control)))
                    values.clear();valuecontrols.clear();definitions.clear()
            assignment=e.op in (hx.cot_asg,hx.cot_asgadd,hx.cot_asgsub,hx.cot_asgxor,hx.cot_asgband)
            if assignment:
                lhs=norm(e.x,False);rhs=norm(e.y)
                if e.op!=hx.cot_asg:rhs=({hx.cot_asgadd:'add',hx.cot_asgsub:'sub',hx.cot_asgxor:'xor',hx.cot_asgband:'band'}[e.op],lhs,rhs)
                event={'lhs':lhs,'rhs':rhs,'constant_rhs':constant_expr(e.y) if e.op==hx.cot_asg else ('unknown',),'site':int(e.ea),'lhs_width':e.x.type.get_size(),'byte_wrap':e.x.type.get_size()==1 or e.y.type.get_size()==1,'lookup_byte_wrap':False,'wrapped_lookups':[],'typed_moduli':[],'signed_reads':False,'control':tuple(control),'values_before':dict(active_values),**info}
                class ByteVisitor(hx.ctree_visitor_t):
                    def __init__(self):super().__init__(hx.CV_FAST)
                    def visit_expr(self,q):
                        if q.op in (hx.cot_umod,hx.cot_smod):event['typed_moduli'].append((norm(q),constant_expr(q.y)))
                        if q.op in (hx.cot_idx,hx.cot_ptr) and q.type.get_size()==1 and q.type.is_signed():event['signed_reads']=True
                        if q.op==hx.cot_idx and q.y.type.get_size()==1:event['wrapped_lookups'].append(norm(q))
                        if q.op==hx.cot_ptr:
                            class CastVisitor(hx.ctree_visitor_t):
                                def __init__(self):super().__init__(hx.CV_FAST)
                                def visit_expr(self,z):
                                    if z.op==hx.cot_cast and z.type.get_size()==1:
                                        load=norm(q)
                                        if load[0]=='load' and norm(z)==load[2]:event['wrapped_lookups'].append(load)
                                    return 0
                            CastVisitor().apply_to(q.x,None)
                        return 0
                ByteVisitor().apply_to(e.y,None)
                events.append(event)
                if lhs[0]=='load' and rhs[0]=='load' and lhs[1]==rhs[1]:
                    for prior in reversed(events[:-1]):
                        if prior.get('loop')!=event.get('loop'):continue
                        if prior['lhs']==rhs and prior['rhs']==lhs:
                            for key,definition in list(definitions.items()):
                                if definition==rhs:definitions[key]=lhs
                            break
                if lhs[0]=='var':
                    idx=lhs[1]
                    # Expand only side-effect-free read temporaries, never accumulators.
                    if (loads(rhs) or (idx in ptrvars and rhs[0]=='var' and rhs[1] in ptrvars)) and not any(n==lhs for n in walk(rhs)):
                        definitions[idx]=rhs
                    else:definitions.pop(idx,None)
                    v=_eval(('castint',e.x.type.get_size()*8,bool(e.x.type.is_signed()),constant_expr(e.y)),active_values) if e.op==hx.cot_asg else None
                    valuecontrols[idx]=value_scope
                    if v is None: values.pop(idx,None)
                    if v is not None:
                        values[idx]=v
                        size=variables[idx].type().get_size()
                        if 0<size<=16:
                            for j,b in enumerate((v % (1<<(size*8))).to_bytes(size,byteorder)):memory[lhs][j]=b
            return 0
    Visitor().apply_to(cf.body,None)
    # Recover initialized local bytes from bounded constant assignment loops. The
    # interpreter supports arithmetic and fixed static reads, but no calls.
    def evaluate(expr,env):
        if not isinstance(expr,tuple):return None
        if expr[0]=='typedop':
            left=evaluate(expr[4],env);right=evaluate(expr[5],env)
            if left is None or right is None:return None
            return _eval(('typedop',expr[1],expr[2],expr[3],('num',left),('num',right)),{})
        if expr[0] in ('typedload','static'):
            import ida_segment
            if expr[0]=='typedload':
                index=_eval(expr[2],env)
                if expr[1][0]!='obj' or index is None:return None
                address=expr[1][1]+index;width=expr[3];signed=expr[4]
            else:address,width,signed=expr[1:]
            seg=ida_segment.getseg(address)
            if not seg or seg.perm & ida_segment.SEGPERM_WRITE or not 0<width<=16 or address+width>seg.end_ea:return None
            raw=ib.get_bytes(address,width)
            return int.from_bytes(raw,byteorder,signed=signed) if raw and len(raw)==width else None
        if expr[0]=='castint':
            val=evaluate(expr[3],env);bits=expr[1]
            if val is None or not 0<bits<=128:return None
            val%=1<<bits
            return val-(1<<bits) if expr[2] and val&(1<<(bits-1)) else val
        if expr[0]=='load':
            idx=_eval(expr[2],env)
            if idx is None:return None
            root=expr[1]
            if root[0]=='obj':
                import ida_segment
                seg=ida_segment.getseg(root[1]+idx)
                if not seg or seg.perm & ida_segment.SEGPERM_WRITE:return None
                raw=ib.get_bytes(root[1]+idx,1)
                return raw[0] if raw else None
            # Local load folding needs memory SSA; decline rather than use a final snapshot.
            return None
        if expr[0]=='obj':
            import ida_segment
            seg=ida_segment.getseg(expr[1])
            if not seg or seg.perm & ida_segment.SEGPERM_WRITE:return None
            raw=ib.get_bytes(expr[1],1)
            return raw[0] if raw else None
        new=tuple(('num',v) if isinstance(q,tuple) and (v:=evaluate(q,env)) is not None else q for q in expr)
        return _eval(new,env)
    groups=defaultdict(list)
    for e in events:
        if e.get('loop') is not None: groups[e['loop']].append(e)
        else:
            lhs=e['lhs']; rhs=e.get('constant_rhs',('unknown',)); env={}
            idx=0 if lhs[0]=='var' else _eval(lhs[2],env) if lhs[0]=='load' else None
            root=lhs if lhs[0]=='var' else lhs[1] if lhs[0]=='load' else None
            value=evaluate(rhs,env); width=e['lhs_width']
            if root is not None and idx is not None and value is not None and 0<width<=16:
                cells={idx+j:b for j,b in enumerate((value%(1<<(width*8))).to_bytes(width,byteorder))}
                local_sources.append({'root':root,'cells':cells,'start':e['site'],'end':e['site'],'control':e['control'],'events':[e]})
    for lid,group in groups.items():
        meta=loopinfo[lid]; bound=meta.get('loop_bound')
        if not meta['simple_body'] or any(lid in call['loops'] for call in calls):continue
        if not meta['zero_unit'] or bound is None or not 0<bound<=MAX_MATERIAL:continue
        ivar=meta['stepvar']
        for e in group:
            if e['lhs'][0]!='load' or e['lhs_width']!=1:continue
            if any(q['lhs']==('var',ivar) and q['rhs'] not in (('num',0),('add',('var',ivar),('num',1))) for q in group):continue
            # Branching inside an initializer is deliberately unsupported.
            if any(q['control']!=e['control'] for q in group):continue
            if sum(q['lhs'][0]=='load' for q in group)!=1:continue
            temp={}; ok=True
            for iteration in range(bound):
                env={ivar:iteration};idx=_eval(e['lhs'][2],env);v=evaluate(e.get('constant_rhs',('unknown',)),env)
                if idx is None or v is None or not 0<=idx<MAX_MATERIAL:ok=False;break
                temp[idx]=v&255
            if ok:local_sources.append({'root':e['lhs'][1],'cells':temp,'start':e['site'],'end':e['site'],'control':e['control'],'events':[e],'transform':repr(e['rhs'])})
    return {'events':events,'states':recognize(events),'calls':calls,'copies':copies,'arg_indices':arg_indices,'values':values,'memory':memory,'local_sources':local_sources}

```

`plugin/crypto_ui.py`:

```py
"""Native-crypto observations with explicit provenance and reference previews."""
import html
import json
from pathlib import Path
import re
import tempfile

from PyQt5 import QtCore, QtWidgets
import ida_bytes
import ida_kernwin

from crypto_reference import rc4_transform
from ui_style import polish_table

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]


class CryptoPanel(QtWidgets.QWidget):
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.report = None
        layout = QtWidgets.QVBoxLayout(self)
        heading = QtWidgets.QLabel("<h2>Native cryptography</h2><p>Inspect algorithm structure, recovered material, and the operations connecting them.</p>")
        heading.setWordWrap(True)
        layout.addWidget(heading)
        actions = QtWidgets.QHBoxLayout()
        scan = QtWidgets.QPushButton("Scan crypto & materials")
        scan.setObjectName("primaryAction")
        scan.clicked.connect(lambda: self.scan_database())
        actions.addWidget(scan)
        export = QtWidgets.QPushButton("Export findings")
        export.clicked.connect(self.export)
        actions.addWidget(export)
        self.automatic = QtWidgets.QCheckBox("Include in Triage database scan")
        self.automatic.setChecked(True)
        actions.addWidget(self.automatic)
        actions.addStretch()
        layout.addLayout(actions)
        self.status = QtWidgets.QLabel("Ready. Detection and key/data recovery are reported separately.")
        self.status.setWordWrap(True)
        self.status.setTextInteractionFlags(QtCore.Qt.TextSelectableByMouse)
        layout.addWidget(self.status)
        split = QtWidgets.QSplitter(QtCore.Qt.Vertical)
        self.table = QtWidgets.QTableWidget(0, 4)
        self.table.setHorizontalHeaderLabels(["Algorithm / status", "Function", "Recovered material", "Observation"])
        polish_table(self.table)
        self.table.setEditTriggers(QtWidgets.QAbstractItemView.NoEditTriggers)
        self.table.setSelectionBehavior(QtWidgets.QAbstractItemView.SelectRows)
        self.table.setSelectionMode(QtWidgets.QAbstractItemView.SingleSelection)
        self.table.horizontalHeader().setSectionResizeMode(QtWidgets.QHeaderView.ResizeToContents)
        self.table.horizontalHeader().setStretchLastSection(True)
        self.table.itemSelectionChanged.connect(self.show_details)
        self.table.cellDoubleClicked.connect(self.jump_row)
        split.addWidget(self.table)
        self.details = QtWidgets.QTextBrowser()
        self.details.setOpenLinks(False)
        self.details.setOpenExternalLinks(False)
        self.details.anchorClicked.connect(self.jump)
        split.addWidget(self.details)
        split.setSizes([220, 420])
        layout.addWidget(split, 1)
        preview = QtWidgets.QGroupBox("Reference transform preview")
        grid = QtWidgets.QGridLayout(preview)
        self.key_choice = QtWidgets.QComboBox()
        self.data_choice = QtWidgets.QComboBox()
        grid.addWidget(QtWidgets.QLabel("Key material"), 0, 0)
        grid.addWidget(self.key_choice, 0, 1)
        grid.addWidget(QtWidgets.QLabel("Data operand"), 1, 0)
        grid.addWidget(self.data_choice, 1, 1)
        run = QtWidgets.QPushButton("Preview RC4 transform")
        run.clicked.connect(self.preview)
        grid.addWidget(run, 0, 2, 2, 1)
        self.preview_output = QtWidgets.QPlainTextEdit()
        self.preview_output.setReadOnly(True)
        self.preview_output.setMaximumHeight(125)
        self.preview_output.setPlaceholderText("Choose recovered material to apply the standard algorithm locally. Native detection remains a separate observation.")
        grid.addWidget(self.preview_output, 2, 0, 1, 3)
        layout.addWidget(preview)

    def scan_database(self, report=None):
        ida_kernwin.show_wait_box("Analyzing native crypto structure…")
        try:
            from crypto_scan import scan_crypto
            new_report = scan_crypto()
            self.report = new_report
            self.populate()
            return new_report
        except Exception as exc:
            self.status.setText("Crypto scan stopped: %s. Previous results retained." % exc)
            return None
        finally:
            ida_kernwin.hide_wait_box()

    def populate(self):
        self.table.setRowCount(0)
        findings = self.report.get("findings", [])
        for row, finding in enumerate(findings):
            self.table.insertRow(row)
            material = ", ".join("%s (%d bytes)" % (item.get("role", "material"), item.get("length", 0))
                                 for item in finding.get("materials", []) if item.get("hex")) or "Not recovered"
            values = [finding.get("algorithm", "") + " · " + finding.get("status", "candidate"),
                      finding.get("name", hex(finding["function"])), material, finding.get("summary", "")]
            for col, value in enumerate(values):
                cell = QtWidgets.QTableWidgetItem(value)
                cell.setToolTip(value)
                cell.setData(QtCore.Qt.UserRole, row)
                self.table.setItem(row, col, cell)
        coverage = self.report.get("coverage", {})
        native = coverage.get('native', {})
        self.status.setText("%d findings · %d functions instruction-scanned · %d decompiled for RC4 · %d analysis failures. Candidates require review; key/data recovery is separate." %
                            (len(findings), native.get('scanned', 0), coverage.get("decompiled", 0), coverage.get("failed", 0) + native.get('failed', 0)))
        if findings:
            self.table.selectRow(0)
        else:
            notes = self.report.get("notes", [])
            self.details.setPlainText("No supported native crypto structure found.\n" + "\n".join(map(str, notes)))

    def selected(self):
        row = self.table.currentRow()
        return self.report.get("findings", [])[row] if self.report and row >= 0 else None

    @staticmethod
    def address_link(ea):
        return '<a href="ida:%x">%#x</a>' % (ea, ea) if isinstance(ea, int) else "address unavailable"

    def show_details(self):
        finding = self.selected()
        if not finding:
            return
        esc = html.escape
        link = self.address_link
        dark = self.details.palette().base().color().lightness() < 128
        parts = ['<style>a {color:%s;}</style>' % ("#80c4ff" if dark else "#095aba"),
                 "<h2>%s · %s</h2>" % (esc(finding.get("algorithm", "Crypto")), esc(finding.get("status", "candidate"))),
                 "<p><b>%s</b> · %s</p>" % (esc(finding.get("name", "")), link(finding["function"])),
                 "<p>%s</p><h3>Algorithm observations</h3>" % esc(finding.get("summary", ""))]
        for evidence in finding.get("evidence", []):
            if isinstance(evidence, dict):
                parts.append("<p>%s · %s</p>" % (link(evidence.get("site")), esc(evidence.get("description", ""))))
            else:
                parts.append("<p>%s</p>" % esc(str(evidence)))
        self.key_choice.clear()
        self.data_choice.clear()
        self.preview_output.clear()
        parts.append("<h3>Recovered material and provenance</h3>")
        materials = finding.get("materials", [])
        if not materials:
            parts.append("<p>No key/data bytes were recovered. Algorithm recognition alone does not identify material.</p>")
        for index, material in enumerate(materials):
            role = material.get("role", "material")
            label = ("Effective key material" if material.get("transform_operations") else "Key material") if role == "key" else "Data operand — direction requires context" if role in ("data", "ciphertext") else role
            parts.append("<h4>%s · %s bytes</h4><p>%s<br>%s</p>" % (
                esc(label), material.get("length", "?"), link(material.get("address", material.get("site"))),
                esc(material.get("basis", ""))))
            if material.get("source_addresses"):
                parts.append("<p>Constant sources: %s</p>" % " · ".join(link(ea) for ea in material["source_addresses"]))
            if material.get("initializer_sites"):
                parts.append("<p>Initializer operations: %s</p>" % " · ".join(link(ea) for ea in material["initializer_sites"]))
            if material.get("transform_operations"):
                parts.append("<p>Recovered byte transforms: %s</p>" % esc(", ".join(material["transform_operations"])))
            if material.get("hex"):
                parts.append("<p><code>%s</code></p>" % esc(material["hex"]))
                try:
                    data = bytes.fromhex(material["hex"])
                    parts.append("<p>Bytes as text: <code>%s</code></p>" % esc(repr(data[:128])))
                    target = self.key_choice if role == "key" else self.data_choice
                    target.addItem("%s · %d bytes · %s" % (role, len(data), hex(material.get("site", finding["function"]))), index)
                except ValueError:
                    pass
        if finding.get("limitations"):
            parts.append("<h3>Analysis limits</h3>")
            parts.extend("<p>%s</p>" % esc(str(note)) for note in finding["limitations"])
        if self.report.get("notes"):
            parts.append("<h3>Scan coverage</h3>")
            parts.extend("<p>%s</p>" % esc(str(note)) for note in self.report["notes"])
        self.details.setHtml("".join(parts))

    def preview(self):
        finding = self.selected()
        if not finding or self.key_choice.currentIndex() < 0 or self.data_choice.currentIndex() < 0:
            self.preview_output.setPlainText("Select a finding with recovered key and data material first.")
            return
        try:
            materials = finding["materials"]
            key = bytes.fromhex(materials[self.key_choice.currentData()]["hex"])
            data = bytes.fromhex(materials[self.data_choice.currentData()]["hex"])
            output = rc4_transform(key, data)
            self.preview_output.setPlainText("Standard RC4 applied to the selected material. This preview does not prove native execution or data direction.\nHex: %s\nBytes: %r" % (output.hex(), output[:2048]))
        except (ValueError, IndexError, KeyError) as exc:
            self.preview_output.setPlainText(str(exc))

    def jump(self, url):
        if re.fullmatch(r"ida:[0-9a-fA-F]+", url.toString()):
            ea = int(url.toString()[4:], 16)
            if ida_bytes.is_mapped(ea):
                ida_kernwin.jumpto(ea)

    def jump_row(self, row, col):
        finding = self.selected()
        if finding:
            ida_kernwin.jumpto(finding["function"])

    def export(self):
        if self.report is None:
            self.status.setText("Run a crypto scan first.")
            return
        try:
            directory = ROOT / "exports"
            directory.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
            target = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="crypto-", dir=str(directory)))
            target.chmod(0o700)
            path = target / "crypto.json"
            path.write_text(json.dumps(self.report, indent=2))
            self.status.setText("Saved locally: " + str(path))
        except OSError as exc:
            self.status.setText(str(exc))
```

`plugin/debugger.py`:

```py
"""IDA 9 / PyQt5 stop dashboard; reads debuggee only while suspended."""
from datetime import datetime
import weakref

from PyQt5 import QtCore, QtGui, QtWidgets
import ida_dbg
import ida_ida
import ida_idaapi
import ida_idd
import ida_kernwin
import ida_name


class _StopHooks(ida_dbg.DBG_Hooks):
    def __init__(self, panel):
        super().__init__()
        self.panel = weakref.ref(panel)

    def event(self, text):
        panel = self.panel()
        if panel is not None and not panel.closed:
            panel.record_stop(text)
            QtCore.QTimer.singleShot(0, panel.refresh)

    def dbg_suspend_process(self):
        self.event('Suspended')

    def dbg_bpt(self, tid, ea):
        self.event(f'Breakpoint {ea:#x} · thread {tid}')
        return 0

    def dbg_step_into(self):
        self.event('Step into')

    def dbg_step_over(self):
        self.event('Step over')

    def dbg_step_until_ret(self):
        self.event('Step out')

    def dbg_run_to(self, pid, tid, ea):
        self.event(f'Run to {ea:#x}')

    def dbg_process_exit(self, pid, tid, ea, code):
        panel = self.panel()
        if panel is not None:
            panel.previous = {}
        self.event(f'Process exited ({code})')

    def dbg_process_start(self, pid, tid, ea, name, base, size):
        panel = self.panel()
        if panel is not None:
            panel.previous = {}
        self.event(f'Process {pid} started')

    def dbg_request_error(self, command, notification):
        self.event(f'Debugger request failed: {command}, event {notification}')

    def dbg_bpt_changed(self, event, bpt):
        panel = self.panel()
        if panel is not None and not panel.closed:
            QtCore.QTimer.singleShot(0, panel.refresh)


class DebuggerPanel(QtWidgets.QWidget):
    """Registers with change highlighting, pointer-aware stack, memory, stops."""
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.closed = False
        self.previous = {}
        self.last_snapshot = {}
        self.last_register_rows = []
        self.last_changed = []
        layout = QtWidgets.QVBoxLayout(self)
        toolbar = QtWidgets.QHBoxLayout()
        self.controls = []
        for title, request in [('Continue', ida_dbg.request_continue_process),
                               ('Step into', ida_dbg.request_step_into),
                               ('Step over', ida_dbg.request_step_over),
                               ('Step out', ida_dbg.request_step_until_ret)]:
            button = QtWidgets.QPushButton(title)
            button.clicked.connect(lambda checked=False, req=request: self.request(req))
            toolbar.addWidget(button)
            self.controls.append(button)
        refresh = QtWidgets.QPushButton('Refresh')
        refresh.clicked.connect(self.refresh)
        toolbar.addWidget(refresh)
        layout.addLayout(toolbar)
        self.status = QtWidgets.QLabel('Start a debugger session and suspend to inspect live state.')
        layout.addWidget(self.status)
        tabs = QtWidgets.QTabWidget()
        self.registers = self.table(['Register', 'Value', 'Symbol / delta'])
        self.stack = self.table(['Stack address', 'Pointer value', 'Symbol'])
        self.breakpoints = self.table(['Address', 'Enabled', 'Condition'])
        tabs.addTab(self.registers, 'Registers')
        tabs.addTab(self.stack, 'Stack')
        memory_page = QtWidgets.QWidget()
        memory_layout = QtWidgets.QVBoxLayout(memory_page)
        memory_row = QtWidgets.QHBoxLayout()
        self.memory_address = QtWidgets.QLineEdit()
        self.memory_address.setPlaceholderText('Hex address or symbol; empty = stack pointer')
        self.memory_address.returnPressed.connect(self.refresh)
        memory_row.addWidget(self.memory_address)
        memory_layout.addLayout(memory_row)
        self.memory = QtWidgets.QPlainTextEdit()
        self.memory.setReadOnly(True)
        self.memory.setFont(QtGui.QFont('monospace'))
        memory_layout.addWidget(self.memory)
        tabs.addTab(memory_page, 'Memory (256 bytes)')
        tabs.addTab(self.breakpoints, 'Breakpoints')
        self.log = QtWidgets.QPlainTextEdit()
        self.log.setReadOnly(True)
        self.log.document().setMaximumBlockCount(250)
        tabs.addTab(self.log, 'Stop history')
        layout.addWidget(tabs)
        self.registers.itemDoubleClicked.connect(self.jump_value)
        self.stack.itemDoubleClicked.connect(self.jump_value)
        self.breakpoints.itemDoubleClicked.connect(self.jump_breakpoint)
        self.hooks = _StopHooks(self)
        self.hooks.hook()
        self.observed_state = None
        self.state_timer = QtCore.QTimer(self)
        self.state_timer.setInterval(250)
        self.state_timer.timeout.connect(self.observe_state)
        self.state_timer.start()
        self.refresh()

    @staticmethod
    def table(headers):
        table = QtWidgets.QTableWidget(0, len(headers))
        table.setHorizontalHeaderLabels(headers)
        table.setEditTriggers(QtWidgets.QAbstractItemView.NoEditTriggers)
        table.setSelectionBehavior(QtWidgets.QAbstractItemView.SelectRows)
        table.horizontalHeader().setStretchLastSection(True)
        table.setFont(QtGui.QFont('monospace'))
        return table

    def record_stop(self, text):
        self.log.appendPlainText(f'{datetime.now():%H:%M:%S}  {text}')

    def request(self, request):
        if ida_dbg.get_process_state() != ida_dbg.DSTATE_SUSP:
            self.status.setText('Suspend the debuggee before issuing execution commands.')
            return
        if request():
            ida_dbg.run_requests()
            self.status.setText('Debugger request submitted; waiting for next stop.')
            for button in self.controls:
                button.setEnabled(False)
        else:
            self.status.setText('Debugger rejected the request; inspect IDA output.')

    @staticmethod
    def populate(table, rows, changed=()):
        table.setRowCount(len(rows))
        for row, values in enumerate(rows):
            for col, value in enumerate(values):
                item = QtWidgets.QTableWidgetItem(str(value))
                if row in changed:
                    item.setForeground(QtGui.QColor('#d08700'))
                    font = item.font()
                    font.setBold(True)
                    item.setFont(font)
                table.setItem(row, col, item)
        table.resizeColumnsToContents()

    def observe_state(self):
        """Poll only execution state so external F9 cannot leave a live label."""
        if self.closed:
            return
        state = ida_dbg.get_process_state()
        if state == self.observed_state:
            return
        self.observed_state = state
        suspended = state == ida_dbg.DSTATE_SUSP
        for button in self.controls:
            button.setEnabled(suspended)
        if not suspended:
            self.status.setText('No suspended debuggee. Values below are from the last stop.')

    def refresh(self):
        if self.closed:
            return
        self.observed_state = ida_dbg.get_process_state()
        suspended = self.observed_state == ida_dbg.DSTATE_SUSP
        for button in self.controls:
            button.setEnabled(suspended)
        bp_rows = []
        for index in range(ida_dbg.get_bpt_qty()):
            bpt = ida_dbg.bpt_t()
            if ida_dbg.getn_bpt(index, bpt):
                bp_rows.append((f'{bpt.ea:#x}', bool(bpt.flags & ida_dbg.BPT_ENABLED), bpt.condition))
        self.populate(self.breakpoints, bp_rows)
        if not suspended:
            self.status.setText('No suspended debuggee. Values below are from the last stop.')
            return
        try:
            ip, sp = ida_dbg.get_ip_val(), ida_dbg.get_sp_val()
            self.status.setText(f'Suspended · IP {ip:#x} {ida_name.get_ea_name(ip)} · SP {sp:#x}')
            rows, changed, current = [], [], {}
            definitions = list(ida_idd.dbg_get_registers() or ())
            primary = ['RIP', 'EIP', 'PC', 'RSP', 'ESP', 'SP', 'RBP', 'EBP',
                       'RAX', 'RBX', 'RCX', 'RDX', 'RSI', 'RDI', 'R8', 'R9',
                       'R10', 'R11', 'R12', 'R13', 'R14', 'R15', 'EAX', 'EBX',
                       'ECX', 'EDX', 'ESI', 'EDI', 'RFLAGS', 'EFLAGS']
            rank = {name: index for index, name in enumerate(primary)}
            definitions.sort(key=lambda definition: rank.get(definition[0].upper(), len(rank)))
            for definition in definitions:
                name = definition[0]
                try:
                    value = ida_dbg.get_reg_val(name)
                except Exception:
                    continue  # Some debugger register classes are unavailable on this target.
                current[name] = value
                detail = ida_name.get_ea_name(value) if isinstance(value, int) else ''
                if name in self.previous and value != self.previous[name]:
                    changed.append(len(rows))
                    if isinstance(value, int) and isinstance(self.previous[name], int):
                        detail += f'  Δ {value - self.previous[name]:+#x}'
                rendered = hex(value) if isinstance(value, int) else value.hex() if isinstance(value, bytes) else str(value)
                rows.append((name, rendered, detail))
            if current == self.previous and self.last_register_rows:
                rows, changed = self.last_register_rows, self.last_changed
            else:
                self.last_register_rows, self.last_changed = rows, changed
            self.populate(self.registers, rows, changed)
            self.previous = current
            size = 8 if ida_ida.inf_is_64bit() else 4
            data = ida_idd.dbg_read_memory(sp, 32 * size) or b''
            order = 'big' if ida_ida.inf_is_be() else 'little'
            stack_rows = []
            for offset in range(0, len(data) - size + 1, size):
                value = int.from_bytes(data[offset:offset+size], order)
                stack_rows.append((f'{sp+offset:#x}', f'{value:#x}', ida_name.get_ea_name(value)))
            self.populate(self.stack, stack_rows)
            address = self.memory_address.text().strip()
            if address:
                try:
                    address = int(address, 16)
                except ValueError:
                    address = ida_name.get_name_ea(ida_idaapi.BADADDR, address)
                    if address == ida_idaapi.BADADDR:
                        raise ValueError('Unknown memory symbol')
            else:
                address = sp
            memory = ida_idd.dbg_read_memory(address, 256) or b''
            lines = []
            for offset in range(0, len(memory), 16):
                chunk = memory[offset:offset+16]
                ascii_text = ''.join(chr(b) if 32 <= b < 127 else '.' for b in chunk)
                lines.append(f'{address+offset:016x}  {chunk.hex(" "):<47}  {ascii_text}')
            self.memory.setPlainText('\n'.join(lines) or 'Memory unavailable at this address.')
            self.last_snapshot = {'ip': ip, 'sp': sp, 'registers': len(rows), 'changed': len(changed), 'stack_rows': len(stack_rows), 'memory_bytes': len(memory), 'breakpoints': len(bp_rows)}
        except Exception as exc:
            self.status.setText(f'Debugger refresh failed: {exc}')
            self.record_stop(str(exc))

    def jump_value(self, item):
        cell = item.tableWidget().item(item.row(), 1)
        try:
            address = int(cell.text(), 16)
            if 0 <= address < ida_idaapi.BADADDR:
                ida_kernwin.jumpto(address)
        except (ValueError, OverflowError):
            pass

    def jump_breakpoint(self, item):
        ida_kernwin.jumpto(int(self.breakpoints.item(item.row(), 0).text(), 16))

    def shutdown(self):
        if not self.closed:
            self.closed = True
            self.state_timer.stop()
            self.hooks.unhook()

    def closeEvent(self, event):
        self.shutdown()
        super().closeEvent(event)


```

`plugin/go_recovery.py`:

```py
"""Current Go pclntab symbol recovery for ELF; preserves analyst-assigned names."""
import json
import hashlib
import subprocess
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

import ida_bytes
import ida_funcs
import ida_kernwin
import ida_name
import ida_nalt
import idaapi

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
HELPER = str(ROOT / "backend" / "go-symbols")  # built with: go build -o go-symbols go_symbols.go


def recover():
    path = ida_nalt.get_input_file_path()
    expected = bytes(ida_nalt.retrieve_input_file_sha256())
    with open(path, "rb") as stream:
        actual = hashlib.file_digest(stream, "sha256").digest()
    if not expected or actual != expected:
        raise RuntimeError("Input file SHA-256 differs from the IDA database; reload the correct binary.")
    result = subprocess.run(
        [HELPER, path],
        capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30, check=False,
    )
    if result.returncode:
        raise RuntimeError(result.stderr.strip() or "Go symbol recovery failed")
    data = json.loads(result.stdout)
    delta = ida_nalt.get_imagebase() - data["image_base"]
    planned, preserved, skipped = [], 0, 0
    for symbol in data["functions"]:
        ea = symbol["address"] + delta
        function = ida_funcs.get_func(ea)
        if function is None or function.start_ea != ea:
            skipped += 1
            continue
        old_name = ida_name.get_name(ea)
        if ida_bytes.has_user_name(ida_bytes.get_flags(ea)) or not old_name.startswith("sub_"):
            preserved += 1
            continue
        name = ida_name.validate_name(symbol["name"], ida_name.VNT_IDENT)
        if name:
            planned.append({"address": hex(ea), "old_name": old_name, "name": name,
                            "go_name": symbol["name"]})
    return {"parsed": len(data["functions"]), "planned": planned, "sha256": actual.hex(),
            "imagebase": ida_nalt.get_imagebase(),
            "preserved": preserved, "skipped": skipped, "rebase_delta": delta}


def apply_plan(plan):
    """Apply only unchanged autogenerated names, retaining a rollback journal."""
    if (bytes(ida_nalt.retrieve_input_file_sha256()).hex() != plan["sha256"]
            or ida_nalt.get_imagebase() != plan["imagebase"]):
        raise RuntimeError("Database changed or rebased since the preview; recover again.")
    stamp = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y%m%dT%H%M%S%fZ")
    journal_dir = ROOT / "exports" / "go-renames"
    journal_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    journal = journal_dir / ("go-renames-" + stamp + ".jsonl")
    applied = 0
    with journal.open("x", encoding="utf-8") as stream:
        stream.write(json.dumps({"input": ida_nalt.get_input_file_path(), "sha256": plan["sha256"],
                                 "imagebase": ida_nalt.get_imagebase()}) + "\n")
        for item in plan["planned"]:
            ea = int(item["address"], 16)
            if ida_name.get_name(ea) != item["old_name"]:
                continue
            if ida_bytes.has_user_name(ida_bytes.get_flags(ea)):
                continue
            stream.write(json.dumps(dict(item, status="pending")) + "\n")
            stream.flush()
            success = bool(ida_name.set_name(ea, item["name"], ida_name.SN_CHECK))
            stream.write(json.dumps(dict(item, status="applied" if success else "failed")) + "\n")
            stream.flush()
            applied += int(success)
    return {"applied": applied, "journal": str(journal)}


class GoRecoverPlugin(idaapi.plugin_t):
    flags = idaapi.PLUGIN_UNL
    comment = "Recover stripped Go ELF function names from pclntab"
    help = "Uses a separate Go stdlib parser and preserves existing user names."
    wanted_name = "Arsenal: Recover Go ELF symbols"
    wanted_hotkey = ""

    def init(self):
        return idaapi.PLUGIN_OK

    def run(self, arg):
        try:
            result = recover()
            ida_kernwin.msg("[Arsenal Go] Parsed %d, candidates %d, preserved %d, skipped %d.\n" %
                           (result["parsed"], len(result["planned"]),
                            result["preserved"], result["skipped"]))
            for item in result["planned"][:25]:
                ida_kernwin.msg("  %s: %s -> %s\n" %
                               (item["address"], item["old_name"], item["name"]))
            if result["planned"] and ida_kernwin.ask_yn(
                    ida_kernwin.ASKBTN_NO,
                    "Recover %d autogenerated Go function names?\n"
                    "Preview is in Output; existing user names are preserved.\n"
                    "Every change is recorded in a rollback journal." % len(result["planned"])
            ) == ida_kernwin.ASKBTN_YES:
                applied = apply_plan(result)
                ida_kernwin.msg("[Arsenal Go] Applied %d; journal: %s\n" %
                               (applied["applied"], applied["journal"]))
        except Exception as error:
            ida_kernwin.warning("Go symbol recovery: %s" % error)

    def term(self):
        pass


def PLUGIN_ENTRY():
    return GoRecoverPlugin()
```

`plugin/input_flow.py`:

```py
"""Observed input destinations and local uses, with reversible persistent names.

Call sites, prompt evidence, and bounded local dependencies. No analyzed code runs.
"""
import hashlib
import html
import json
from pathlib import Path
import re

from PyQt5 import QtCore, QtGui, QtWidgets
import ida_bytes
import ida_funcs
import ida_gdl
import ida_hexrays as hx
import ida_idaapi
import ida_kernwin
import ida_lines
import ida_nalt
import idc
import idautils

from triage_rules import rules_for, normalize_api
from triage_scan import imported_targets, call_sites, check_cancel
from ui_style import polish_table

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]

OUTPUT_ARGUMENTS = {'puts': 0, 'printf': 0, 'fputs': 0, 'fprintf': 1,
                    '__printf_chk': 1, '__fprintf_chk': 2, 'write': 1,
                    'std::ostream output': 1}
RETURN_INPUTS = {'getchar', 'getc', 'fgetc', 'getwchar', 'getenv'}
COPY_APIS = {'memcpy', 'memmove', 'strcpy', 'strncpy'}
READ_ONLY_APIS = {'strlen', 'strnlen', 'puts', 'fputs', 'write'}
CPP_INPUTS = {'std::istream extraction', 'std::getline'}


def input_rules(api):
    if api in CPP_INPUTS:
        return [{'category': 'Input taking', 'buffer_arg': 1}]
    return rules_for(api)


def input_targets():
    """Extend real imports/thunks only; an arbitrary function name is not evidence."""
    imports, targets = imported_targets()
    allowed = set(OUTPUT_ARGUMENTS) | COPY_APIS | READ_ONLY_APIS | RETURN_INPUTS
    targets.update({item['address']: normalize_api(item['name']) for item in imports
                    if normalize_api(item['name']) in allowed})
    for item in imports:
        if not item['name'].startswith('_Z'):
            continue
        demangled = idc.demangle_name(normalize_api(item['name']), idc.get_inf_attr(idc.INF_LONG_DN)) or ''
        stream = 'std::basic_istream<' in demangled or 'std::istream' in demangled
        if stream and not re.search(r'\(\s*\*', demangled) and ('std::operator>>' in demangled or re.search(r'std::(?:basic_istream<.*>|istream)::operator>>', demangled)):
            targets[item['address']] = 'std::istream extraction'
        elif stream and 'std::getline<' in demangled:
            targets[item['address']] = 'std::getline'
        elif 'std::operator<<' in demangled and 'basic_ostream<' in demangled and 'char const*' in demangled:
            targets[item['address']] = 'std::ostream output'
    thunks = []
    for ea in idautils.Functions():
        fn = ida_funcs.get_func(ea)
        if fn and fn.flags & ida_funcs.FUNC_THUNK:
            target = ida_funcs.calc_thunk_func_target(fn)
            thunks.append((ea, target[0] if isinstance(target, tuple) else target))
    for _ in range(8):
        additions = {ea: targets[target] for ea, target in thunks
                     if ea not in targets and target in targets}
        if not additions:
            break
        targets.update(additions)
    return imports, targets


def prompt_candidates(outputs, site, block, max_distance=512):
    """Nearby preceding output in the same block is a clue, never a proven prompt."""
    if block is None:
        return []
    candidates = [dict(output, confidence='heuristic',
                       reason='Literal output precedes the input call in the same basic block; execution and prompt intent are not proven.')
                  for output in outputs
                  if output.get('block') == block and 0 < site - output['site'] <= max_distance
                  and re.search(r'\b(?:enter|input|type|provide|password|passphrase|flag|username)\b|[:?]\s*$',
                                output.get('text', ''), re.I)]
    return sorted(candidates, key=lambda item: site - item['site'])[:3]


def trace_local_events(events, limit=256):
    """Track local dependencies within a machine basic block, stopping on uncertainty.

    Inputs are conditional on successful reads. Routes are evidence paths rather
    than proof of user-controlled bytes; pointer aliases and callees are not solved.
    """
    tracked, flows, previous = {}, [], None
    for event in sorted(events, key=lambda item: (item['site'], item.get('order', 0))):
        block = event.get('block')
        if block is None or block != previous:
            tracked.clear()
        previous = block
        if block is None:
            continue
        kind = event['kind']
        if kind == 'source':
            tracked[event['variable']] = {event['source']: [event]}
        elif kind == 'write':
            destination = event.get('destination')
            if destination is None:
                tracked.clear()
                continue
            dependencies = {}
            for variable in event.get('reads', []):
                for source, route in tracked.get(variable, {}).items():
                    dependencies[source] = route + [event]
            tracked.pop(destination, None)
            if dependencies:
                tracked[destination] = dependencies
        elif kind == 'call':
            api = event.get('api')
            comparison = any(rule['category'] == 'Data comparison' for rule in rules_for(api))
            args = event.get('arguments', [])
            empty = comparison and (event.get('count') == 0 or
                                     len(args) >= 2 and args[0] is not None and args[0] == args[1])
            copy = api in COPY_APIS and len(args) >= 2
            for index, variable in enumerate(args):
                if copy and index == 0 or empty:
                    continue
                for source, route in tracked.get(variable, {}).items():
                    flows.append({'source': source, 'site': event['site'],
                                  'kind': 'Input checking candidate' if comparison else 'Input consumer',
                                  'api': api or 'custom / unresolved call', 'argument': index,
                                  'route': route + [event], 'target': event.get('target'),
                                  'confidence': 'same-basic-block dependency',
                                  'limitation': 'Conditional on a successful input read. The call result and validation intent require inspection.'})
                    if len(flows) >= limit:
                        return flows
            if copy:
                destination, origin = args[:2]
                copied = {source: route + [event] for source, route in tracked.get(origin, {}).items()}
                # The destination may alias another tracked pointer. Keep only
                # the newly established destination, never stale alias facts.
                tracked.clear()
                if destination is None:
                    continue
                else:
                    # Unknown / zero length copies cannot establish a dependency.
                    if copied and (api == 'strcpy' or isinstance(event.get('count'), int) and event['count'] > 0):
                        tracked[destination] = copied
            elif not comparison and api not in READ_ONLY_APIS:
                # Unknown callees may mutate aliases or globals. A later source
                # event reintroduces a known receiver for an input-taking call.
                tracked.clear()
    return flows


def flag(value):
    return bool(value() if callable(value) else value)


def binary_hash():
    return bytes(ida_nalt.retrieve_input_file_sha256()).hex()


def variable_key(var):
    key = {'definition': int(var.defea)}
    if flag(var.is_stk_var):
        key.update(kind='stack', offset=int(var.get_stkoff()))
    elif flag(var.is_reg1):
        key.update(kind='register', register=int(var.get_reg1()))
    elif flag(var.is_reg2):
        key.update(kind='register_pair', registers=[int(var.get_reg1()), int(var.get_reg2())])
    else:
        return None
    return key


def journal_path():
    database = idc.get_idb_path()
    key = hashlib.sha256((database + '\0' + binary_hash()).encode()).hexdigest()[:20]
    directory = ROOT / 'exports' / 'input-renames'
    directory.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    return directory / (key + '.json')


def load_journal():
    path = journal_path()
    if not path.exists():
        return {'database': idc.get_idb_path(), 'binary_sha256': binary_hash(), 'renames': []}
    data = json.loads(path.read_text())
    if data.get('database') != idc.get_idb_path() or data.get('binary_sha256') != binary_hash():
        raise ValueError('Rename journal belongs to a different database or binary.')
    return data


def save_journal(data):
    path = journal_path()
    temporary = path.with_suffix('.tmp')
    temporary.write_text(json.dumps(data, indent=2))
    temporary.replace(path)


def _unwrap(expr):
    while expr.op == hx.cot_cast:
        expr = expr.x
    return expr


def analyze_function(ea, targets=None, known=None, auto_rename=True):
    """Return input sites, receivers, direct occurrences and bounded dependencies."""
    fn = ida_funcs.get_func(ea)
    if not fn:
        raise ValueError('Select an address inside a function.')
    ea = fn.start_ea
    if targets is None:
        _, targets = input_targets()
    if known is None:
        known = call_sites(targets).get(ea, {})
    result = {'function': ea, 'function_name': ida_funcs.get_func_name(ea),
              'inputs': [], 'notes': [], 'renames': [], 'source_sites': [], 'flows': []}
    blocks = [(b.start_ea, b.end_ea) for b in ida_gdl.FlowChart(fn)]
    def block_at(site):
        return next((start for start, end in blocks if start <= site < end), None)
    for call in known.values():
        if any(rule['category'] == 'Input taking' for rule in input_rules(call['api'])):
            result['source_sites'].append(dict(call, title='Input taken here',
                                               receiver='not recovered', prompts=[]))
    if not hx.init_hexrays_plugin():
        result['notes'].append('Hex-Rays unavailable; destination variables cannot be recovered.')
        return result
    if fn.end_ea - fn.start_ea > 65536:
        result['notes'].append('Function exceeds the bounded decompilation budget.')
        return result
    cfunc = hx.decompile(ea)
    if not cfunc:
        result['notes'].append('Decompilation unavailable.')
        return result
    variables = cfunc.get_lvars()
    args = [index for index, var in enumerate(variables) if flag(var.is_arg_var)]
    destinations, occurrences, events, outputs = {}, [], [], []
    imported_data_names = {}
    for module in range(ida_nalt.get_import_module_qty()):
        def remember_import(address, name, ordinal):
            if name:
                imported_data_names[address] = normalize_api(name)
            return True
        ida_nalt.enum_import_names(module, remember_import)

    def root(expr):
        expr = _unwrap(expr)
        if expr.op == hx.cot_ref:
            expr = _unwrap(expr.x)
        return expr.v.idx if expr.op == hx.cot_var else None

    def literal(expr):
        expr = _unwrap(expr)
        return int(expr.numval()) if expr.op == hx.cot_num else None

    def string(expr):
        value = _unwrap(expr)
        if value.op != hx.cot_obj:
            return None
        raw = ida_bytes.get_strlit_contents(value.obj_ea, -1, ida_nalt.STRTYPE_C)
        return raw[:512].decode('utf-8', errors='replace') if raw else None

    def expression_vars(expr):
        found = set()
        class Variables(hx.ctree_visitor_t):
            def __init__(self):
                super().__init__(hx.CV_FAST)
            def visit_expr(self, value):
                if value.op == hx.cot_var:
                    found.add(value.v.idx)
                return 0
        Variables().apply_to(expr, None)
        return sorted(found)

    def add_source(var_index, source):
        source['title'] = 'Input taken here'
        source['prompts'] = []
        source['id'] = '%x:%s' % (source['site'], var_index)
        destinations.setdefault(var_index, []).append(source)
        events.append({'site': source['site'], 'block': block_at(source['site']),
                       'kind': 'source', 'order': 2, 'variable': var_index,
                       'source': source['id'], 'api': source['api']})

    class Visitor(hx.ctree_parentee_t):
        def visit_expr(self, expr):
            if hx.cot_asg <= expr.op <= hx.cot_asgumod:
                lhs = _unwrap(expr.x)
                # Stores through pointers are a conservative alias boundary.
                destination = lhs.v.idx if lhs.op == hx.cot_var else None
                rhs = _unwrap(expr.y)
                reads = expression_vars(rhs) if rhs.op != hx.cot_call else []
                if expr.op != hx.cot_asg and destination is not None:
                    reads = sorted(set(reads) | {destination})
                site = expr.ea
                if rhs.op == hx.cot_call:
                    site = rhs.ea
                events.append({'site': site, 'block': block_at(site), 'kind': 'write',
                               'order': 1 if rhs.op == hx.cot_call else -1,
                               'destination': destination, 'reads': reads,
                               'expression': ida_lines.tag_remove(expr.print1(cfunc) or '')})
            if expr.op == hx.cot_var:
                parents = [parent for parent in self.parents if parent is not None]
                site = expr.ea
                for parent in reversed(parents):
                    if site == ida_idaapi.BADADDR and parent.ea != ida_idaapi.BADADDR:
                        site = parent.ea
                snippet_node = None
                kind, detail = 'Local use', 'Direct variable occurrence; aliases and downstream callees are not followed.'
                for parent in reversed(parents):
                    if parent.op == hx.cot_call:
                        callee = _unwrap(parent.cexpr.x)
                        api = targets.get(callee.obj_ea) if callee.op == hx.cot_obj else None
                        kind = 'Call argument'
                        detail = ('Passed to ' + (api or 'an unresolved/custom call') + '; callee behavior is a trace boundary.')
                        site = parent.ea
                        snippet_node = parent.cexpr
                        break
                    if hx.cot_asg <= parent.op <= hx.cot_asgumod:
                        kind, detail = 'Assignment / possible overwrite', 'Variable occurs in an assignment; this view does not propagate derived values.'
                        site = parent.ea
                        snippet_node = parent.cexpr
                        break
                occurrences.append({'variable_index': expr.v.idx, 'site': site, 'kind': kind, 'detail': detail, '_expression_node': snippet_node})
            if expr.op != hx.cot_call:
                return 0
            callee = _unwrap(expr.x)
            api = targets.get(callee.obj_ea) if callee.op == hx.cot_obj else None
            events.append({'site': expr.ea, 'block': block_at(expr.ea), 'kind': 'call',
                           'order': 0, 'api': api, 'target': callee.obj_ea if callee.op == hx.cot_obj else None,
                           'arguments': [root(arg) for arg in expr.a],
                           'count': literal(expr.a[2]) if len(expr.a) > 2 else None})
            if api in OUTPUT_ARGUMENTS and len(expr.a) > OUTPUT_ARGUMENTS[api]:
                text = string(expr.a[OUTPUT_ARGUMENTS[api]])
                if text and (api != 'write' or literal(expr.a[0]) in (1, 2)):
                    outputs.append({'site': expr.ea, 'block': block_at(expr.ea),
                                    'api': api, 'text': text})
            if (expr.ea, api) not in known:
                return 0
            if api in RETURN_INPUTS:
                for parent in reversed(list(self.parents)):
                    if parent.op == hx.cot_cast:
                        continue
                    if parent.op == hx.cot_asg:
                        var_index = root(parent.cexpr.x)
                        if var_index is not None:
                            add_source(var_index, {'api': api, 'site': expr.ea,
                                       'length': 1 if api != 'getenv' else None,
                                       'receiver_argument': None, 'receiver_kind': 'return value',
                                       'stream': 'stdin' if api in {'getchar', 'getwchar'} else 'environment' if api == 'getenv' else 'unknown'})
                    break
            indices = {rule['buffer_arg'] for rule in input_rules(api)
                       if rule['category'] == 'Input taking' and rule['buffer_arg'] is not None}
            # Varargs destinations require a validated literal format. Do not
            # guess va_list receivers or format-dependent argument positions.
            if api in {'scanf', '__isoc99_scanf', '__isoc23_scanf', 'fscanf', '__isoc99_fscanf', '__isoc23_fscanf'}:
                fmt_index = 1 if 'fscanf' in api else 0
                fmt = _unwrap(expr.a[fmt_index]) if len(expr.a) > fmt_index else None
                raw = ida_bytes.get_strlit_contents(fmt.obj_ea, -1, ida_nalt.STRTYPE_C) if fmt is not None and fmt.op == hx.cot_obj else None
                if raw:
                    text = raw.decode('ascii', errors='replace')
                    tokens = list(re.finditer(r'%(?:%|\*?(?:[1-9][0-9]*)?(?:hh|ll|[hljztL])?[diouxXfFeEgGaAcspn]|\*?(?:[1-9][0-9]*)?\[[^\]]+\])', text))
                    leftovers = re.sub(r'%(?:%|\*?(?:[1-9][0-9]*)?(?:hh|ll|[hljztL])?[diouxXfFeEgGaAcspn]|\*?(?:[1-9][0-9]*)?\[[^\]]+\])', '', text)
                    if '%' not in leftovers and all('$' not in token.group() for token in tokens):
                        position = fmt_index + 1
                        for token in tokens:
                            if token.group() != '%%' and not token.group().startswith('%*'):
                                # %n records a count and does not take input.
                                if not token.group().endswith('n'):
                                    indices.add(position)
                                position += 1
                    else:
                        result['notes'].append('%s at 0x%x: format is unsupported; no receiver guessed.' % (api, expr.ea))
                else:
                    result['notes'].append('%s at 0x%x: format is not a literal; no receiver guessed.' % (api, expr.ea))
            for index in sorted(indices):
                if index >= len(expr.a):
                    continue
                var_index = root(expr.a[index])
                if var_index is None:
                    result['notes'].append('%s at 0x%x: destination is global, offset or an expression; no local renamed.' % (api, expr.ea))
                    continue
                length = None
                length_index = {'read': 2, 'pread': 2, 'recv': 2, 'recvfrom': 2, 'fgets': 1, 'ReadFile': 2, 'ReadConsoleA': 2, 'ReadConsoleW': 2}.get(api)
                if length_index is not None and len(expr.a) > length_index:
                    length = literal(expr.a[length_index])
                if api == 'fread' and len(expr.a) > 2:
                    size, count = literal(expr.a[1]), literal(expr.a[2])
                    length = size * count if size is not None and count is not None else None
                if length is not None and (length <= 0 or (api == 'fgets' and length <= 1)):
                    result['notes'].append('%s at 0x%x: the literal read bound cannot receive input bytes; no input destination added.' % (api, expr.ea))
                    result['source_sites'] = [site for site in result['source_sites'] if site['site'] != expr.ea]
                    continue
                source = {'api': api, 'site': expr.ea, 'length': length,
                          'receiver_argument': index,
                          'stream': ('stdin' if api in {'scanf', '__isoc99_scanf', '__isoc23_scanf', 'gets'} else 'unknown')}
                stream_index = {'fgets': 2, 'fread': 3, 'fscanf': 0, '__isoc99_fscanf': 0,
                                '__isoc23_fscanf': 0}.get(api)
                if stream_index is not None and len(expr.a) > stream_index:
                    stream_expr = _unwrap(expr.a[stream_index])
                    if stream_expr.op == hx.cot_obj:
                        # The exact imported global symbol, never a local variable named stdin.
                        if imported_data_names.get(stream_expr.obj_ea) in {'stdin', '__stdinp'}:
                            source['stream'] = 'stdin'
                if api in {'read', 'pread', 'recv', 'recvfrom'} and len(expr.a):
                    source['descriptor'] = literal(expr.a[0])
                    if api == 'read' and source['descriptor'] == 0:
                        source['stream'] = 'stdin'
                add_source(var_index, source)
            return 0
    Visitor().apply_to(cfunc.body, None)
    # A recognized C main signature exposes argv at function entry. The vector
    # itself also contains argv[0]; never pretend every element is user data.
    if result['function_name'] in {'main', '_main', 'wmain', '_wmain'} and len(args) >= 2:
        argv_index = args[1]
        argv_type = variables[argv_index].type()
        if argv_type.is_ptr() and argv_type.get_pointed_object().is_ptr() and any(use['variable_index'] == argv_index for use in occurrences):
            add_source(argv_index, {'api': 'argv', 'site': ea, 'length': None,
                                   'receiver_argument': 1, 'receiver_kind': 'argument vector',
                                   'stream': 'argv', 'includes_program_name': True})
            result['notes'].append('argv is supplied at main entry. argv[0] is the executable name; inspect the actual element and argc before configuring a solver.')
    result['flows'] = trace_local_events(events)
    if len(result['flows']) >= 256:
        result['notes'].append('Local dependency results reached the 256-consumer display budget; additional consumers may exist.')
    def nearby_prompts(site):
        previous_read = max((other['site'] for other in result['source_sites']
                             if other['site'] < site and block_at(other['site']) == block_at(site)), default=-1)
        return prompt_candidates([output for output in outputs if output['site'] > previous_read], site, block_at(site))
    for sources in destinations.values():
        for source in sources:
            source['prompts'] = nearby_prompts(source['site'])
    for site in result['source_sites']:
        site['prompts'] = nearby_prompts(site['site'])
        receivers = [source['id'] for sources in destinations.values() for source in sources
                     if source['site'] == site['site']]
        if receivers:
            site['receiver'] = 'recovered'
            site['receiver_ids'] = receivers
    journal = load_journal()
    saved = hx.lvar_uservec_t()
    hx.restore_user_lvar_settings(saved, ea)
    names = {var.name for var in variables}
```
