```py
    for var_index, sources in sorted(destinations.items(), key=lambda pair: min(s['site'] for s in pair[1])):
        var = variables[var_index]
        old = var.name
        own = next((entry for entry in journal['renames'] if entry['function'] == ea and entry['new'] == old and entry.get('locator') == variable_key(var) and entry.get('status') == 'applied' and not entry.get('undone')), None)
        saved_info = saved.find_info(var)
        user_named = bool(saved_info and saved_info.name)
        if auto_rename and not any(source['api'] == 'argv' for source in sources) and not user_named and not own and variable_key(var) is not None:
            number = 1
            while 'input%d' % number in names:
                number += 1
            new = 'input%d' % number
            entry = {'function': ea, 'old': old, 'new': new, 'variable_index': var_index,
                     'status': 'pending', 'locator': variable_key(var)}
            journal['renames'].append(entry)
            save_journal(journal)
            if hx.rename_lvar(ea, old, new):
                entry['status'] = 'applied'
                names.discard(old)
                names.add(new)
                result['renames'].append(entry.copy())
                var.name = new
                old = new
            else:
                entry['status'] = 'failed'
            save_journal(journal)
        capacity = int(var.type().get_size()) if var.type().is_array() else None
        record = {'name': old, 'variable_index': var_index, 'sources': sorted(sources, key=lambda s: s['site']),
                  'capacity': capacity, 'argument_index': args.index(var_index) if var_index in args else None,
                  'preserved_user_name': user_named and not own,
                  'flows': [flow for flow in result['flows'] if flow['source'] in {s['id'] for s in sources}],
                  'uses': []}
        seen = set()
        for use in sorted(occurrences, key=lambda use: use['site']):
            key = (use['site'], use['kind'])
            if use['variable_index'] == var_index and key not in seen:
                seen.add(key)
                record['uses'].append(use.copy())
        result['inputs'].append(record)
    for record in result['inputs']:
        for use in record['uses']:
            node = use.pop('_expression_node', None)
            use['expression'] = ida_lines.tag_remove(node.print1(cfunc) or '') if node is not None else ''
    return result


def undo_renames():
    journal = load_journal()
    restored, skipped = 0, 0
    for entry in reversed(journal['renames']):
        if entry.get('undone') or entry.get('status') != 'applied':
            continue
        cfunc = hx.decompile(entry['function'])
        variables = cfunc.get_lvars() if cfunc else []
        names = {var.name for var in variables}
        matches = [var for var in variables if variable_key(var) == entry.get('locator') and var.name == entry['new']]
        if len(matches) == 1 and entry['old'] not in names and hx.rename_lvar(entry['function'], entry['new'], entry['old']):
            entry['undone'] = True
            restored += 1
        else:
            skipped += 1
    save_journal(journal)
    return restored, skipped


class InputFlowPanel(QtWidgets.QWidget):
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.solver_callback = None
        self.records = {}
        layout = QtWidgets.QVBoxLayout(self)
        title = QtWidgets.QLabel('<h2>Input entry points & flow</h2><p>Start at the read instruction, inspect nearby prompts, then follow local dependencies to consumers and comparisons.</p>')
        title.setWordWrap(True)
        layout.addWidget(title)
        bar = QtWidgets.QHBoxLayout()
        for text, handler in [('Current function', self.analyze_current), ('Scan database', self.scan_database), ('Undo our names', self.undo), ('Configure symbolic inputs', self.configure_solver)]:
            button = QtWidgets.QPushButton(text)
            button.clicked.connect(lambda checked=False, fn=handler: fn())
            bar.addWidget(button)
        layout.addLayout(bar)
        self.auto_rename = QtWidgets.QCheckBox('Automatically name input destinations input1, input2, … (saved analyst names are preserved)')
        self.auto_rename.setChecked(True)
        layout.addWidget(self.auto_rename)
        self.status = QtWidgets.QLabel('Analyze a function or scan the database. Analyst names are preserved.')
        self.status.setWordWrap(True)
        layout.addWidget(self.status)
        splitter = QtWidgets.QSplitter(QtCore.Qt.Vertical)
        self.table = QtWidgets.QTableWidget(0, 6)
        self.table.setHorizontalHeaderLabels(['Input taken at', 'Input / receiver', 'Function', 'Source', 'Read bound', 'Prompt clue'])
        polish_table(self.table)
        self.table.setSelectionBehavior(QtWidgets.QAbstractItemView.SelectRows)
        self.table.setSelectionMode(QtWidgets.QAbstractItemView.SingleSelection)
        self.table.setEditTriggers(QtWidgets.QAbstractItemView.NoEditTriggers)
        self.table.horizontalHeader().setSectionResizeMode(QtWidgets.QHeaderView.ResizeToContents)
        self.table.horizontalHeader().setStretchLastSection(True)
        self.table.itemSelectionChanged.connect(self.show_details)
        splitter.addWidget(self.table)
        self.details = QtWidgets.QTextBrowser()
        text_light = self.details.palette().color(QtGui.QPalette.Text).lightness() > 128
        self.details.document().setDefaultStyleSheet('a { color: %s; } code { font-family: monospace; }' % ('#8ecbff' if text_light else '#165b9c'))
        self.details.setOpenLinks(False)
        self.details.setOpenExternalLinks(False)
        self.details.anchorClicked.connect(self.jump)
        splitter.addWidget(self.details)
        splitter.setStretchFactor(0, 2)
        splitter.setStretchFactor(1, 3)
        layout.addWidget(splitter, 1)

    def analyze_current(self):
        try:
            result = analyze_function(ida_kernwin.get_screen_ea(), auto_rename=self.auto_rename.isChecked())
            self.records[result['function']] = result
            self.populate()
        except Exception as error:
            self.status.setText('Input analysis failed: ' + str(error))

    def scan_database(self, report=None):
        try:
            _, targets = input_targets()
            calls = call_sites(targets)
            results = {}
            ida_kernwin.show_wait_box('Finding direct input destinations…')
            for ea, known in calls.items():
                check_cancel()
                if any(any(rule['category'] == 'Input taking' for rule in input_rules(call['api'])) for call in known.values()):
                    results[ea] = analyze_function(ea, targets, known, self.auto_rename.isChecked())
            for name in ('main', '_main', 'wmain', '_wmain'):
                ea = idc.get_name_ea_simple(name)
                if ea != ida_idaapi.BADADDR and ea not in results:
                    check_cancel()
                    results[ea] = analyze_function(ea, targets, calls.get(ea, {}), self.auto_rename.isChecked())
            self.records = results
            self.populate()
        except Exception as error:
            self.status.setText('Input scan stopped: ' + str(error))
        finally:
            ida_kernwin.hide_wait_box()

    def populate(self):
        self.table.setRowCount(0)
        renamed = sum(len(result['renames']) for result in self.records.values())
        count = 0
        for ea, result in self.records.items():
            for index, item in enumerate(result['inputs']):
                row = self.table.rowCount()
                self.table.insertRow(row)
                prompts = [prompt['text'] for source in item['sources'] for prompt in source.get('prompts', [])]
                values = [', '.join('0x%x' % s['site'] for s in item['sources']), item['name'], result['function_name'], ', '.join(dict.fromkeys(s['api'] for s in item['sources'])),
                          ', '.join(str(s['length']) if s['length'] is not None else 'unknown' for s in item['sources']),
                          prompts[0] if prompts else '—']
                for column, value in enumerate(values):
                    cell = QtWidgets.QTableWidgetItem(value)
                    cell.setData(QtCore.Qt.UserRole, (ea, index))
                    self.table.setItem(row, column, cell)
                count += 1
            for index, source in enumerate(result.get('source_sites', [])):
                if source['receiver'] != 'not recovered':
                    continue
                row = self.table.rowCount()
                self.table.insertRow(row)
                values = ['0x%x' % source['site'], 'receiver not recovered', result['function_name'], source['api'], 'unknown',
                          source['prompts'][0]['text'] if source.get('prompts') else '—']
                for column, value in enumerate(values):
                    cell = QtWidgets.QTableWidgetItem(value)
                    cell.setData(QtCore.Qt.UserRole, (ea, -index - 1))
                    self.table.setItem(row, column, cell)
        self.status.setText('%d input destinations · %d functions analyzed · %d names changed. Flow is bounded to one basic block; prompts and validation intent remain candidates.' % (count, len(self.records), renamed))
        if self.table.rowCount():
            self.table.selectRow(0)
        else:
            notes = [note for record in self.records.values() for note in record['notes']]
            self.details.setPlainText('No direct local input destinations recovered.\n' + '\n'.join(notes))

    def selected(self):
        row = self.table.currentRow()
        if row < 0 or not self.table.item(row, 0):
            return None, None
        ea, index = self.table.item(row, 0).data(QtCore.Qt.UserRole)
        if index < 0:
            return self.records[ea], {'unresolved_source': self.records[ea]['source_sites'][-index - 1]}
        return self.records[ea], self.records[ea]['inputs'][index]

    def show_details(self):
        result, item = self.selected()
        if not item:
            return
        esc = html.escape
        def link(ea):
            return '<a href="ida:%x">0x%x</a>' % (ea, ea) if ea != ida_idaapi.BADADDR else 'address unavailable'
        if 'unresolved_source' in item:
            source = item['unresolved_source']
            parts = ['<h2>Input-taking call · %s</h2><p>%s · %s</p>' % (esc(source['api']), link(source['site']), esc(result['function_name'])),
                     '<p>The call is observed, but its receiver was not recovered. Open this address to inspect its arguments. The read may fail or request zero bytes.</p>']
            for prompt in source.get('prompts', []):
                parts.append('<h3>Possible prompt</h3><p>%s · %s<br>%s</p>' % (esc(prompt['text']), link(prompt['site']), esc(prompt['reason'])))
            parts.extend('<p>%s</p>' % esc(note) for note in result['notes'])
            self.details.setHtml(''.join(parts))
            return
        parts = ['<h2>Input destination · %s</h2>' % esc(item['name']), '<p>%s · %s</p>' % (esc(result['function_name']), link(result['function'])),
                 '<p><b>Scope:</b> observed read destinations and bounded same-basic-block dependencies. Successful input, runtime reachability and validation intent still require inspection.</p>', '<h3>Input taken here</h3>']
        for source in item['sources']:
            parts.append('<p><b>%s</b> at %s · read bound: %s · stream: %s%s</p>' % (esc(source['api']), link(source['site']), source['length'] if source['length'] is not None else 'unknown', esc(source['stream']), ' · destination reused by multiple reads' if len(item['sources']) > 1 else ''))
            for prompt in source.get('prompts', []):
                parts.append('<p><b>Possible prompt</b> · %s<br><code>%s</code><br>%s</p>' % (link(prompt['site']), esc(prompt['text']), esc(prompt['reason'])))
        if item['capacity'] is not None:
            parts.append('<p>Inferred array capacity: %d bytes. A requested read may fail, read fewer bytes, or exceed capacity.</p>' % item['capacity'])
        parts.append('<h3>Where this input goes</h3>')
        for flow in item.get('flows', []):
            route = ' → '.join(('%s %s' % (esc(step.get('api') or step['kind']), link(step['site']))) for step in flow['route'])
            parts.append('<p><b>%s · %s</b> · %s · argument %d<br>%s<br>%s</p>' %
                         (esc(flow['kind']), esc(flow['api']), link(flow['site']), flow['argument'], route, esc(flow['limitation'])))
            if flow.get('target') is not None:
                parts.append('<p>Inspect callee: %s</p>' % link(flow['target']))
        if not item.get('flows'):
            parts.append('<p>No bounded dependency reached a consumer. Inspect direct uses below; branches, unknown stores and custom calls stop propagation.</p>')
        parts.append('<h3>All direct uses · may precede input or follow overwrite</h3>')
        for use in item['uses'][:256]:
            parts.append('<p><b>%s</b> · %s<br>%s</p>' % (esc(use['kind']), link(use['site']), esc(use['detail']) + ('<br><code>' + esc(use.get('expression', '')) + '</code>' if use.get('expression') else '')))
        for note in result['notes']:
            parts.append('<p>%s</p>' % esc(note))
        self.details.setHtml(''.join(parts))

    def jump(self, url):
        if re.fullmatch(r'ida:[0-9a-fA-F]+', url.toString()):
            ea = int(url.toString()[4:], 16)
            if ida_bytes.is_mapped(ea):
                ida_kernwin.jumpto(ea)

    def undo(self):
        try:
            restored, skipped = undo_renames()
            self.status.setText('%d names restored; %d skipped because the current name changed or conflicts. Disable automatic naming before scanning to keep defaults.' % (restored, skipped))
        except Exception as error:
            self.status.setText('Undo failed: ' + str(error))

    def configure_solver(self):
        result, selected = self.selected()
        if not result:
            self.status.setText('Select an input destination first.')
            return
        if 'unresolved_source' in selected:
            self.status.setText('Receiver not recovered. Inspect the input call and configure symbolic inputs manually.')
            return
        if not callable(self.solver_callback):
            self.status.setText('The symbolic input editor is unavailable.')
            return
        inputs = []
        warnings = []
        if any(len(item['sources']) > 1 for item in result['inputs']):
            self.status.setText('Transfer blocked: a destination receives multiple reads. Configure one symbolic input per actual read in the solver editor after checking control flow.')
            return
        if any(source['stream'] != 'stdin' for item in result['inputs'] for source in item['sources']):
            self.status.setText('Transfer blocked: one or more source streams are not proven stdin. Inspect the descriptor/stream and configure the solver manually.')
            return
        for item in result['inputs']:
            lengths = [source['length'] for source in item['sources'] if source['length'] is not None and source['length'] > 0]
            inputs.append({'name': item['name'], 'length': min(lengths) if lengths else min(item['capacity'] or 16, 256),
                           'argument_index': item['argument_index'], 'sources': item['sources'], 'capacity': item['capacity']})
        warnings.append('Detected lengths are suggestions. Review sources, argument types, stream ordering and bounds before Explore.')
        self.solver_callback({'function': result['function'], 'inputs': inputs,
                              'mode': 'stdin', 'warnings': warnings})

```

`plugin/listing_tools.py`:

```py
"""Keyboard search, local identifier focus, and non-persistent listing colors."""
import re

from PyQt5 import QtWidgets
import ida_funcs
import ida_idp
import ida_idaapi
import ida_kernwin
import ida_lines
import ida_search
import ida_ua


_IDENTIFIER = re.compile(r"[A-Za-z_$][A-Za-z0-9_$@]*")


def match_ranges(text, term, whole_identifier=False):
    """Return offsets into a tag-free listing line."""
    if not term:
        return []
    if whole_identifier:
        pattern = r"(?<![A-Za-z0-9_$@])%s(?![A-Za-z0-9_$@])" % re.escape(term)
        return [(item.start(), item.end() - item.start())
                for item in re.finditer(pattern, text)]
    return [(item.start(), item.end() - item.start())
            for item in re.finditer(re.escape(term), text, re.IGNORECASE)]


def identifier_at(text, column):
    """Find the identifier under a listing cursor column."""
    column = max(0, min(int(column), len(text)))
    for item in _IDENTIFIER.finditer(text):
        if item.start() <= column < item.end() or (column == len(text) and item.end() == column):
            return item.group(0)
    return ""


class ListingTools:
    ACTIONS = (
        ("find", "Find in current listing", "Ctrl+Shift+F"),
        ("next", "Find next", "Ctrl+Shift+N"),
        ("previous", "Find previous", "Ctrl+Shift+P"),
        ("focus", "Focus identifier occurrences", None),
        ("clear", "Clear listing focus", None),
    )

    def __init__(self):
        self.term = ""
        self.whole_identifier = False
        self.function_range = None
        self.hooks = ListingRenderHooks(self)
        self.actions = []

    @staticmethod
    def supported(widget):
        return widget is not None and ida_kernwin.get_widget_type(widget) in (
            ida_kernwin.BWN_DISASM, ida_kernwin.BWN_PSEUDOCODE)

    def register(self):
        self.hooks.hook()
        for kind, title, shortcut in self.ACTIONS:
            name = "arsenal:listing_" + kind
            handler = ListingAction(self, kind)
            desc = ida_kernwin.action_desc_t(name, title, handler, shortcut)
            registered = ida_kernwin.register_action(desc)
            if registered:
                self.actions.append(name)
                ida_kernwin.attach_action_to_menu("Edit/Plugins/", name, ida_kernwin.SETMENU_APP)
            else:
                ida_kernwin.msg("[Arsenal] Could not register listing action: %s\n" % title)

    def unregister(self):
        self.hooks.unhook()
        for name in self.actions:
            ida_kernwin.unregister_action(name)
        self.actions.clear()

    def activate(self, kind, widget=None):
        widget = widget or ida_kernwin.get_current_viewer()
        if not self.supported(widget):
            ida_kernwin.msg("[Arsenal] Open a disassembly or pseudocode listing first.\n")
            return
        if kind == "find":
            self.find_prompt(widget)
        elif kind == "next":
            self.navigate(widget, ida_search.SEARCH_DOWN)
        elif kind == "previous":
            self.navigate(widget, ida_search.SEARCH_UP)
        elif kind == "focus":
            self.focus_identifier(widget)
        elif kind == "clear":
            self.clear_focus(widget)

    def _function_for(self, widget):
        ea = self._viewer_ea(widget)
        return ida_funcs.get_func(ea) if ea != ida_idaapi.BADADDR else None

    @staticmethod
    def _viewer_ea(widget, mouse=False):
        try:
            place, _, _ = ida_kernwin.get_custom_viewer_place(widget, mouse)
            return place.toea() if place else ida_idaapi.BADADDR
        except Exception:
            return ida_idaapi.BADADDR

    def _cursor_word(self, widget, mouse=False):
        try:
            line = ida_lines.tag_remove(ida_kernwin.get_custom_viewer_curline(widget, mouse))
            _, column, _ = ida_kernwin.get_custom_viewer_place(widget, mouse)
            return identifier_at(line, column)
        except Exception as exc:
            ida_kernwin.msg("[Arsenal] Could not read the listing cursor word: %s\n" % exc)
            return ""

    def find_prompt(self, widget):
        default = self._cursor_word(widget)
        term, accepted = QtWidgets.QInputDialog.getText(
            QtWidgets.QApplication.activeWindow(), "Find in listing",
            "Text in the current listing:", QtWidgets.QLineEdit.Normal, default)
        if not accepted or not term:
            return
        self._set_focus(term, widget, False)
        self.navigate(widget, ida_search.SEARCH_DOWN)

    def focus_identifier(self, widget):
        term = self._cursor_word(widget, True) or self._cursor_word(widget)
        if not term:
            term, accepted = QtWidgets.QInputDialog.getText(
                QtWidgets.QApplication.activeWindow(), "Focus identifier",
                "Identifier to highlight in this function:")
            if not accepted or not term:
                return
        self._set_focus(term, widget, True)
        ida_kernwin.msg("[Arsenal] Highlighting '%s' in the current function; use Ctrl+Shift+N/P to move between matches.\n" % term)

    def _set_focus(self, term, widget, whole_identifier):
        fn = self._function_for(widget)
        self.term = term
        self.whole_identifier = whole_identifier
        self.function_range = (fn.start_ea, fn.end_ea) if fn else None
        ida_kernwin.refresh_custom_viewer(widget)
        ida_kernwin.refresh_idaview_anyway()

    def clear_focus(self, widget):
        self.term = ""
        self.whole_identifier = False
        self.function_range = None
        ida_kernwin.refresh_custom_viewer(widget)
        ida_kernwin.refresh_idaview_anyway()

    def navigate(self, widget, direction):
        if not self.term:
            self.find_prompt(widget)
            return
        start = self._viewer_ea(widget)
        if start == ida_idaapi.BADADDR:
            start = ida_kernwin.get_screen_ea()
        flags = direction | ida_search.SEARCH_NEXT
        found = ida_search.find_text(start, 0, 0, self.term, flags)
        fn = self._function_for(widget)
        bounds = (fn.start_ea, fn.end_ea) if fn else None
        if bounds and not bounds[0] <= found < bounds[1]:
            wrap_start = bounds[1] if direction == ida_search.SEARCH_UP else bounds[0] - 1
            found = ida_search.find_text(wrap_start, 0, 0, self.term, flags)
            if not bounds[0] <= found < bounds[1]:
                found = ida_idaapi.BADADDR
        if found == ida_idaapi.BADADDR:
            ida_kernwin.msg("[Arsenal] No more matches for '%s' in this function.\n" % self.term)
            return
        ida_kernwin.jumpto(found)


class ListingAction(ida_kernwin.action_handler_t):
    def __init__(self, owner, kind):
        super().__init__()
        self.owner, self.kind = owner, kind

    def activate(self, ctx):
        self.owner.activate(self.kind, getattr(ctx, "widget", None))
        return 1

    def update(self, ctx):
        return (ida_kernwin.AST_ENABLE_FOR_IDB if
                self.owner.supported(getattr(ctx, "widget", None)) else
                ida_kernwin.AST_DISABLE_FOR_IDB)


class ListingRenderHooks(ida_kernwin.UI_Hooks):
    """Render call and identifier overlays without modifying IDB item colors."""
    def __init__(self, owner):
        super().__init__()
        self.owner = owner

    def get_lines_rendering_info(self, out, widget, rendering_info):
        widget_type = ida_kernwin.get_widget_type(widget)
        if widget_type not in (ida_kernwin.BWN_DISASM, ida_kernwin.BWN_PSEUDOCODE):
            return
        focus = self.owner.term
        focus_range = self.owner.function_range
        exact = self.owner.whole_identifier
        for section in rendering_info.sections_lines:
            for line in section:
                ea = line.at.toea()
                if ea == ida_idaapi.BADADDR:
                    continue
                if (widget_type == ida_kernwin.BWN_DISASM and
                        self._is_call(ea)):
                    entry = ida_kernwin.line_rendering_output_entry_t(line)
                    entry.bg_color = ida_kernwin.CK_EXTRA5
                    entry.flags |= ida_kernwin.LROEF_FULL_LINE
                    out.entries.push_back(entry)
                if (not focus or not focus_range or
                        not focus_range[0] <= ea < focus_range[1]):
                    continue
                plain = ida_lines.tag_remove(line.line)
                for column, count in match_ranges(plain, focus, exact):
                    entry = ida_kernwin.line_rendering_output_entry_t(line)
                    entry.bg_color = ida_kernwin.CK_EXTRA2
                    entry.cpx = column
                    entry.nchars = count
                    entry.flags |= ida_kernwin.LROEF_CPS_RANGE
                    out.entries.push_back(entry)

    @staticmethod
    def _is_call(ea):
        insn = ida_ua.insn_t()
        return bool(ida_ua.decode_insn(insn, ea) and ida_idp.is_call_insn(insn))


class ListingPopupHook(ida_kernwin.UI_Hooks):
    def finish_populating_widget_popup(self, widget, popup):
        if not ListingTools.supported(widget):
            return
        for kind, _, _ in ListingTools.ACTIONS:
            ida_kernwin.attach_action_to_popup(
                widget, popup, "arsenal:listing_" + kind, "Arsenal/Listing tools/")

```

`plugin/outcome_scan.py`:

```py
"""Bounded, read-only output discovery; candidates are never proof of acceptance.

The pure classifier is deliberately independent of IDA. The adapter adds code
references and direct literal output evidence. No analyzed code is executed.
"""
import re

_FAILURE = re.compile(r"\b(?:incorrect|wrong|invalid|denied|rejected|failed|failure)\b|\b(?:not|isn't|is not|never)\s+(?:correct|valid|accepted|successful)\b|\btry again\b", re.I)
_SUCCESS = re.compile(r"^(?:yes[!,.: ]+)?(?:correct|success|accepted|congratulations|congrats|well done|good job|you win|access granted|flag accepted|password accepted|welcome[, ]+admin(?:istrator)?)(?:[!.,:\s]|$)", re.I)
_PROMPT = re.compile(r"\b(?:enter|provide|type|input|give me)\s+(?:(?:the|your|a|an)\s+)?(?:correct\s+)?(?:flag|password|passphrase|key|input|secret|code|name|username|number)\b|^(?:flag|password|passphrase|username)\s*[:?]", re.I)


def classify_message(message):
    """Return a lexical candidate or None; never equate wording with behavior."""
    if not isinstance(message, str) or not message or len(message) > 512:
        return None
    text = message.strip()
    if not text or any(ord(ch) < 32 and ch not in '\n\r\t' for ch in text):
        return None
    # Negation and failure precede positive wording (notably 'incorrect').
    if _FAILURE.search(text):
        return {'kind': 'failure', 'reason': 'Failure or rejection wording'}
    if _PROMPT.search(text):
        return {'kind': 'prompt', 'reason': 'Explicit request for user input'}
    if _SUCCESS.search(text):
        # These describe instrumentation or advice rather than acceptance.
        if re.search(r'\b(?:rate|count|counter|probability|example|message|syntax|usage|format|method|procedure|answer is|answer should|answer must)\b', text, re.I):
            return None
        return {'kind': 'success', 'reason': 'Positive outcome wording'}
    return None


def literal_printf(text):
    """Return fixed printf output only when no interpolation is present."""
    remainder = text.replace('%%', '')
    return None if '%' in remainder else text.replace('%%', '%')


def output_api(name):
    """Normalize only supported output APIs and explicit ABI decorations."""
    if not isinstance(name, str):
        return None
    name = name.split('@', 1)[0]
    for prefix in ('__imp_', 'imp_', 'j_'):
        if name.startswith(prefix):
            name = name[len(prefix):]
    if name in {'puts', 'printf', '__printf_chk'}:
        return name
    if name in {'_puts', '_printf'}:
        return name[1:]
    return None


def rank_candidates(candidates):
    """Attach safe-to-offer stdout text, rejecting substring false goals."""
    failures = [item['message'] for item in candidates if item['kind'] == 'failure']
    for item in candidates:
        item['stdout_find'] = None
        item['goal_address'] = None
        if item['kind'] != 'success' or not item.get('literal_stdout'):
            continue
        output = item.get('output_text', item['message'])
        if not output or any(output in failure for failure in failures):
            item['evidence'].append('Cannot suggest stdout: success text also occurs in rejection text.')
            continue
        item['stdout_find'] = output
    qualified = [item for item in candidates if item['stdout_find'] and
                 any(other['kind'] == 'failure' and other['function'] == item['function']
                     and other.get('literal_stdout') for other in candidates)]
    messages = {item['stdout_find'] for item in qualified}
    candidates.sort(key=lambda item: (-item['score'], item['function'], item['site']))
    return next(iter(messages)) if len(messages) == 1 else None


def scan_outcomes(max_strings=25000, max_functions=100, max_function_bytes=32768):
    """Find referenced messages and, where available, confirm output arguments.

    stdout suggestions use literal puts/printf/__printf_chk calls to verified
    imports. Conditional expressions, variable aliases, Rust formatting and
    wrappers remain reference-only candidates. These are explicit boundaries.
    """
    import ida_bytes
    import ida_funcs
    import ida_idaapi
    import ida_kernwin
    import ida_nalt
    import idautils

    report = {'candidates': [], 'suggested_stdout': None,
              'notes': ['Message wording is a hypothesis about program behavior, not proof of acceptance.',
                        'A message reference is never used as a success address; conditional selection can reference both outcomes.',
                        'Only direct literal puts/printf output is eligible for a stdout suggestion. Wrappers, aliases, and runtime formatting remain unconfirmed.'],
              'coverage': {'strings': 0, 'candidate_functions': 0, 'decompiled': 0,
                           'decompile_errors': 0, 'skipped_functions': 0, 'truncated': False}}
    candidates = report['candidates']
    records = {}
    functions = set()
    for index, string in enumerate(idautils.Strings()):
        if index >= max_strings:
            report['coverage']['truncated'] = True
            break
        if index % 128 == 0 and ida_kernwin.user_cancelled():
            raise InterruptedError('Outcome scan cancelled.')
        report['coverage']['strings'] += 1
        # stdout matcher is UTF-8; don't misrepresent wide-string byte output.
        if string.strtype != ida_nalt.STRTYPE_C:
            continue
        raw = ida_bytes.get_strlit_contents(string.ea, -1, ida_nalt.STRTYPE_C)
        if not raw:
            continue
        try:
            message = raw.decode('utf-8')
        except UnicodeDecodeError:
            continue
        classification = classify_message(message)
        if not classification:
            continue
        for ref_index, ref in enumerate(idautils.XrefsTo(string.ea)):
            if ref_index >= 512:
                report['coverage']['truncated'] = True
                break
            fn = ida_funcs.get_func(ref.frm)
            if not fn or not ida_bytes.is_code(ida_bytes.get_flags(ref.frm)):
                continue
            functions.add(fn.start_ea)
            item = {'kind': classification['kind'], 'message': message,
                    'string_ea': int(string.ea), 'site': int(ref.frm),
                    'function': int(fn.start_ea), 'function_name': ida_funcs.get_func_name(fn.start_ea),
                    'evidence': [classification['reason'], 'Code references this string; output and branch meaning are not yet confirmed.'],
                    'status': 'Referenced wording candidate', 'score': 30,
                    'literal_stdout': False, 'stdout_find': None, 'goal_address': None}
            records.setdefault((fn.start_ea, string.ea), []).append(item)
            candidates.append(item)
    report['coverage']['candidate_functions'] = len(functions)
    try:
        import ida_hexrays as hx
        available = hx.init_hexrays_plugin()
    except ImportError:
        available = False
    if not available:
        report['notes'].append('Hex-Rays unavailable; literal output confirmation skipped.')
        rank_candidates(candidates)
        return report

    targets = {}
    supported = {'puts': 0, 'printf': 0, '__printf_chk': 1}
    for index in range(ida_nalt.get_import_module_qty()):
        def visit(ea, name, ordinal):
            canonical = output_api(name or '')
            if canonical in supported:
                targets[ea] = canonical
            return True
        ida_nalt.enum_import_names(index, visit)
    thunks = []
    for ea in idautils.Functions():
        fn = ida_funcs.get_func(ea)
        if fn and fn.flags & ida_funcs.FUNC_THUNK:
            target = ida_funcs.calc_thunk_func_target(fn)
            thunks.append((ea, target[0] if isinstance(target, tuple) else target))
    for _ in range(8):
        additions = {ea: targets[target] for ea, target in thunks if ea not in targets and target in targets}
        if not additions:
            break
        targets.update(additions)

    def unwrap(expr):
        while expr.op == hx.cot_cast:
            expr = expr.x
        return expr

    for index, ea in enumerate(sorted(functions)):
        if ida_kernwin.user_cancelled():
            raise InterruptedError('Outcome scan cancelled.')
        fn = ida_funcs.get_func(ea)
        if index >= max_functions or fn.end_ea - fn.start_ea > max_function_bytes:
            report['coverage']['skipped_functions'] += 1
            report['coverage']['truncated'] = True
            continue
        try:
            cfunc = hx.decompile(ea)
            if not cfunc:
                report['coverage']['decompile_errors'] += 1
                continue
            class Visitor(hx.ctree_visitor_t):
                def __init__(self):
                    super().__init__(hx.CV_FAST)

                def visit_expr(self, expr):
                    if expr.op != hx.cot_call or expr.ea == ida_idaapi.BADADDR:
                        return 0
                    callee = unwrap(expr.x)
                    api = targets.get(callee.obj_ea) if callee.op == hx.cot_obj else None
                    if api not in supported or len(expr.a) <= supported[api]:
                        return 0
                    value = unwrap(expr.a[supported[api]])
                    if value.op != hx.cot_obj:
                        return 0
                    for record in records.get((ea, value.obj_ea), []):
                        output = record['message'] if api == 'puts' else literal_printf(record['message'])
                        if output is None:
                            record['evidence'].append('Output format contains interpolation; fixed stdout text is not established.')
                            continue
                        record.update(site=int(expr.ea), status='Direct literal stdout candidate', score=80,
                                      literal_stdout=True, output_text=output)
                        record['evidence'] = [entry for entry in record['evidence']
                                              if entry != 'Code references this string; output and branch meaning are not yet confirmed.']
                        record['evidence'].append('Literal argument to verified imported %s at %#x; acceptance meaning remains a lexical hypothesis.' % (api, expr.ea))
                    return 0
            Visitor().apply_to(cfunc.body, None)
            report['coverage']['decompiled'] += 1
        except Exception as error:
            report['coverage']['decompile_errors'] += 1
            report['notes'].append('Function %#x: %s: %s' % (ea, type(error).__name__, error))
    report['suggested_stdout'] = rank_candidates(candidates)
    return report

```

`plugin/protection_scan.py`:

```py
"""Bounded protection observations and UPX extraction to a separate artifact.

Entropy, section names and control-flow shapes are evidence for analyst review,
not proof of a packer, virtualization or an opaque predicate.
"""
from collections import Counter
import hashlib
import math
import os
from pathlib import Path
import shutil
import struct
import subprocess
import tempfile
import time


def entropy(data):
    if not data:
        return None
    size = len(data)
    return -sum((n / size) * math.log2(n / size) for n in Counter(data).values())


def sample_ranges(start, size, budget=65536, windows=8):
    """Nonoverlapping samples spread across a range, with a strict byte budget."""
    if size <= 0 or budget <= 0 or windows <= 0:
        return []
    if size <= budget:
        return [(start, size)]
    count = min(windows, budget)
    width = budget // count
    if count == 1:
        return [(start, width)]
    return [(start + (size - width) * i // (count - 1), width) for i in range(count)]


def section_observations(sections, entry=None):
    findings = []
    names = {s['name'].casefold().lstrip('.') for s in sections}
    for family, pair in (('UPX', {'upx0', 'upx1'}), ('MPRESS', {'mpress1', 'mpress2'}),
                         ('ASPack', {'aspack', 'adata'})):
        if pair <= names:
            matched = [s for s in sections if s['name'].casefold().lstrip('.') in pair]
            findings.append({'kind': family + ' section-name candidate', 'address': matched[0]['start'],
                             'summary': 'Matching section names: ' + ', '.join(s['name'] for s in matched),
                             'details': 'Section names can be copied or renamed. This alone does not confirm packing or select an unpacker.'})
    for section in sections:
        label, address = section['name'], section['start']
        if section.get('writable') and section.get('executable'):
            findings.append({'kind': 'Writable executable segment', 'address': address,
                             'summary': label + ' has both write and execute permissions.',
                             'details': 'This may support runtime-generated or modified code. JIT runtimes and ordinary linker layouts can also use these permissions.'})
        score = section.get('entropy')
        sampled = section.get('sample_bytes', 0)
        if score is not None and sampled >= 1024 and score >= 7.2:
            findings.append({'kind': 'High byte entropy', 'address': address,
                             'summary': '%s: %.3f bits/byte over %d initialized sample bytes.' % (label, score, sampled),
                             'details': 'Compression, encryption, multimedia and ordinary tables can all produce high entropy. Samples are spread across the segment; this is not a whole-segment measurement unless sample_bytes equals its size.'})
        if entry is not None and address <= entry < section['end']:
            if section.get('writable') or (score is not None and score >= 7.2 and sampled >= 1024):
                findings.append({'kind': 'Entry segment observation', 'address': entry,
                                 'summary': 'Image entry is in %s, which is writable or has high sampled entropy.' % label,
                                 'details': 'The entry address is reported by IDA. It is not an inferred original entry point.'})
    return findings


def function_observations(function, name, blocks, indirect_sites):
    """Flag dispatch-shaped graphs; do not call compiler switches obfuscation."""
    findings = []
    nodes = {block['start'] for block in blocks}
    incoming = Counter()
    successors = {}
    for block in blocks:
        targets = set(block['successors']) & nodes
        successors[block['start']] = targets
        incoming.update(targets)
    if len(blocks) >= 8:
        for address in nodes:
            if incoming[address] >= 4 and len(successors.get(address, ())) >= 3:
                findings.append({'kind': 'Dispatch-shaped control flow', 'address': address,
                                 'function': function, 'name': name,
                                 'summary': '%s: block has %d incoming and %d outgoing edges (%d blocks in function).' % (name, incoming[address], len(successors[address]), len(blocks)),
                                 'details': 'A dispatcher can occur in flattened control flow, interpreters, parsers and ordinary switches. This structural heuristic does not prove obfuscation or identify opaque predicates.'})
    if len(indirect_sites) >= 3:
        findings.append({'kind': 'Repeated indirect branches', 'address': indirect_sites[0],
                         'function': function, 'name': name,
                         'summary': '%s: %d indirect unconditional branches.' % (name, len(indirect_sites)),
                         'details': 'Computed jumps may implement dispatch or jump tables. Calls and returns are excluded; no target or devirtualization result is guessed.',
                         'sites': indirect_sites[:32]})
    return findings


def scan_database(max_functions=5000, max_function_bytes=65536, max_segments=256):
    import ida_bytes
    import ida_funcs
    import ida_gdl
    import ida_ida
    import ida_kernwin
    import ida_nalt
    import ida_segment
    import ida_ua
    import idautils
    import idc

    def cancelled():
        if ida_kernwin.user_cancelled():
            raise InterruptedError('Protection scan cancelled.')

    report = {'sections': [], 'findings': [], 'functions_scanned': 0, 'functions_skipped': 0,
              'truncated': False, 'errors': [], 'input_file': ida_nalt.get_input_file_path(),
              'limits': {'segment_sample_bytes': 65536, 'functions': max_functions,
                         'function_bytes': max_function_bytes, 'segments': max_segments}}
    entry = ida_ida.inf_get_start_ea()
    for number, address in enumerate(idautils.Segments()):
        cancelled()
        if number >= max_segments:
            report['truncated'] = True
            break
        segment = ida_segment.getseg(address)
        samples = bytearray()
        ranges = sample_ranges(segment.start_ea, segment.end_ea - segment.start_ea)
        for start, size in ranges:
            cancelled()
            blob = ida_bytes.get_bytes(start, size)
            if blob is not None:
                samples.extend(value for offset, value in enumerate(blob) if ida_bytes.is_loaded(start + offset))
        report['sections'].append({'name': ida_segment.get_segm_name(segment),
                                   'start': segment.start_ea, 'end': segment.end_ea,
                                   'size': segment.end_ea - segment.start_ea,
                                   'writable': bool(segment.perm & ida_segment.SEGPERM_WRITE),
                                   'executable': bool(segment.perm & ida_segment.SEGPERM_EXEC),
                                   'sample_bytes': len(samples), 'sample_ranges': ranges,
                                   'entropy': entropy(samples)})
    report['findings'].extend(section_observations(report['sections'], entry))
    for number, address in enumerate(idautils.Functions()):
        cancelled()
        if number >= max_functions:
            report['truncated'] = True
            break
        function = ida_funcs.get_func(address)
        if not function or function.flags & (ida_funcs.FUNC_THUNK | ida_funcs.FUNC_LIB) or function.end_ea - function.start_ea > max_function_bytes:
            report['functions_skipped'] += 1
            continue
        try:
            blocks = [{'start': b.start_ea, 'successors': [s.start_ea for s in b.succs()]}
                      for b in ida_gdl.FlowChart(function)]
            indirect = []
            for index, site in enumerate(idautils.FuncItems(address)):
                if index % 512 == 0:
                    cancelled()
                instruction = ida_ua.insn_t()
                if not ida_ua.decode_insn(instruction, site):
                    continue
                mnemonic = instruction.get_canon_mnem().lower()
                if mnemonic in ('jmp', 'br', 'bx') and instruction.ops[0].type in (
                        ida_ua.o_reg, ida_ua.o_phrase, ida_ua.o_displ, ida_ua.o_mem):
                    # ARM BX LR is a return, not a dispatcher observation.
                    if mnemonic != 'bx' or idc.print_operand(site, 0).lower() not in ('lr', 'r14'):
                        indirect.append(site)
            report['findings'].extend(function_observations(address, idc.get_func_name(address), blocks, indirect))
            report['functions_scanned'] += 1
        except InterruptedError:
            raise
        except Exception as exc:
            if len(report['errors']) < 20:
                report['errors'].append('%#x: %s' % (address, exc))
    return report


def file_sha256(path):
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with Path(path).open('rb') as source:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: source.read(1024 * 1024), b''):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()


def executable_format(path):
    """Validate basic ELF/PE header boundaries, without loading or executing."""
    path = Path(path)
    size = path.stat().st_size
    with path.open('rb') as stream:
        header = stream.read(64)
        if header[:4] == b'\x7fELF' and len(header) >= 52:
            bits, endian = header[4], header[5]
            if bits not in (1, 2) or endian not in (1, 2) or header[6] != 1:
                raise ValueError('Invalid ELF identification.')
            minimum = 52 if bits == 1 else 64
            if len(header) < minimum:
                raise ValueError('Truncated ELF header.')
            order = '<' if endian == 1 else '>'
            offset, length = (40, 2) if bits == 1 else (52, 2)
            if int.from_bytes(header[offset:offset + length], 'little' if endian == 1 else 'big') != minimum:
                raise ValueError('Invalid ELF header size.')
            phoff = struct.unpack_from(order + ('I' if bits == 1 else 'Q'), header, 28 if bits == 1 else 32)[0]
            phsize, phnum = struct.unpack_from(order + 'HH', header, 42 if bits == 1 else 54)
            if not phnum or phoff < minimum or phsize < (32 if bits == 1 else 56) or phoff + phsize * phnum > size:
                raise ValueError('Invalid ELF program header bounds.')
            return 'ELF%d' % (32 if bits == 1 else 64)
        if header[:2] == b'MZ' and len(header) >= 64:
            offset = struct.unpack_from('<I', header, 60)[0]
            if offset < 64 or offset + 24 > size:
                raise ValueError('Invalid PE header offset.')
            stream.seek(offset)
            coff = stream.read(24)
            if coff[:4] != b'PE\0\0':
                raise ValueError('Missing PE signature.')
            sections = struct.unpack_from('<H', coff, 6)[0]
            optional_size = struct.unpack_from('<H', coff, 20)[0]
            if not sections or optional_size < 2 or offset + 24 + optional_size + 40 * sections > size:
                raise ValueError('Invalid PE section header bounds.')
            magic = stream.read(2)
            if magic not in (b'\x0b\x01', b'\x0b\x02'):
                raise ValueError('Unsupported PE optional header.')
            return 'PE32+' if magic == b'\x0b\x02' else 'PE32'
    raise ValueError('Expected an ELF or PE executable header.')


def _run_upx(arguments, timeout, cancel, output=None, max_output=512 * 1024 * 1024):
    if cancel is not None and cancel.is_set():
        raise InterruptedError('UPX extraction cancelled.')
    env = os.environ.copy()
    env.pop('UPX', None)
    # Log to a temporary file to avoid unbounded pipe buffers from malformed input.
    with tempfile.TemporaryFile() as log:
        process = subprocess.Popen(arguments, stdout=log, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, env=env)
        start = time.monotonic()
        try:
            while process.poll() is None:
                if cancel is not None and cancel.is_set():
                    raise InterruptedError('UPX extraction cancelled.')
                if time.monotonic() - start > timeout:
                    raise TimeoutError('UPX exceeded the %ds stage time limit.' % timeout)
                if output is not None and output.exists() and output.stat().st_size > max_output:
                    raise ValueError('UPX output exceeded the size limit.')
                if os.fstat(log.fileno()).st_size > 1024 * 1024:
                    raise ValueError('UPX diagnostics exceeded the 1 MiB limit.')
                time.sleep(0.05)
        finally:
            if process.poll() is None:
                process.kill()
            process.wait()
        log.seek(0)
        message = log.read(16384).decode('utf-8', 'replace')
        if process.returncode:
            raise RuntimeError('UPX failed (%d): %s' % (process.returncode, message.strip()))
        return message


def unpack_upx(source, destination, timeout=60, cancel=None):
    """Integrity-test a snapshot, unpack it, then publish a new validated file.

    Never overwrites an existing path. The input executable is never launched.
    UPX handles standard supported formats; custom stubs may need manual work.
    """
    tool = shutil.which('upx')
    if tool is None:
        raise RuntimeError('UPX is not installed or is not on PATH.')
    source = Path(source).resolve(strict=True)
    destination = Path(destination).absolute()
    if destination.exists() or destination.is_symlink():
        raise FileExistsError('Choose a new output filename; existing files are preserved.')
    if not source.is_file() or source.stat().st_size > 256 * 1024 * 1024:
        raise ValueError('Input must be a regular file no larger than 256 MiB.')
    source_format = executable_format(source)
    original_hash = file_sha256(source)
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='.arsenal-upx-', dir=destination.parent) as directory:
        snapshot = Path(directory) / 'packed.bin'
        output = Path(directory) / 'unpacked.bin'
        shutil.copyfile(source, snapshot)
        if file_sha256(snapshot) != original_hash:
            raise RuntimeError('Input changed while taking the extraction snapshot.')
        test_log = _run_upx([tool, '--test', '--no-progress', str(snapshot)], timeout, cancel)
        unpack_log = _run_upx([tool, '-d', '--no-progress', '--no-mode', '--no-owner', '--no-time',
                               '-o', str(output), str(snapshot)], timeout, cancel, output)
        if cancel is not None and cancel.is_set():
            raise InterruptedError('UPX extraction cancelled.')
        if not output.is_file() or output.stat().st_size > 512 * 1024 * 1024:
            raise ValueError('UPX produced no output or exceeded the 512 MiB limit.')
        output_format = executable_format(output)
        if source_format != output_format:
            raise ValueError('UPX changed the executable format unexpectedly.')
        if file_sha256(source) != original_hash:
            raise RuntimeError('Original input changed during extraction; output was not published.')
        output_hash = file_sha256(output)
        if output_hash == original_hash:
            raise ValueError('UPX output is unchanged from the packed input.')
        output.chmod(0o600)
        # Same-directory staging permits atomic publication without overwriting.
        os.link(output, destination)
        return {'source': str(source), 'output': str(destination), 'source_sha256': original_hash,
                'output_sha256': output_hash, 'format': output_format,
                'output_bytes': output.stat().st_size, 'test_log': test_log, 'unpack_log': unpack_log,
                'validation': 'UPX integrity test and basic executable header bounds passed. The target was not executed.'}

```

`plugin/protection_ui.py`:

```py
"""Protection evidence and explicit extraction to a separate executable file."""
import html
import json
from pathlib import Path
import threading

from PyQt5 import QtCore, QtWidgets
import ida_kernwin
import ida_nalt

from protection_scan import scan_database, unpack_upx
from ui_style import polish_table


class UnpackWorker(QtCore.QThread):
    completed = QtCore.pyqtSignal(dict)
    failed = QtCore.pyqtSignal(str)

    def __init__(self, source, destination, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.source, self.destination = source, destination
        self.cancel = threading.Event()

    def run(self):
        try:
            self.completed.emit(unpack_upx(self.source, self.destination, cancel=self.cancel))
        except Exception as exc:
            self.failed.emit(str(exc))


class ProtectionPanel(QtWidgets.QWidget):
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.report = None
        self.worker = None
        self.last_unpack = None
        layout = QtWidgets.QVBoxLayout(self)
        intro = QtWidgets.QLabel('Inspect packing clues, segment entropy and dispatch-shaped control flow. '
                                'Each finding includes its evidence and uncertainty.')
        intro.setWordWrap(True)
        layout.addWidget(intro)
        row = QtWidgets.QHBoxLayout()
        scan = QtWidgets.QPushButton('Scan protections')
        scan.clicked.connect(self.scan)
        row.addWidget(scan)
        export = QtWidgets.QPushButton('Export findings')
        export.clicked.connect(self.export)
        row.addWidget(export)
        self.unpack_button = QtWidgets.QPushButton('Unpack UPX to new file…')
        self.unpack_button.clicked.connect(self.unpack)
        row.addWidget(self.unpack_button)
        self.cancel_button = QtWidgets.QPushButton('Cancel extraction')
        self.cancel_button.setEnabled(False)
        self.cancel_button.clicked.connect(self.cancel_unpack)
        row.addWidget(self.cancel_button)
        layout.addLayout(row)
        self.status = QtWidgets.QLabel('Ready. UPX extraction preserves the input and creates a separate artifact.')
        self.status.setWordWrap(True)
        self.status.setTextInteractionFlags(QtCore.Qt.TextSelectableByMouse)
        layout.addWidget(self.status)
        split = QtWidgets.QSplitter(QtCore.Qt.Vertical)
        self.table = QtWidgets.QTableWidget(0, 3)
        polish_table(self.table)
        self.table.setHorizontalHeaderLabels(['Observation', 'Address', 'Evidence'])
        self.table.setEditTriggers(QtWidgets.QAbstractItemView.NoEditTriggers)
        self.table.setSelectionBehavior(QtWidgets.QAbstractItemView.SelectRows)
        self.table.setSelectionMode(QtWidgets.QAbstractItemView.SingleSelection)
        self.table.horizontalHeader().setStretchLastSection(True)
        self.table.setColumnWidth(0, 230)
        self.table.setColumnWidth(1, 110)
        self.table.itemSelectionChanged.connect(self.details_for_selection)
        self.table.cellDoubleClicked.connect(self.jump_row)
        split.addWidget(self.table)
        self.details = QtWidgets.QTextBrowser()
        self.details.setOpenLinks(False)
        self.details.setOpenExternalLinks(False)
        self.details.anchorClicked.connect(self.jump)
        split.addWidget(self.details)
        split.setSizes([250, 200])
        layout.addWidget(split, 1)
        self.sections = QtWidgets.QPlainTextEdit()
        self.sections.setReadOnly(True)
        self.sections.setMaximumHeight(140)
        self.sections.setPlaceholderText('Segment sample coverage and entropy appear here after scanning.')
        layout.addWidget(self.sections)

    def scan(self):
        ida_kernwin.show_wait_box('Scanning protection evidence…')
        try:
            report = scan_database()
            signals = QtCore.QSignalBlocker(self.table)
            self.table.clearSelection()
            self.table.setRowCount(0)
            self.report = report
            self.table.setRowCount(len(report['findings']))
            for row, finding in enumerate(report['findings']):
                for column, value in enumerate((finding['kind'], hex(finding['address']), finding['summary'])):
                    item = QtWidgets.QTableWidgetItem(value)
                    item.setData(QtCore.Qt.UserRole, row)
                    item.setToolTip(value)
                    self.table.setItem(row, column, item)
            del signals
            self.sections.setPlainText('\n'.join(
                '%s  %#x  %s  %d/%d bytes sampled  %s' % (
                    s['name'], s['start'],
                    'entropy %.3f' % s['entropy'] if s['entropy'] is not None else 'no initialized sample',
                    s['sample_bytes'], s['size'], ('W' if s['writable'] else '-') + ('X' if s['executable'] else '-'))
                for s in report['sections']))
            self.status.setText('%d observations · %d functions scanned · %d skipped%s%s' % (
                len(report['findings']), report['functions_scanned'], report['functions_skipped'],
                ' · scan budget reached' if report['truncated'] else '',
                ' · %d analysis errors (export for details)' % len(report['errors']) if report['errors'] else ''))
            if report['findings']:
                self.table.selectRow(0)
            else:
                self.details.setPlainText('No supported protection clues found. This does not establish that the binary is unpacked or unobfuscated.')
        except Exception as exc:
            self.status.setText('Protection scan stopped: %s. Previous results retained.' % exc)
        finally:
            ida_kernwin.hide_wait_box()

    def selected_finding(self):
        items = self.table.selectedItems()
        if not items or not self.report:
            return None
        index = items[0].data(QtCore.Qt.UserRole)
        findings = self.report['findings']
        if isinstance(index, int) and 0 <= index < len(findings):
            return findings[index]
        return None

    def details_for_selection(self):
        finding = self.selected_finding()
        if finding:
            text = '<h3>%s</h3><p>%s</p><p>%s</p>' % tuple(html.escape(finding[k]) for k in ('kind', 'summary', 'details'))
            sites = finding.get('sites', [finding['address']])
            text += '<p>' + ' · '.join('<a href="ida:%x">%#x</a>' % (site, site) for site in sites) + '</p>'
            self.details.setHtml(text)

    def jump_row(self, *args):
        finding = self.selected_finding()
        if finding:
            ida_kernwin.jumpto(finding['address'])

    def jump(self, url):
        if url.scheme() == 'ida':
            try:
                ida_kernwin.jumpto(int(url.path(), 16))
            except ValueError:
                pass

    def export(self):
        if self.report is None and self.last_unpack is None:
            self.status.setText('Scan protections or unpack a file first.')
            return
        path, _ = QtWidgets.QFileDialog.getSaveFileName(self, 'Export protection evidence', 'protection-report.json', 'JSON (*.json)')
        if path:
            try:
                Path(path).write_text(json.dumps({'scan': self.report, 'unpack': self.last_unpack}, indent=2))
                self.status.setText('Exported ' + path)
            except OSError as exc:
                self.status.setText('Export failed: ' + str(exc))

    def unpack(self):
        if self.worker is not None:
            return
        source = ida_nalt.get_input_file_path()
        if not source or not Path(source).is_file():
            self.status.setText('The database input file is unavailable. Restore its path before unpacking.')
            return
        path, _ = QtWidgets.QFileDialog.getSaveFileName(self, 'Create a new unpacked artifact', source + '.unpacked', 'All files (*)')
        if not path:
            return
        if Path(path).exists() or Path(path).is_symlink():
            self.status.setText('Choose a new output filename; existing files are preserved.')
            return
        self.worker = UnpackWorker(source, path, self)
        self.worker.completed.connect(self.unpack_complete)
        self.worker.failed.connect(lambda error: self.status.setText('Extraction stopped: ' + error))
        self.worker.finished.connect(self.worker_finished)
        self.unpack_button.setEnabled(False)
        self.cancel_button.setEnabled(True)
        self.status.setText('Checking the packed snapshot, then extracting with UPX…')
        self.worker.start()

    def unpack_complete(self, result):
        self.last_unpack = result
        self.status.setText('Created %s (%d bytes). Open this separate file in IDA to analyze the unpacked code.' % (result['output'], result['output_bytes']))
        self.details.setPlainText(result['validation'] + '\n\nSource SHA-256: ' + result['source_sha256'] +
                                  '\nOutput SHA-256: ' + result['output_sha256'])

    def worker_finished(self):
        worker, self.worker = self.worker, None
        if worker is not None:
            worker.deleteLater()
        self.unpack_button.setEnabled(True)
        self.cancel_button.setEnabled(False)

    def cancel_unpack(self):
        if self.worker is not None:
            self.worker.cancel.set()
            self.status.setText('Cancelling extraction…')

    def shutdown(self):
        if self.worker is not None:
            self.worker.cancel.set()
            self.worker.wait()
```

`plugin/smt_ui.py`:

```py
"""Asynchronous Z3 workbench for SMT-LIB and exported angr path constraints."""
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import tempfile

from PyQt5 import QtCore, QtWidgets

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]


class SMTPanel(QtWidgets.QWidget):
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.process = None
        self.job = None
        self.snapshot_provider = None
        self.cancelled = False
        layout = QtWidgets.QVBoxLayout(self)
        label = QtWidgets.QLabel('Solve bit-vector and arithmetic constraints directly. Load an angr snapshot '
                                'to inspect one path; a partial path does not include the success goal.')
        label.setWordWrap(True)
        layout.addWidget(label)
        row = QtWidgets.QHBoxLayout()
        for title, callback in [('Load SMT-LIB…', self.load), ('Save SMT-LIB…', self.save),
                                ('Load latest angr snapshot', self.load_snapshot)]:
            button = QtWidgets.QPushButton(title)
            button.clicked.connect(callback)
            row.addWidget(button)
        layout.addLayout(row)
        self.editor = QtWidgets.QPlainTextEdit()
        self.editor.setAccessibleName('SMT-LIB constraints')
        self.editor.setPlaceholderText('Enter SMT-LIB declarations and assertions…')
        self.editor.setPlainText('; Example: recover an 8-bit input after XOR and modular addition\n'
                                 '(declare-const input (_ BitVec 8))\n'
                                 '(assert (= (bvadd (bvxor input #x5a) #x03) #x1e))\n')
        layout.addWidget(self.editor, 2)
        self.predicate_enabled = QtWidgets.QCheckBox('Check whether a condition is constant under these assumptions')
        layout.addWidget(self.predicate_enabled)
        self.predicate = QtWidgets.QLineEdit()
        self.predicate.setAccessibleName('Boolean SMT-LIB predicate')
        self.predicate.setPlaceholderText('Boolean expression, e.g. (= (bvand input #x01) #x00)')
        self.predicate.setToolTip('Checks the assumptions and both branches. Unsatisfiable assumptions mean unreachable; an unknown query remains inconclusive.')
        self.predicate.setEnabled(False)
        self.predicate_enabled.toggled.connect(self.predicate.setEnabled)
        layout.addWidget(self.predicate)
        row = QtWidgets.QHBoxLayout()
        self.timeout = QtWidgets.QSpinBox()
        self.timeout.setRange(1, 86400)
        self.timeout.setValue(60)
        row.addWidget(QtWidgets.QLabel('Time limit (seconds)'))
        row.addWidget(self.timeout)
        self.models = QtWidgets.QSpinBox()
        self.models.setRange(1, 16)
        row.addWidget(QtWidgets.QLabel('Maximum models'))
        row.addWidget(self.models)
        self.run_button = QtWidgets.QPushButton('Solve with Z3')
        self.run_button.clicked.connect(self.run)
        self.predicate_enabled.toggled.connect(lambda enabled: self.run_button.setText(
            'Check predicate' if enabled else 'Solve with Z3'))
        self.predicate_enabled.toggled.connect(lambda enabled: self.models.setEnabled(not enabled))
        row.addWidget(self.run_button)
        self.stop_button = QtWidgets.QPushButton('Cancel')
        self.stop_button.clicked.connect(self.cancel)
        self.stop_button.setEnabled(False)
        row.addWidget(self.stop_button)
        layout.addLayout(row)
        self.status = QtWidgets.QLabel('Ready')
        self.status.setWordWrap(True)
        self.status.setTextInteractionFlags(QtCore.Qt.TextSelectableByMouse)
        layout.addWidget(self.status)
        self.output = QtWidgets.QPlainTextEdit()
        self.output.setReadOnly(True)
        self.output.setAccessibleName('Z3 result and model values')
        layout.addWidget(self.output, 1)
        self.deadline = QtCore.QTimer(self)
        self.deadline.setSingleShot(True)
        self.deadline.timeout.connect(self.cancel)

    def read_script(self, path):
        path = Path(path)
        if path.stat().st_size > 4 * 1024 * 1024:
            raise ValueError('SMT-LIB file exceeds 4 MiB')
        self.editor.setPlainText(path.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
        self.status.setText('Loaded ' + str(path))

    def load(self):
        path, _ = QtWidgets.QFileDialog.getOpenFileName(self, 'Load constraints', str(ROOT), 'SMT-LIB (*.smt2 *.smt);;All files (*)')
        if path:
            try:
                self.read_script(path)
            except Exception as exc:
                self.status.setText(str(exc))

    def save(self):
        path, _ = QtWidgets.QFileDialog.getSaveFileName(self, 'Save constraints', str(ROOT / 'constraints.smt2'), 'SMT-LIB (*.smt2)')
        if path:
            try:
                Path(path).write_text(self.editor.toPlainText(), encoding='utf-8')
                self.status.setText('Saved ' + path)
            except Exception as exc:
                self.status.setText(str(exc))

    def load_snapshot(self):
        try:
            path = self.snapshot_provider() if self.snapshot_provider else None
            if path is None or not Path(path).is_file():
                raise ValueError('Enable Save SMT snapshots in the angr panel and run a solve first.')
            self.read_script(path)
        except Exception as exc:
            self.status.setText(str(exc))

    def run(self):
        if self.process is not None:
            return
        try:
            script = self.editor.toPlainText()
            if not script.strip() or len(script.encode('utf-8')) > 4 * 1024 * 1024:
                raise ValueError('Provide SMT-LIB up to 4 MiB')
            jobs = ROOT / 'jobs'
            jobs.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
            self.job = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix='z3-', dir=str(jobs)))
            req = {'engine': 'z3', 'smtlib': script, 'timeout': self.timeout.value(),
                   'max_models': self.models.value()}
            if self.predicate_enabled.isChecked():
                expression = self.predicate.text().strip()
                if not expression:
                    raise ValueError('Enter a Boolean SMT-LIB predicate')
                req['predicate'] = expression
            (self.job / 'request.json').write_text(json.dumps(req), encoding='utf-8')
            self.output.clear()
            self.cancelled = False
            self.process = QtCore.QProcess(self)
            self.process.finished.connect(self.finished)
            self.process.errorOccurred.connect(self.process_error)
            self.run_button.setEnabled(False)
            self.stop_button.setEnabled(True)
            self.status.setText('Solving · ' + str(self.job))
            self.deadline.start((req['timeout'] + 10) * 1000)
            self.process.start(os.environ.get('ARSENAL_PYTHON', str(ROOT / 'venv/bin/python')), [str(ROOT / 'backend/solve.py'),
                               str(self.job / 'request.json'), str(self.job / 'result.json')])
        except Exception as exc:
            self.status.setText(str(exc))

    def process_error(self, error):
        if error == QtCore.QProcess.FailedToStart and self.process is not None:
            self.status.setText('Could not start solver: ' + self.process.errorString())
            self.finished(-1, QtCore.QProcess.CrashExit)

    def finished(self, code, exit_status):
        self.deadline.stop()
        process, self.process = self.process, None
        if process is not None:
            process.deleteLater()
        self.run_button.setEnabled(True)
        self.stop_button.setEnabled(False)
        if self.job is None:
            return
        try:
            path = self.job / 'result.json'
            if not path.is_file():
                raise ValueError('Cancelled' if self.cancelled else 'Solver exited without a result (code %d)' % code)
            result = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
            self.output.setPlainText(json.dumps(result, indent=2))
            self.status.setText(('Cancelled' if self.cancelled else result.get('status', 'unknown')) + ' · ' + str(self.job))
        except Exception as exc:
            self.status.setText(str(exc))

    def cancel(self):
        if self.process is not None:
            self.cancelled = True
            self.process.terminate()
            self.status.setText('Cancelling…')

    def shutdown(self):
        self.deadline.stop()
        if self.process is not None:
            process = self.process
            process.terminate()
            if not process.waitForFinished(2000):
                process.kill()
                process.waitForFinished(1000)
```
