`plugin/symbolic_inputs.py`:

```py
"""Editable, named symbolic buffers shared by the solver and input discovery."""
import re

from PyQt5 import QtCore, QtWidgets


def escaped_text(text):
    """Allow the five documented ASCII escapes without altering UTF-8 text."""
    values = {"n": "\n", "r": "\r", "t": "\t", "0": "\0", "\\": "\\"}
    return re.sub(r"\\([nrt0\\])", lambda match: values[match.group(1)], text)


def display_text(text):
    for old, new in (("\\", "\\\\"), ("\n", "\\n"), ("\r", "\\r"), ("\t", "\\t"), ("\0", "\\0")):
        text = text.replace(old, new)
    return text


class SymbolicInputs(QtWidgets.QWidget):
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.constraints = {}
        layout = QtWidgets.QVBoxLayout(self)
        layout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        self.note = QtWidgets.QLabel()
        self.note.setWordWrap(True)
        layout.addWidget(self.note)
        self.table = QtWidgets.QTableWidget(0, 6)
        self.table.setHorizontalHeaderLabels(["Name", "Bytes", "Prefix", "Suffix (\\n)", "Printable", "Call arg #"])
        self.table.setAlternatingRowColors(True)
        self.table.verticalHeader().hide()
        self.table.setSelectionBehavior(QtWidgets.QAbstractItemView.SelectRows)
        self.table.setMinimumHeight(115)
        self.table.setMaximumHeight(220)
        self.table.horizontalHeader().setSectionResizeMode(QtWidgets.QHeaderView.Stretch)
        layout.addWidget(self.table)
        actions = QtWidgets.QHBoxLayout()
        add = QtWidgets.QPushButton("+ Add input")
        add.clicked.connect(lambda: self.add_input())
        remove = QtWidgets.QPushButton("Remove selected")
        remove.clicked.connect(self.remove_selected)
        constraints = QtWidgets.QPushButton("Byte constraints…")
        constraints.clicked.connect(self.edit_constraints)
        actions.addWidget(add)
        actions.addWidget(remove)
        actions.addWidget(constraints)
        actions.addStretch()
        layout.addLayout(actions)
        self.mode = "stdin"
        self.set_mode("stdin")

    def set_mode(self, mode):
        self.mode = mode
        self.table.setColumnHidden(5, mode != "call")
        notes = {
            "stdin": "Inputs form one stdin stream in row order. Set exact byte counts and suffixes (e.g. \\n) to match the program's reads.",
            "argv": "Each row is one command-line argument, in row order. NUL bytes are excluded.",
            "call": "Each row is a separate NUL-terminated buffer. Call arg # is the zero-based pointer argument position. Use advanced call_args for integer arguments.",
        }
        self.note.setText(notes[mode])

    def add_input(self, spec=None):
        spec = spec or {}
        row = self.table.rowCount()
        existing = {self.table.item(i, 0).text() for i in range(row)}
        number = 1
        while "input%d" % number in existing:
            number += 1
        name = spec.get("name", "input%d" % number)
        self.table.insertRow(row)
        values = (name, str(spec.get("length", 16)), display_text(spec.get("prefix", "")),
                  display_text(spec.get("suffix", "")), "",
                  str(spec["argument_index"] if spec.get("argument_index") is not None else row))
        for col, value in enumerate(values):
            item = QtWidgets.QTableWidgetItem(value)
            if col == 4:
                item.setFlags(QtCore.Qt.ItemIsEnabled | QtCore.Qt.ItemIsUserCheckable | QtCore.Qt.ItemIsSelectable)
                item.setCheckState(QtCore.Qt.Checked if spec.get("printable", True) else QtCore.Qt.Unchecked)
            self.table.setItem(row, col, item)
        if "byte_constraints" in spec:
            self.constraints[row] = spec["byte_constraints"]

    def remove_selected(self):
        # Keep per-row constraints attached when preceding rows are removed.
        rows = sorted({item.row() for item in self.table.selectedItems()}, reverse=True)
        for row in rows:
            self.table.removeRow(row)
            self.constraints = {index - (index > row): value for index, value in self.constraints.items() if index != row}

    def set_inputs(self, specs):
        self.table.setRowCount(0)
        self.constraints = {}
        for spec in specs:
            self.add_input(spec)

    def edit_constraints(self):
        row = self.table.currentRow()
        if row < 0:
            QtWidgets.QMessageBox.information(self, "Byte constraints", "Select an input row first.")
            return
        import json
        value, accepted = QtWidgets.QInputDialog.getMultiLineText(
            self, "Byte constraints for " + self.table.item(row, 0).text(),
            'JSON list; e.g. [{"offset":0,"value":65},{"offset":1,"min":48,"max":57}]',
            json.dumps(self.constraints.get(row, []), indent=2))
        if not accepted:
            return
        try:
            data = json.loads(value)
            length = int(self.table.item(row, 1).text())
            if not isinstance(data, list):
                raise ValueError("Enter a JSON list of byte constraints.")
            for item in data:
                if not isinstance(item, dict) or set(item) - {"offset", "value", "min", "max"}:
                    raise ValueError("Allowed keys: offset, value, min, max.")
                if type(item.get("offset")) is not int or not 0 <= item["offset"] < length:
                    raise ValueError("Each offset must fall within this input.")
                if not any(key in item for key in ("value", "min", "max")):
                    raise ValueError("Each entry needs value, min, or max.")
                if any(type(item[key]) is not int or not 0 <= item[key] <= 255
                       for key in ("value", "min", "max") if key in item):
                    raise ValueError("Constraint values must be integers from 0 to 255.")
            self.constraints[row] = data
            self.table.item(row, 0).setToolTip(json.dumps(data))
        except (ValueError, TypeError) as exc:
            QtWidgets.QMessageBox.warning(self, "Invalid byte constraints", str(exc))

    def specifications(self, allow_argument_gaps=False):
        if not 1 <= self.table.rowCount() <= 32:
            raise ValueError("Configure between 1 and 32 symbolic inputs.")
        inputs, positions, names = [], [], set()
        for row in range(self.table.rowCount()):
            name = self.table.item(row, 0).text().strip()
            if not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]{0,63}", name) or name in names:
                raise ValueError("Each input needs a unique identifier (input1, input2, …).")
            names.add(name)
            try:
                length = int(self.table.item(row, 1).text())
            except ValueError:
                raise ValueError("Input byte counts must be whole numbers.")
            if not 1 <= length <= 65536:
                raise ValueError("Each input must contain 1–65536 bytes.")
            spec = {"name": name, "length": length,
                    "prefix": escaped_text(self.table.item(row, 2).text()),
                    "suffix": escaped_text(self.table.item(row, 3).text()),
                    "printable": self.table.item(row, 4).checkState() == QtCore.Qt.Checked}
            if any(len(spec[field].encode("utf-8")) > length for field in ("prefix", "suffix")):
                raise ValueError("Prefix/suffix exceeds the length of " + name)
            if spec["printable"] and any(not 0x20 <= byte <= 0x7e
                    for field in ("prefix", "suffix") for byte in spec[field].encode("utf-8")):
                raise ValueError(name + ": prefix/suffix includes nonprintable bytes. Uncheck Printable for this row.")
            if row in self.constraints:
                spec["byte_constraints"] = self.constraints[row]
            inputs.append(spec)
            if self.mode == "call":
                try:
                    positions.append(int(self.table.item(row, 5).text()))
                except ValueError:
                    raise ValueError("Call argument positions must be whole numbers starting at zero.")
        if sum(item["length"] for item in inputs) > 65536:
            raise ValueError("Combined symbolic inputs exceed 65536 bytes.")
        if self.mode == "call" and (len(set(positions)) != len(positions) or any(not 0 <= p < 64 for p in positions)):
            raise ValueError("Call argument positions must be unique numbers between 0 and 63.")
        if self.mode == "call" and not allow_argument_gaps and sorted(positions) != list(range(len(positions))):
            raise ValueError("Call arg # must cover 0..N−1 once each. For mixed integer/buffer arguments, set advanced call_args and keep default table positions.")
        args = [{"kind": "buffer", "input": inputs[index]["name"]}
                for index in sorted(range(len(positions)), key=lambda index: positions[index])]
        return inputs, args

    def preset(self):
        inputs, _ = self.specifications(allow_argument_gaps=True)
        for row, item in enumerate(inputs):
            item["argument_index"] = int(self.table.item(row, 5).text()) if self.mode == "call" else row
        return inputs

```

`plugin/triage.py`:

```py
"""Local evidence-based navigation, export and string-decoding helpers."""
import base64
import html
import json
from pathlib import Path
import time

from PyQt5 import QtCore, QtWidgets
import ida_bytes
import ida_funcs
import ida_gdl
import ida_idaapi
import ida_kernwin
import ida_lines
import ida_nalt
import ida_name
import idautils
import idc

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
EXPORTS = ROOT / "exports"
from triage_scan import collect_triage
from triage_rules import CATEGORY_ORDER


def function_context(ea):
    fn = ida_funcs.get_func(ea)
    if not fn:
        raise ValueError("Select an address inside a function.")
    start = fn.start_ea
    result = {
        "binary": ida_nalt.get_input_file_path(),
        "sha256": bytes(ida_nalt.retrieve_input_file_sha256()).hex(),
        "image_base": ida_nalt.get_imagebase(), "address": start,
        "rva": start - ida_nalt.get_imagebase(), "name": ida_funcs.get_func_name(start),
        "callers": [{"address": x.frm, "name": ida_funcs.get_func_name(x.frm)}
                    for x in idautils.XrefsTo(start) if x.iscode],
        "blocks": [{"start": b.start_ea, "end": b.end_ea,
                    "successors": [s.start_ea for s in b.succs()]}
                   for b in ida_gdl.FlowChart(fn)],
        "disassembly": [], "strings": [],
    }
    seen = set()
    for index, head in enumerate(idautils.FuncItems(start)):
        if index >= 10000:
            result["truncated"] = "First 10000 function items only"
            break
        result["disassembly"].append({"address": head, "text":
                                      ida_lines.tag_remove(idc.generate_disasm_line(head, 0) or "")})
        for target in idautils.DataRefsFrom(head):
            raw = ida_bytes.get_strlit_contents(target, -1, ida_nalt.STRTYPE_C)
            if raw and target not in seen:
                seen.add(target)
                result["strings"].append({"address": target,
                                           "text": raw[:1024].decode("utf-8", errors="replace")})
    try:
        import ida_hexrays
        if ida_hexrays.init_hexrays_plugin():
            cfunc = ida_hexrays.decompile(start)
            if cfunc:
                result["pseudocode"] = str(cfunc)
    except Exception as exc:
        result["decompiler_note"] = str(exc)
    return result


def export_bridges(context, directory):
    """Generate attach-only templates. Never start the input binary."""
    module = Path(context["binary"]).name
    rva = context["rva"]
    if rva < 0:
        raise ValueError("Selected function precedes the image base.")
    frida = """// Generated locally by IDA Arsenal. Load only into an authorized process.
// RVA is relative to the image base; module must already be loaded.
const moduleName = %s;
const module = Process.getModuleByName(moduleName);
const target = module.base.add(%s);
console.log('Hooking', moduleName, target);
Interceptor.attach(target, {
  onEnter(args) {
    // Raw first four ABI argument slots: not inferred function types.
    console.log('enter', target, args[0], args[1], args[2], args[3]);
    console.log(Thread.backtrace(this.context, Backtracer.ACCURATE)
      .map(DebugSymbol.fromAddress).join('\\n'));
  },
  onLeave(retval) { console.log('return', retval); }
});
""" % (json.dumps(module), hex(rva))
    (directory / "hook.frida.js").write_text(frida)
    gdb = """# Source after attaching to your process in GDB/pwndbg.
# Set $arsenal_base to the runtime load address matching IDA's image base.
# Example: set $arsenal_base = 0x555555554000
# Then: arsenal-break
define arsenal-break
  if $arsenal_base == 0
    echo Set $arsenal_base to the runtime module base first.\\n
  else
    break *($arsenal_base + %s)
    x/12i ($arsenal_base + %s)
  end
end
set $arsenal_base = 0
""" % (hex(rva), hex(rva))
    (directory / "breakpoint.gdb").write_text(gdb)
    # Interoperable symbols permit importing recovered names in other disassemblers.
    (directory / "symbols.json").write_text(json.dumps({
        "binary_sha256": context["sha256"], "image_base": context["image_base"],
        "symbols": [{"rva": ea - context["image_base"], "name": name}
                    for ea, name in idautils.Names() if ea >= context["image_base"]]
    }, indent=2))


def xor_candidates(data):
    suggestions = []
    if not data:
        return suggestions
    for key in range(256):
        decoded = bytes(c ^ key for c in data)
        ratio = sum(32 <= c < 127 or c in (9, 10, 13) for c in decoded) / len(decoded)
        if ratio >= 0.85:
            suggestions.append((ratio, key, decoded))
    suggestions.sort(key=lambda x: (-x[0], x[1]))
    return suggestions[:12]


class TriagePanel(QtWidgets.QWidget):
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.report = None
        self.last_export = None
        self.records = {}

            "Scanning also maps and names input destinations. Manage naming and Undo in Input flow."
            " Native algorithm and material findings appear in Crypto."

        layout.addWidget(label)

                                ("Export current function dossier + bridges", self.export_function)):

        self.search.textChanged.connect(self.filter_results)

    def scan(self):
        ida_kernwin.show_wait_box("Scanning references for Arsenal…")
        try:
            new_report = collect_triage()
            self.report = new_report
            self.records = {r["address"]: r for r in
                            new_report["ranked_functions"] + new_report["context_functions"]}
            self.filter_results()
            if getattr(self, "input_flow_panel", None) is not None:
                self.input_flow_panel.scan_database(new_report)
            if getattr(self, 'outcome_callback', None) is not None:
                self.outcome_callback()
            crypto = getattr(self, "crypto_panel", None)
            if crypto is not None and crypto.automatic.isChecked():
                crypto_report = crypto.scan_database()
                if crypto_report is not None:
                    new_report["native_crypto"] = crypto_report
                    self.status.setText(self.status.text() + " · %d native crypto findings (Crypto tab)" %
                                        len(crypto_report.get("findings", [])))
        except Exception as exc:
            self.status.setText("Scan failed: " + str(exc))
        finally:
            ida_kernwin.hide_wait_box()

    def filter_results(self, *args):
        if self.report is None:
            return
        category = self.category_filter.currentText()
        rows = list(self.report["ranked_functions"])
        if self.show_context.isChecked() or category == "Context clues":
            rows += self.report["context_functions"]
        term = self.search.text().casefold().strip()
        rows = [r for r in rows if (category == "All behaviors" or category in r["categories"])
                and (not term or term in (r["name"] + " " + r["title"] + " " + r["summary"] +
                                         " " + " ".join(r["evidence"]) + " " +
                                         " ".join(c["text"] for c in r["context"])).casefold())]
        self.table.setSortingEnabled(False)
        self.table.setRowCount(0)
        self.table.setRowCount(len(rows))
        for row, record in enumerate(rows):
            for col, text in enumerate((record["categories"][0], record["name"],
                                        hex(record["address"]), record["summary"])):
                item = QtWidgets.QTableWidgetItem(text)
                item.setData(QtCore.Qt.UserRole, record["address"])
                item.setToolTip(record["title"] if col == 0 else text)
                self.table.setItem(row, col, item)
        for col, width in enumerate((220, 240, 110, 440)):
            self.table.setColumnWidth(col, width)
        self.table.setSortingEnabled(True)
        notes = len(self.report["analysis_notes"])
        self.status.setText("%d shown · %d classified functions · %d string-only clues%s" %
                            (len(rows), len(self.report["ranked_functions"]),
                             len(self.report["context_functions"]),
                             " · %d limited local analyses (see function details)" % notes if notes else ""))
        if rows:
            self.table.selectRow(0)
        else:
            self.details.setPlainText("No matching functions. Unresolved indirect calls and inlined code may be missed.")

    def show_details(self):
        selected = self.table.selectedItems()
        if not selected:
            return
        record = self.records.get(selected[0].data(QtCore.Qt.UserRole))
        if not record:
            return
        escape = html.escape
        def link(ea):
            return '<a href="ida:%x">%#x</a>' % (ea, ea)
        dark = self.details.palette().base().color().lightness() < 128
        parts = [
            "<style>a { color: %s; }</style>" % ("#80c4ff" if dark else "#095aba"),
            "<h2>%s</h2>" % escape(record["categories"][0]),
            "<p><b>%s</b> · %s</p>" % (escape(record["name"]), link(record["address"])),
        ]
        if len(record["categories"]) > 1:
            parts.append("<p>Also: %s</p>" % escape(" · ".join(record["categories"][1:])))
        for category in record["categories"]:
            if category != record["categories"][0]:
                parts.append("<h3>%s</h3>" % escape(category))
            findings = [f for f in record["findings"] if f["category"] == category]
            if not findings:
                parts.append("<p>Interesting text is referenced here. No supported API-call behavior was established.</p>")
            for finding in findings:
                parts.append("<p><b>%s</b><br>%s: <b>%s</b> at %s" %
                             (escape(finding["summary"]), escape(finding["basis"]),
                              escape(finding["api"]), link(finding["site"])))
                if "source" in finding:
                    parts.append("<br>Input operation: <b>%s</b> at %s" %
                                 (escape(finding["source"]["api"]), link(finding["source"]["site"])))
                parts.append("<br><i>%s</i></p>" % escape(finding["limitation"]))
        if record["context"]:
            parts.append("<h3>Referenced text — context only</h3>")
            for clue in record["context"][:30]:
                parts.append("<p>%s · %s<br>%s</p>" %
                             (link(clue["site"]), link(clue["address"]), escape(clue["text"])))
            if len(record["context"]) > 30:
                parts.append("<p>More referenced text is included in the JSON export.</p>")
        if record["analysis_note"]:
            parts.append("<h3>Analysis limit</h3><p>%s</p>" % escape(record["analysis_note"]))
        self.details.setHtml("".join(parts))

    def evidence_jump(self, url):
        if url.scheme() == "ida":
            try:
                ea = int(url.path(), 16)
                if ida_bytes.is_mapped(ea):
                    ida_kernwin.jumpto(ea)
            except ValueError:
                pass

    def jump(self, row, column):
        item = self.table.item(row, column)
        if item:
            ida_kernwin.jumpto(item.data(QtCore.Qt.UserRole))

    def recover_go(self):
        try:
            from go_recovery import GoRecoverPlugin
            GoRecoverPlugin().run(0)
        except Exception as exc:
            self.status.setText("Go recovery failed: " + str(exc))

    def new_export(self):
        import tempfile
        EXPORTS.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
        directory = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix=time.strftime("%Y%m%d-%H%M%S-"), dir=str(EXPORTS)))
        directory.chmod(0o700)
        self.last_export = directory
        return directory

    def export_report(self):
        if self.report is None:
            self.scan()
        if self.report is not None:
            try:
                path = self.new_export() / "triage.json"
                path.write_text(json.dumps(self.report, indent=2))
                self.status.setText("Saved locally: " + str(path))
            except Exception as exc:
                self.status.setText(str(exc))

    def export_function(self):
        try:
            context = function_context(ida_kernwin.get_screen_ea())
            directory = self.new_export()
            (directory / "function.json").write_text(json.dumps(context, indent=2))
            export_bridges(context, directory)
            (directory / "README.md").write_text(
                "# Function dossier\n\n"
                "Local evidence for later review. No network requests were made.\n"
                "Review names as hypotheses; validate behavior against code and runtime evidence.\n"
                "function.json contains CFG, xrefs, strings, disassembly and available pseudocode.\n"
                "hook.frida.js logs entry/return and stack after you attach to a process.\n"
                "breakpoint.gdb defines arsenal-break; set $arsenal_base after sourcing.\n"
                "symbols.json uses RVAs and an input hash for cross-disassembler import.\n"
                "Frida module must already be loaded; ARM Thumb targets may require bit 0 set.\n")
            self.status.setText("Dossier and bridges saved locally: " + str(directory))
        except Exception as exc:
            self.status.setText("Export failed: " + str(exc))

    def decode(self):
        try:
            selected, start, end = ida_kernwin.read_range_selection(ida_kernwin.get_current_viewer())
            if not selected:
                start = ida_kernwin.get_screen_ea()
                end = start + self.length.value()
            size = min(end - start, 65536)
            data = ida_bytes.get_bytes(start, size)
            if not data:
                raise ValueError("No readable bytes at cursor.")
            lines = ["Address %#x; %d bytes. Ranked by printable ratio; inspect before using." %
                     (start, len(data)), "Original hex: " + data[:512].hex()]
            for score, key, decoded in xor_candidates(data):
                lines.append("XOR %#04x (%.0f%% printable): %r" % (key, score * 100, decoded[:512]))
            try:
                lines.append("Base64: %r" % base64.b64decode(data.strip(), validate=True)[:512])
            except ValueError:
                pass
            for encoding in ("utf-16-le", "utf-16-be"):
                try:
                    lines.append("%s: %r" % (encoding, data.decode(encoding)[:512]))
                except UnicodeError:
                    pass
            self.preview.setPlainText("\n\n".join(lines))
        except Exception as exc:
            self.preview.setPlainText(str(exc))
```

`plugin/triage_rules.py`:

```py
"""Auditable API rules. A matching name alone never establishes a call."""
import re

CATEGORY_ORDER = (
    "Input checking", "Debugger checks", "Network", "Input taking",
    "Data comparison", "Data parsing", "Cryptography", "Process execution", "Process tracing",
    "Process information",
    "Dynamic loading", "Memory management", "File access", "Context clues",
)

# Explicit spellings prevent e.g. fread/pthread or connect/connectivity matches.
# Names are case sensitive: APIs and C symbols are case sensitive too.
RULES = {}


def add(names, category, summary, limitation="", buffer_arg=None):
    for name in names.split():
        RULES.setdefault(name, []).append({
            "category": category, "summary": summary, "limitation": limitation,
            "buffer_arg": buffer_arg,
        })


add("read pread recv recvfrom", "Input taking", "Reads bytes into a caller-provided buffer.",
    "A read call does not prove user interaction or successful input.", 1)
add("fread fgets gets ReadConsoleA ReadConsoleW", "Input taking",
    "Reads input into a caller-provided buffer.",
    "The stream and read result must be inspected to establish the source.", 0)
# Win32 ReadConsole uses buffer at index 1, unlike the C stream APIs above.
for _name in ("ReadConsoleA", "ReadConsoleW"):
    RULES[_name][0]["buffer_arg"] = 1
add("ReadFile", "Input taking", "Requests bytes from a file or device handle.",
    "The handle may represent a file, pipe, console or device.", 1)
add("scanf fscanf vscanf vfscanf __isoc99_scanf __isoc99_fscanf __isoc23_scanf __isoc23_fscanf",
    "Input taking", "Parses formatted values from a stream.",
    "The stream's source and parsed values still need inspection.")
add("sscanf vsscanf __isoc99_sscanf", "Data parsing", "Parses formatted values from an existing string.",
    "The string may be internal data; parsing does not establish external input.")
add("getchar getc fgetc getwchar getenv GetEnvironmentVariableA GetEnvironmentVariableW",
    "Input taking", "Retrieves a character or environment value.",
    "The value's later use is not established by this call.")
add("strcmp strncmp memcmp bcmp strcasecmp strncasecmp wcscmp wcsncmp lstrcmpA lstrcmpW",
    "Data comparison", "Compares byte sequences or strings.",
    "Comparisons also serve sorting, dispatch and internal bookkeeping; input validation is not implied.")
add("socket connect accept accept4 bind listen send sendto recv recvfrom shutdown getaddrinfo "
    "WSAStartup WSASocketA WSASocketW WSAConnect WSASend WSARecv "
    "InternetOpenA InternetOpenW InternetConnectA InternetConnectW "
    "InternetReadFile InternetWriteFile HttpSendRequestA HttpSendRequestW "
    "WinHttpOpen WinHttpConnect WinHttpSendRequest WinHttpReceiveResponse WinHttpReadData "
    "curl_easy_init curl_easy_setopt curl_easy_perform SSL_connect SSL_accept SSL_read SSL_write",
    "Network", "Invokes a socket, HTTP or TLS API.",
    "This identifies an API call, not a successful connection or a remote endpoint.")
add("IsDebuggerPresent CheckRemoteDebuggerPresent", "Debugger checks",
    "Queries whether a process is being debugged.",
    "The call's result and response still need inspection; anti-debugging intent is not implied.")
add("ptrace", "Process tracing", "Invokes the process-tracing interface.",
    "ptrace is also used by legitimate debuggers; the request determines its role.")
add("NtQueryInformationProcess ZwQueryInformationProcess", "Process information",
    "Queries process information.",
    "Only specific information classes query debugger state.")
add("system popen execve execv execvp execl execlp posix_spawn posix_spawnp "
    "CreateProcessA CreateProcessW WinExec ShellExecuteA ShellExecuteW",
    "Process execution", "Requests command or process execution.",
    "The command, arguments and success are not established.")
add("dlopen dlsym dlmopen LoadLibraryA LoadLibraryW LoadLibraryExA LoadLibraryExW GetProcAddress",
    "Dynamic loading", "Loads a module or resolves a symbol.",
    "Dynamic loading is common legitimate behavior and does not imply evasion.")
add("mmap mprotect VirtualAlloc VirtualAllocEx VirtualProtect VirtualProtectEx "
    "ReadProcessMemory WriteProcessMemory",
    "Memory management", "Maps, protects, reads or writes process memory.",
    "Permissions, process ownership and purpose require argument inspection.")
add("fopen fopen64 open open64 openat fclose unlink unlinkat rename "
    "CreateFileA CreateFileW WriteFile DeleteFileA DeleteFileW",
    "File access", "Invokes a file or path API.",
    "The referenced path and the operation's success are not established.")
add("RC4 RC4_set_key AES_encrypt AES_decrypt AES_set_encrypt_key AES_set_decrypt_key "
    "SHA1 SHA256 SHA512 SHA256_Init SHA256_Update SHA256_Final "
    "MD5 MD5_Init MD5_Update MD5_Final HMAC "
    "EVP_EncryptInit_ex EVP_EncryptUpdate EVP_EncryptFinal_ex "
    "EVP_DecryptInit_ex EVP_DecryptUpdate EVP_DecryptFinal_ex "
    "EVP_DigestInit_ex EVP_DigestUpdate EVP_DigestFinal_ex "
    "BCryptEncrypt BCryptDecrypt BCryptHashData BCryptGenRandom "
    "CryptEncrypt CryptDecrypt CryptHashData CryptGenRandom",
    "Cryptography", "Invokes a recognized cryptographic API.",
    "Algorithm, key material and security properties require inspection.")


def normalize_api(name):
    """Strip import/ABI decorations, never arbitrary leading/trailing letters."""
    value = name
    for prefix in ("__imp_", "_imp__", "j_"):
        if value.startswith(prefix):
            value = value[len(prefix):]
            break
    value = value.lstrip(".")
    value = re.sub(r"@{1,2}(?:GLIBC|GLIBCXX)_[0-9.]+$", "", value)
    value = re.sub(r"@\d+$", "", value)
    if value not in RULES and value.startswith("_") and value[1:] in RULES:
        value = value[1:]
    return value


def rules_for(api, arguments=None):
    result = [dict(rule) for rule in RULES.get(api, [])]
    arguments = arguments or {}
    if api == "ptrace" and arguments.get(0) == 0:
        result = [{
            "category": "Debugger checks",
            "summary": "Requests PTRACE_TRACEME, a common debugger-detection mechanism.",
            "limitation": "Also used by programs cooperating with a tracer; intent is not proven.",
            "buffer_arg": None,
        }]
    elif api in ("NtQueryInformationProcess", "ZwQueryInformationProcess") and arguments.get(1) == 7:
        result = [{
            "category": "Debugger checks",
            "summary": "Queries a debugger-related process information class (%d)." % arguments[1],
            "limitation": "The returned value and program response still need inspection.",
            "buffer_arg": None,
        }]
    return result


def category_sort(category):
    return CATEGORY_ORDER.index(category) if category in CATEGORY_ORDER else len(CATEGORY_ORDER)

```

`plugin/triage_scan.py`:

```py
"""Read-only IDA call evidence and deliberately conservative local input flow."""
from collections import defaultdict
import re

import ida_bytes
import ida_funcs
import ida_gdl
import ida_idaapi
import ida_idp
import ida_kernwin
import ida_lines
import ida_nalt
import ida_ua
import ida_xref
import idautils
import idc

from triage_rules import RULES, normalize_api, rules_for, category_sort


def check_cancel():
    if ida_kernwin.user_cancelled():
        raise InterruptedError("Scan cancelled; previous results retained.")


def imported_targets():
    imports, targets = [], {}
    for index in range(ida_nalt.get_import_module_qty()):
        check_cancel()
        def visit(ea, name, ordinal):
            if name:
                imports.append({"address": ea, "name": name})
                canonical = normalize_api(name)
                if canonical in RULES:
                    targets[ea] = canonical
            return True
        ida_nalt.enum_import_names(index, visit)
    # Resolve only real import thunks, not user functions with familiar names.
    thunks = []
    for index, ea in enumerate(idautils.Functions()):
        if index % 256 == 0:
            check_cancel()
        fn = ida_funcs.get_func(ea)
        if fn and fn.flags & ida_funcs.FUNC_THUNK:
            target = ida_funcs.calc_thunk_func_target(fn)
            if isinstance(target, tuple):
                target = target[0]
            thunks.append((ea, target))
    for _ in range(8):
        additions = {ea: targets[target] for ea, target in thunks
                     if ea not in targets and target in targets}
        if not additions:
            break
        targets.update(additions)
    return imports, targets


def call_sites(targets):
    callers = defaultdict(dict)
    for target, api in targets.items():
        check_cancel()
        for ref in idautils.XrefsTo(target):
            fn = ida_funcs.get_func(ref.frm)
            if not fn or fn.flags & (ida_funcs.FUNC_THUNK | ida_funcs.FUNC_LIB):
                continue
            insn = ida_ua.insn_t()
            if not ida_ua.decode_insn(insn, ref.frm):
                continue
            call = ida_idp.is_call_insn(insn)
            tail = (ref.type in (ida_xref.fl_JN, ida_xref.fl_JF)
                    and idc.print_insn_mnem(ref.frm).lower() in ("jmp", "b", "br", "bx"))
            if not call and not tail:
                continue
            callers[fn.start_ea][(ref.frm, api)] = {
                "site": ref.frm, "target": target, "api": api,
                "instruction": ida_lines.tag_remove(idc.generate_disasm_line(ref.frm, 0) or ""),
                "kind": "tail call" if tail else "call",
            }
    return callers


def local_details(fn_ea, known_calls, targets):
    """Recover literal arguments and same-basic-block read→comparison evidence.

    No propagation through aliases, branches, user wrappers or unknown calls.
    Unknown stores/calls invalidate tracked input. Missing Hex-Rays is explicit.
    """
    import ida_hexrays as hx
    if not hx.init_hexrays_plugin():
        return {}, [], "Hex-Rays unavailable; argument and input-flow checks were skipped."
    fn = ida_funcs.get_func(fn_ea)
    if fn.end_ea - fn.start_ea > 32768 or len(known_calls) > 128:
        return {}, [], "Function exceeds local-flow budget; API calls are still listed."
    cfunc = hx.decompile(fn_ea)
    if not cfunc:
        return {}, [], "Decompilation unavailable; API calls are still listed."
    variables = cfunc.get_lvars()
    blocks = [(b.start_ea, b.end_ea) for b in ida_gdl.FlowChart(fn)]
    def block(ea):
        return next((start for start, end in blocks if start <= ea < end), None)

    def unwrap(expr):
        while expr.op == hx.cot_cast:
            expr = expr.x
        return expr

    def buffer(expr):
        expr = unwrap(expr)
        if expr.op == hx.cot_ref:
            expr = unwrap(expr.x)
        if expr.op == hx.cot_var:
            var = variables[expr.v.idx]
            if var.is_stk_var() and var.type().is_array():
                return expr.v.idx
        return None

    constants, events, ambiguous = {}, [], False
    class Visitor(hx.ctree_parentee_t):
        def visit_insn(self, insn):
            nonlocal ambiguous
            if insn.op == hx.cit_asm:
                ambiguous = True
            return 0

        def visit_expr(self, expr):
            nonlocal ambiguous
            if expr.op == hx.cot_call:
                callee = unwrap(expr.x)
                api = targets.get(callee.obj_ea) if callee.op == hx.cot_obj else None
                key = (expr.ea, api)
                if key not in known_calls:
                    api = None
                values = {}
                roots = []
                for index, arg in enumerate(expr.a):
                    value = unwrap(arg)
                    if value.op == hx.cot_num:
                        values[index] = int(value.numval())
                    roots.append(buffer(arg))
                constants[key] = values
                # Only a direct result or simple predicate is evidence of use.
                # In particular, return (strcmp(...), 1) discards the result.
                used = False
                allowed = {hx.cot_cast, hx.cot_lnot, hx.cot_eq, hx.cot_ne,
                           hx.cot_sge, hx.cot_uge, hx.cot_sle, hx.cot_ule,
                           hx.cot_sgt, hx.cot_ugt, hx.cot_slt, hx.cot_ult}
                for parent in reversed(list(self.parents)):
                    if parent.op >= hx.cit_empty:
                        used = parent.op in (hx.cit_return, hx.cit_if, hx.cit_switch)
                        break
                    if parent.op not in allowed:
                        break
                events.append((expr.ea, "call", api, roots, used))
                if expr.ea == ida_idaapi.BADADDR:
                    ambiguous = True
            elif hx.cot_asg <= expr.op <= hx.cot_asgumod or expr.op in (
                    hx.cot_preinc, hx.cot_predec, hx.cot_postinc, hx.cot_postdec):
                # Every store invalidates tracking, including possible aliases.
                events.append((expr.ea, "write", None, [], False))
                if expr.ea == ida_idaapi.BADADDR:
                    ambiguous = True
            return 0
    Visitor().apply_to(cfunc.body, None)
    if ambiguous:
        return constants, [], "Input-flow proof skipped because assembly or operations without addresses are present."
    sources, flows = {}, []
    previous_block = None
    # Writes at the same EA as a call are conservatively applied after it.
    for ea, kind, api, roots, used in sorted(events, key=lambda event: (event[0], event[1] == "write")):
        current_block = block(ea)
        if current_block is None or current_block != previous_block:
            sources.clear()
        previous_block = current_block
        if kind == "write":
            sources.clear()
            continue
        rules = rules_for(api)
        comparison = any(r["category"] == "Data comparison" for r in rules)
        if comparison:
            values = constants.get((ea, api), {})
            empty_comparison = (
                api in {"memcmp", "bcmp", "strncmp", "strncasecmp", "wcsncmp"}
                and values.get(2) == 0
            )
            same_buffer = len(roots) >= 2 and roots[0] is not None and roots[0] == roots[1]
            if used and not empty_comparison and not same_buffer:
                for root in roots[:2]:
                    if root is not None and root in sources and sources[root]["site"] < ea:
                        flows.append({"site": ea, "source": sources[root],
                                      "api": api, "buffer": variables[root].name})
            # Even a known comparator is a modeling boundary for later calls.
            sources.clear()
            continue
        sources.clear()
        values = constants.get((ea, api), {})
        count_indices = {
            "read": (2,), "pread": (2,), "recv": (2,), "recvfrom": (2,),
            "fread": (1, 2), "fgets": (1,), "ReadFile": (2,),
            "ReadConsoleA": (2,), "ReadConsoleW": (2,),
        }.get(api, ())
        if any(values.get(index) == 0 for index in count_indices):
            continue
        for rule in rules:
            index = rule["buffer_arg"]
            if rule["category"] == "Input taking" and index is not None and index < len(roots):
                root = roots[index]
                if root is not None and current_block is not None:
                    sources[root] = {"site": ea, "api": api}
    return constants, flows, ""


def collect_triage():
    imports, targets = imported_targets()
    calls = call_sites(targets)
    records = {}
    analysis_notes = []
    for index, (ea, known_calls) in enumerate(calls.items()):
        check_cancel()
        constants, flows, note = {}, [], ""
        apis = {call["api"] for call in known_calls.values()}
        needs_details = ("ptrace" in apis or bool(apis & {"NtQueryInformationProcess", "ZwQueryInformationProcess"})
                         or (any(r["category"] == "Data comparison" for api in apis for r in rules_for(api))
                             and any(r["buffer_arg"] is not None for api in apis for r in rules_for(api))))
        if needs_details:
            try:
                constants, flows, note = local_details(ea, known_calls, targets)
            except Exception as exc:
                note = "Local argument/input-flow analysis unavailable: %s" % exc
            if note:
                analysis_notes.append({"address": ea, "message": note})
        findings = []
        for key, call in sorted(known_calls.items()):
            for rule in rules_for(call["api"], constants.get(key)):
                findings.append({
                    "category": rule["category"], "summary": rule["summary"],
                    "basis": "Observed API call", "site": call["site"], "api": call["api"],
                    "target": call["target"], "kind": call["kind"],
                    "limitation": rule["limitation"],
                })
        for flow in flows:
            findings.append({
                "category": "Input checking", "basis": "Local buffer flow",
                "summary": "%s compares local buffer %s, previously passed to %s in this block; the result feeds a return or condition."
                           % (flow["api"], flow["buffer"], flow["source"]["api"]),
                "site": flow["site"], "api": flow["api"], "source": flow["source"],
                "limitation": "Same-block local-buffer flow only. Read success, validation strength and reachable execution are not proven.",
            })
        categories = sorted({f["category"] for f in findings}, key=category_sort)
        fn = ida_funcs.get_func(ea)
        records[ea] = {
            "address": ea, "name": ida_funcs.get_func_name(ea), "size": fn.end_ea - fn.start_ea,
            "categories": categories, "title": " · ".join(categories), "findings": findings,
            "summary": next(f["summary"] for f in findings if f["category"] == categories[0]),
            "score": len({call["site"] for call in known_calls.values()}),
            "evidence": ["%s at %#x" % (c["api"], c["site"]) for c in known_calls.values()],
            "context": [], "analysis_note": note,
        }
    strings, context_only = [], {}
    pattern = re.compile(r"https?://|password|incorrect|success|flag\{|/proc/|tracerpid|debugger|frida|il2cpp|unity|godot|unreal", re.I)
    for index, entry in enumerate(idautils.Strings()):
        if index % 128 == 0:
            check_cancel()
        text = str(entry)
        if not pattern.search(text):
            continue
        strings.append({"address": entry.ea, "text": text[:500]})
        for ref in idautils.XrefsTo(entry.ea):
            fn = ida_funcs.get_func(ref.frm)
            if not fn or fn.flags & (ida_funcs.FUNC_LIB | ida_funcs.FUNC_THUNK):
                continue
            context = {"address": entry.ea, "site": ref.frm, "text": text[:500]}
            if fn.start_ea in records:
                records[fn.start_ea]["context"].append(context)
            else:
                row = context_only.setdefault(fn.start_ea, {
                    "address": fn.start_ea, "name": ida_funcs.get_func_name(fn.start_ea),
                    "size": fn.end_ea - fn.start_ea, "categories": ["Context clues"],
                    "title": "Context clues", "summary": "References interesting text; behavior is not established.",
                    "findings": [], "context": [], "score": 0, "evidence": [], "analysis_note": "",
                })
                row["context"].append(context)
    names = list(idautils.Names())
    markers = {}
    for label, pattern in {
        "Go": r"runtime[._]main|go:build|\.gopclntab",
        "Rust": r"_RN[vCs]|rust_eh_personality|rust_begin_unwind|core::panicking",
        "Unity / IL2CPP": r"il2cpp_|UnityEngine", "Godot": r"godot_|GDExtension",
        "Unreal": r"UObject|FName::|ProcessEvent|StaticClass",
        "JNI / Android native": r"JNI_OnLoad|Java_[A-Za-z]", "C++ RTTI": r"_ZTI|_ZTV|\?\?_R",
    }.items():
        hits = [name for _, name in names if re.search(pattern, name)]
        if hits:
            markers[label] = hits[:10]
    ranked = sorted(records.values(), key=lambda r: (category_sort(r["categories"][0]), -r["score"], r["address"]))
    return {
        "schema_version": 2, "binary": ida_nalt.get_input_file_path(),
        "image_base": ida_nalt.get_imagebase(), "sha256": bytes(ida_nalt.retrieve_input_file_sha256()).hex(),
        "runtime_name_markers": markers, "ranked_functions": ranked,
        "context_functions": sorted(context_only.values(), key=lambda r: r["address"]),
        "interesting_strings": strings, "imports": imports, "analysis_notes": analysis_notes,
        "note": "Exact imported-API calls and bounded local buffer flow. No behavioral labels from strings alone.",
        "limitations": [
            "No interprocedural taint or general alias analysis; input-check recognition is deliberately narrow.",
            "Unresolved indirect calls, inlined/static library code and raw syscalls can be missed.",
            "API calls establish an operation request, not successful execution or malicious intent.",
            "Existing IDA names/types/xrefs and Hex-Rays analysis affect results.",
        ],
    }
```

```py
"""Palette-aware styling scoped to the Arsenal dock, never IDA's application."""
from PyQt5 import QtGui, QtWidgets


def apply_workbench_theme(widget):
    widget.setObjectName("arsenalWorkbench")
    # IDA applies its theme through stylesheets; the form itself may retain
    # Qt's light default palette until a text view has been polished.
    views = widget.findChildren(QtWidgets.QTextBrowser)
    for view in views:
        view.ensurePolished()
    palette = views[0].palette() if views else widget.palette()
    dark = palette.color(QtGui.QPalette.Base).lightness() < 128
    edge = "#4a515d" if dark else "#cdd5df"
    subtle = "#343b45" if dark else "#f0f4f8"
    accent = "#74b6f4" if dark else "#2468ad"
    base = "#292d34" if dark else "#ffffff"
    ink = "#e3e9f1" if dark else "#203044"
    selected = "#284d70" if dark else "#d5e8fa"
    widget.setStyleSheet("""
        QWidget#arsenalWorkbench { color: %s; }
        #arsenalTitle { font-size: 20px; font-weight: 700; }
        #arsenalSubtitle { color: %s; font-size: 12px; }
        #shortcutHint { color: %s; background: %s; border: 1px solid %s; border-radius: 10px; padding: 4px 9px; }
        QTabWidget::pane { border: 1px solid %s; border-radius: 6px; top: -1px; }
        QTabBar::tab { padding: 9px 13px; margin-right: 2px; border: 1px solid transparent; border-top-left-radius: 5px; border-top-right-radius: 5px; }
        QTabBar::tab:hover { background: %s; }
        QTabBar::tab:selected { color: %s; border-color: %s; border-bottom-color: %s; background: %s; font-weight: 600; }
        QPushButton { padding: 7px 12px; border-radius: 5px; border: 1px solid %s; }
        QPushButton:hover { background: %s; }
        QPushButton:pressed { background: %s; }
        QPushButton:focus, QToolButton:focus, QCheckBox:focus { border: 2px solid %s; }
        QPushButton#primaryAction { background-color: %s; color: %s; font-weight: 600; border: 1px solid %s; }
        QPushButton#primaryAction:hover { background-color: %s; }
        QPushButton#primaryAction:disabled { background-color: %s; color: %s; }
        QLineEdit, QSpinBox, QComboBox { min-height: 26px; padding: 3px 6px; border: 1px solid %s; border-radius: 4px; }
        QLineEdit:focus, QSpinBox:focus, QComboBox:focus { border: 2px solid %s; }
        QTableWidget, QTreeWidget, QTextBrowser, QPlainTextEdit, QListWidget {
            border: 1px solid %s; border-radius: 5px;
        }
        QTableWidget, QTreeWidget { background-color: %s; alternate-background-color: %s; color: %s; selection-background-color: %s; selection-color: %s; }
        QHeaderView::section { background-color: %s; color: %s; padding: 7px 6px; border: 0; border-bottom: 1px solid %s; font-weight: 600; }
        QTableView::item { padding: 5px; }
        QGroupBox { margin-top: 11px; border: 1px solid %s; border-radius: 6px; padding: 12px 8px 8px; }
        QGroupBox::title { subcontrol-origin: margin; left: 10px; padding: 0 5px; font-weight: 600; }
        QToolButton { padding: 5px 8px; }
        QSplitter::handle { background-color: %s; }
    """ % (ink, accent, ink, subtle, edge, edge, subtle, ink, edge, edge, base,
           edge, subtle, subtle, accent, accent, "#152333" if dark else "white",
           accent, accent, subtle, ink, edge, accent, edge, base, subtle, ink,
           selected, ink, subtle, ink, edge, edge, subtle))
    # IDA's outer dock may shrink forms aggressively. Preserve native field
    # heights and let the enclosing scroll area handle a smaller dock.
    for kind in (QtWidgets.QLineEdit, QtWidgets.QSpinBox, QtWidgets.QComboBox):
        for field in widget.findChildren(kind):
            field.ensurePolished()
            field.setMinimumHeight(field.minimumSizeHint().height())


def polish_table(table):
    table.setAlternatingRowColors(True)
    table.setShowGrid(False)
    table.verticalHeader().hide()
    table.verticalHeader().setDefaultSectionSize(32)
    table.setWordWrap(False)
```

```py
"""Harmless native fixtures are the only binaries executed by this test suite."""
import json
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import sys

import pytest

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
FIX = ROOT / 'tests' / 'fixtures'


@pytest.fixture(scope='session', autouse=True)
def compile_fixtures():
    for name, flags in [('check-nopie', ['-no-pie']), ('check-pie', ['-fPIE', '-pie'])]:
        subprocess.run(['cc', '-O0', '-g', *flags, str(FIX/'check.c'), '-o', str(FIX/name)], check=True)
    subprocess.run(['cc', '-O0', '-g', '-fPIE', '-pie', str(FIX/'long_argv_compare.c'),
                    '-o', str(FIX/'long-argv-compare')], check=True)
    if (FIX/'fauxware.c').is_file():  # optional: fetched by tests/fixtures/fetch_fauxware.sh
        subprocess.run(['cc', '-O0', '-g', '-fPIE', '-pie', str(FIX/'fauxware.c'), '-o', str(FIX/'fauxware')], check=True)


def symbols(binary):
    output = subprocess.check_output(['nm', '-n', str(binary)], text=True)
    return {parts[2]: int(parts[0], 16) for line in output.splitlines() if len(parts := line.split()) == 3}


def run(tmp_path, req):
    request, result = tmp_path/'request.json', tmp_path/'result.json'
    request.write_text(json.dumps(req))
    subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(ROOT/'backend/solve.py'), str(request), str(result)], check=True, timeout=100)
    return json.loads(result.read_text())


@pytest.mark.parametrize('name,mode,base', [('check-nopie','stdin',0x400000), ('check-pie','stdin',0), ('check-pie','argv',0x140000000)])
def test_entry_solve(tmp_path, name, mode, base):
    binary = FIX/name
    syms = symbols(binary)
    delta = base if name.endswith('pie') and name != 'check-nopie' else base - 0x400000
    req = dict(binary=str(binary), mode=mode, length=6, base_addr=base, find=[syms['accepted']+delta], avoid=[syms['rejected']+delta], timeout=45)
    result = run(tmp_path, req)
    assert result['status'] == 'found', result
    data = bytes.fromhex(result['input_hex'])
    assert data == b'IDASH!'
    proc = subprocess.run([str(binary), *([data.decode()] if mode == 'argv' else [])], input=data if mode == 'stdin' else None, capture_output=True, timeout=3)
    assert proc.returncode == 0 and b'ACCEPTED' in proc.stdout
    assert result['found_addr'] == syms['accepted']+delta
    assert len(result['trace']) > 3


def test_call_return(tmp_path):
    binary = FIX/'check-pie'
    req = dict(binary=str(binary), mode='call', length=6, base_addr=0, start_addr=symbols(binary)['check'], return_value=1, prefix='IDA', prototype='int check(const char *p)', timeout=30)
    result = run(tmp_path, req)
    assert result['status'] == 'found', result
    assert bytes.fromhex(result['input_hex']) == b'IDASH!'
    assert subprocess.run([str(binary), result['input_text']], capture_output=True).returncode == 0


def test_long_argv_strcmp_strncmp_and_fixed_args(tmp_path):
    binary = FIX/'long-argv-compare'
    expected = ('a_symbolic_argv_string_longer_than_angrs_default_symbolic_scan_window_'
                'is_compared_exactly')
    req = dict(binary=str(binary), mode='argv', length=len(expected), printable=True,
               argv_before=['--token'], argv_after=['--tail'],
               stdout_find='LONG COMPARE ACCEPTED', stdout_avoid='LONG COMPARE REJECTED',
               timeout=30)
    result = run(tmp_path, req)
    assert result['status'] == 'found', result
    assert bytes.fromhex(result['input_hex']).decode() == expected
    proc = subprocess.run([str(binary), '--token', result['input_text'], '--tail'],
                          capture_output=True, timeout=3)
    assert proc.returncode == 0 and b'LONG COMPARE ACCEPTED' in proc.stdout


def test_constraints_unsat(tmp_path):
    binary = FIX/'check-nopie'
    req = dict(binary=str(binary), mode='stdin', length=6, find=[symbols(binary)['accepted']], prefix='XXXXXX', timeout=30)
    result = run(tmp_path, req)
    assert result['status'] == 'not_found', result


def test_invalid_target(tmp_path):
    result = run(tmp_path, dict(binary=str(FIX/'check-pie'), length=6, find=[123], base_addr=0x400000))
    assert result['status'] == 'error' and 'outside' in result['error']


def test_limit(tmp_path):
    binary = FIX/'check-pie'
    result = run(tmp_path, dict(binary=str(binary), length=6, stdout_find='ACCEPTED', max_steps=1))
    assert result['status'] == 'limit', result


def test_fauxware_upstream(tmp_path):
    # angr's teaching crackme: password SOSNEAKY avoids all file access.
    binary = FIX/'fauxware'
    if not binary.is_file():
        pytest.skip('fauxware.c not fetched; run tests/fixtures/fetch_fauxware.sh')
    req = dict(binary=str(binary), mode='stdin', length=18, printable=False, prefix='arsenal!\n', suffix='\n', stdout_find='Welcome to the admin console', stdout_avoid='Go away!', timeout=60)
    result = run(tmp_path, req)
    assert result['status'] == 'found', result
    data = bytes.fromhex(result['input_hex'])
    assert data[9:17] == b'SOSNEAKY', result
    proc = subprocess.run([str(binary)], input=data, cwd=tmp_path, capture_output=True, timeout=3)
    assert b'Welcome to the admin console' in proc.stdout


def test_symbolic_stdout_rejected(tmp_path):
    binary = tmp_path / 'echo'
    subprocess.run(['cc', '-O0', str(FIX/'echo.c'), '-o', str(binary)], check=True)
    result = run(tmp_path, dict(binary=str(binary), mode='stdin', length=1,
                               stdout_find='A', timeout=15))
    assert result['status'] == 'error', result
    assert 'symbolic output' in result['error'], result


def test_target_inside_block(tmp_path):
    binary = FIX/'check-nopie'
    # x86-64 GCC fixture prologue: stop at the mov following push rbp.
    addr = symbols(binary)['accepted'] + 1
    result = run(tmp_path, dict(binary=str(binary), length=6, find=[addr]))
    assert result['status'] == 'found' and result['found_addr'] == addr, result


def test_nonpie_rebase_rejected(tmp_path):
    binary = FIX/'check-nopie'
    target = symbols(binary)['accepted'] + 0x100000
    result = run(tmp_path, dict(binary=str(binary), length=6, base_addr=0x500000, find=[target]))
    assert result['status'] == 'error' and 'cannot rebase' in result['error'], result


def test_watchdog_kills_stalled_worker(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    import importlib.util
    import os
    import time
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('arsenal_solve', ROOT/'backend/solve.py')
    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    spec.loader.exec_module(module)
    request, result = tmp_path/'request.json', tmp_path/'result.json'
    request.write_text(json.dumps({'timeout': 1}))
    monkeypatch.setattr(sys, 'argv', ['solve.py', str(request), str(result)])
    actual_popen = subprocess.Popen
    children = []
    def stalled_worker(_command, **kwargs):
        child = actual_popen([sys.executable, '-c', 'import time; time.sleep(30)'], **kwargs)
        children.append(child)
        return child
    monkeypatch.setattr(module.subprocess, 'Popen', stalled_worker)
    started = time.monotonic()
    assert module.main() == 0
    assert json.loads(result.read_text())['status'] == 'timeout'
    assert time.monotonic() - started < 10
    assert children[0].poll() is not None


@pytest.mark.parametrize('hard_kill', [False, True])
def test_cancel_reaps_worker(tmp_path, hard_kill):
    import psutil
    import time
    # Create a deliberately endless harmless symbolic fixture.
    src = tmp_path/'loop.c'
    src.write_text('int main(void) { volatile unsigned n=0; for (;;) n++; }')
    binary = tmp_path/'loop'
    subprocess.run(['cc', '-O0', str(src), '-o', str(binary)], check=True)
    request, result = tmp_path/'request.json', tmp_path/'result.json'
    request.write_text(json.dumps(dict(binary=str(binary), stdout_find='never', timeout=60, max_steps=1000000)))
    supervisor = subprocess.Popen([sys.executable, str(ROOT/'backend/solve.py'), str(request), str(result)])
    child = None
    try:
        deadline = time.monotonic() + 5
        while time.monotonic() < deadline:
            children = psutil.Process(supervisor.pid).children()
            if children:
                child = children[0]
                break
            time.sleep(0.05)
        assert child is not None
        # Let worker initialize its parent-death signal and enter symbolic execution.
        time.sleep(1.5)
        if hard_kill:
            supervisor.kill()
        else:
            supervisor.terminate()
        supervisor.wait(timeout=4)
        deadline = time.monotonic() + 3
        while time.monotonic() < deadline and child.is_running() and child.status() != psutil.STATUS_ZOMBIE:
            time.sleep(0.05)
        assert not child.is_running() or child.status() == psutil.STATUS_ZOMBIE
        if not hard_kill:
            assert json.loads(result.read_text())['error'] == 'Cancelled by caller'
    finally:
        if supervisor.poll() is None:
            supervisor.kill()
            supervisor.wait()
        if child and child.is_running():
            child.kill()

```

`tests/crypto_fixtures/README.md`:

```md
# Independent RC4 generalization fixtures

These sources are self-contained and independent of the scanner. They do not
read any external target, invoke the scanner, or copy production recognition logic.

Run `python3 tests/crypto_fixtures/build.py` from the Arsenal directory to rebuild
18 artifacts. GCC O0/O2 and Clang O1/O3 exercise fixed/PIE addressing, compiler
loop transformations, and nine stripped executables. Each artifact has a fresh,
deterministically generated key and ciphertext. Key lengths range from 5 to 29
bytes; data lengths range from 23 to 96 bytes. The plaintext is random bytes,
so successful recovery cannot depend on a flag prefix or readable text.

The positive sources cover:

- Combined KSA/PRGA with global key and ciphertext passed at a callsite.
- Separate KSA and PRGA, with a common state array passed by a wrapper.
- Combined implementation with constant-initialized stack key and ciphertext.
- Combined implementation with a randomized, constant XOR mask decoding a global
  encoded key into a local array. The decoded keys have independent lengths of
  7 and 13 bytes. The manifest distinguishes raw bytes from the decoded key.
- Separate KSA/PRGA with an intervening 256-byte `memcpy` state clone, exercising
  interprocedural state relationships and compiler-generated copy loops.

Two additional near-miss artifacts retain RC4-shaped variants with XOR in the
KSA accumulator, or a PRGA index step of two. Neither is standard RC4. A third
function initializes a constant key, passes it to an unknown writer in another
translation unit, then runs standard RC4. Recovering the stale pre-call key is
incorrect; its material must remain unresolved unless the writer is analyzed.
The manifest records these expectations separately. Their native `main` writes
three results, all checked independently by the builder.

All sources also retain four independently callable negative functions: identity
table initialization, a seeded Fisher-Yates shuffle, repeating XOR, and a
modulo-256 lookup loop with no RC4 swap/PRGA relationship. The `used`/`noinline`
attributes retain these routines even though `main` does not call them.

`manifest.json` stores test truth: function roles, pre-strip symbol addresses,
material bytes, sizes and native output expectations. PIE addresses are image
offsets. Production code must never consult this manifest or fixture symbols.
The native executable writes the RC4 transformation of its embedded ciphertext;
the builder checks its output against a separate Python implementation before
recording the artifact as verified. Only these self-made fixtures are executed.

`tests/test_crypto_reference.py` checks the Python implementation against the
first 16 bytes of the 40-bit-key vector in [RFC 6229 section 2](https://www.rfc-editor.org/rfc/rfc6229#section-2),
then checks each native artifact and the RC4 round trip. These tests establish
fixture correctness. Scanner detection and material-binding accuracy require
separate IDA integration assertions; building these fixtures alone does not
establish scanner success.

```

`tests/crypto_fixtures/build.py`:

```py
"""Build independent, reproducible RC4 positives and structurally close negatives.

Nothing here reads external files or imports production scanner code.
Truth, including symbols captured before stripping, is exclusively test data.
"""
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import random
import subprocess

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent


def rc4(key: bytes, data: bytes, *, xor_schedule=False, step=1) -> bytes:
    if not key:
        raise ValueError("RC4 key must be nonempty")
    state = list(range(256))
    j = 0
    for i in range(256):
        j = ((j + state[i]) ^ key[i % len(key)]) % 256 if xor_schedule else (j + state[i] + key[i % len(key)]) % 256
        state[i], state[j] = state[j], state[i]
    i = j = 0
    output = bytearray()
    for octet in data:
        i = (i + step) % 256
        j = (j + state[i]) % 256
        state[i], state[j] = state[j], state[i]
        output.append(octet ^ state[(state[i] + state[j]) % 256])
    return bytes(output)


def symbols(path):
    found = {}
    for line in subprocess.check_output(["nm", "-n", str(path)], text=True).splitlines():
        parts = line.split()
        if len(parts) == 3 and parts[2].startswith(("fixture_", "negative_")):
            found[parts[2]] = "0x%x" % int(parts[0], 16)
    return found


def build():
    cases = []
    configs = [
        ("gcc", "-O0", False, False),
        ("gcc", "-O2", True, True),
        ("clang", "-O1", False, False),
        ("clang", "-O3", True, True),
    ]
    for variant_index, variant in enumerate(("combined", "split", "stack", "encoded", "state_copy", "near_miss")):
        selected_configs = configs[:2] if variant in ("encoded", "state_copy", "near_miss") else configs
        for config_index, (compiler, opt, pie, stripped) in enumerate(selected_configs):
            name = f"{variant}_{compiler}_{opt[1:]}_{'pie' if pie else 'fixed'}"
            folder = ROOT / "bin" / name
            folder.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            rng = random.Random(0xA49C7200 + variant_index * 16 + config_index)
            key_length = (7, 13)[config_index] if variant == "encoded" else (5, 13, 29, 17)[config_index]
            key = bytes(rng.randrange(256) for _ in range(key_length))
            plaintext = bytes(rng.randrange(256) for _ in range((23, 47, 96, 61)[config_index]))
            ciphertext = rc4(key, plaintext)
            key_mask = rng.randrange(1, 256)
            encoded_key = bytes(octet ^ key_mask for octet in key)
            encode = lambda b: ", ".join(f"0x{x:02x}" for x in b)
            (folder / "fixture_values.h").write_text(
                "#include <stdio.h>\n"
                f"#define KEY_BYTES {encode(key)}\n"
                f"#define DATA_BYTES {encode(ciphertext)}\n"
                f"#define DATA_LEN {len(ciphertext)}\n"
                f"#define KEY_LEN {len(key)}\n"
                f"#define ENCODED_KEY_BYTES {encode(encoded_key)}\n"
                f"#define KEY_MASK {key_mask}\n"
            )
            binary = folder / "sample"
            source = "split.c" if variant == "state_copy" else f"{variant}.c"
            command = [compiler, opt, "-g", "-Wall", "-Wextra", "-std=c11",
                       "-fPIE" if pie else "-fno-pie", "-pie" if pie else "-no-pie",
                       "-I", str(folder), str(ROOT / source), "-o", str(binary)]
            if variant == "state_copy":
                command.append("-DCOPY_STATE")
            if variant == "near_miss":
                command.append(str(ROOT / "opaque_mutator.c"))
            subprocess.run(command, check=True, capture_output=True)
            syms = symbols(binary)
            if stripped:
                subprocess.run(["strip", "--strip-all", str(binary)], check=True)
            output = subprocess.check_output([str(binary)])
            expected_output = plaintext
            if variant == "near_miss":
                expected_output = (rc4(key, ciphertext, xor_schedule=True) +
                                   rc4(key, ciphertext, step=2) +
                                   rc4(bytes(x ^ 0x5b for x in key), ciphertext))
            if output != expected_output:
                raise AssertionError(f"Native RC4 output mismatch: {name}")
            roles = {"combined": {"fixture_rc4_combined": "combined"},
                     "split": {"fixture_rc4_schedule": "ksa", "fixture_rc4_stream": "prga"},
                     "stack": {"fixture_rc4_stack": "combined"},
                     "encoded": {"fixture_rc4_encoded": "combined"},
                     "state_copy": {"fixture_rc4_schedule": "ksa", "fixture_rc4_stream": "prga"},
                     "near_miss": {"fixture_rc4_unknown_transform": "combined"}}[variant]
            record = {
                "id": name, "binary": str(binary.relative_to(ROOT)),
                "source": source, "compiler": compiler,
                "optimization": opt, "pie": pie, "stripped": stripped,
                "sha256": hashlib.sha256(binary.read_bytes()).hexdigest(),
                "symbols": syms, "expected_rc4_functions": roles,
                "negative_functions": [s for s in syms if s.startswith("negative_")],
                "key_hex": key.hex(), "ciphertext_hex": ciphertext.hex(),
                "plaintext_hex": plaintext.hex(),
                "key_length": len(key), "data_length": len(ciphertext),
                "binding": "local stack arrays" if variant == "stack" else "global arrays at callsite",
                "native_verification": "pass",
            }
            if variant == "encoded":
                record.update(raw_encoded_key_hex=encoded_key.hex(),
                              key_transform={"operation": "xor", "mask": key_mask},
                              binding="global encoded key, local decoded key, global ciphertext")
            if variant == "state_copy":
                record["state_transform"] = "memcpy of scheduled 256-byte state before PRGA"
            if variant == "near_miss":
                record.update(native_output_hex=expected_output.hex(),
                              expected_unresolved_key_callers=["fixture_unknown_write"],
                              forbidden_stale_key_hex=key.hex(),
                              near_miss_roles={"negative_ksa_xor": "RC4-like variant, not standard RC4",
                                               "negative_prga_step_two": "RC4-like variant, not standard RC4"})
            cases.append(record)
    manifest = {"schema_version": 1, "purpose": "Independent generalization tests; never scanner rules",
                "reference_vectors": "https://www.rfc-editor.org/rfc/rfc6229#section-2",
                "cases": cases}
    (ROOT / "manifest.json").write_text(json.dumps(manifest, indent=2) + "\n")
    return manifest


if __name__ == "__main__":
    print(json.dumps({"built": len(build()["cases"]), "manifest": str(ROOT / "manifest.json")}))

```

`tests/crypto_fixtures/combined.c`:

```c
#include "negative_cases.h"
#include "fixture_values.h"

const unsigned char fixture_key[] = {KEY_BYTES};
const unsigned char fixture_data[] = {DATA_BYTES};

KEEP void fixture_rc4_combined(const unsigned char *key, size_t klen,
                                const unsigned char *data, unsigned char *out, size_t n) {
    unsigned char s[256];
    unsigned i, j = 0;
    for (i = 0; i < 256; ++i) s[i] = (unsigned char)i;
    for (i = 0; i < 256; ++i) {
        j = (j + s[i] + key[i % klen]) & 255;
        unsigned char t = s[i]; s[i] = s[j]; s[j] = t;
    }
    i = 0; j = 0;
    for (size_t p = 0; p < n; ++p) {
        i = (i + 1) & 255; j = (j + s[i]) & 255;
        unsigned char t = s[i]; s[i] = s[j]; s[j] = t;
        out[p] = data[p] ^ s[(s[i] + s[j]) & 255];
    }
}
int main(void) {
    unsigned char out[DATA_LEN];
    fixture_rc4_combined(fixture_key, sizeof fixture_key, fixture_data, out, sizeof out);
    return fwrite(out, 1, sizeof out, stdout) != sizeof out;
}

```

`tests/crypto_fixtures/encoded.c`:

```c
#include "negative_cases.h"
#include "fixture_values.h"

const unsigned char fixture_encoded_key[] = {ENCODED_KEY_BYTES};
const unsigned char fixture_data[] = {DATA_BYTES};

KEEP void fixture_rc4_encoded(unsigned char *out) {
    unsigned char key[KEY_LEN];
    for (unsigned p = 0; p < KEY_LEN; ++p) key[p] = fixture_encoded_key[p] ^ KEY_MASK;
    unsigned char state[256];
    for (unsigned p = 0; p < 256; ++p) state[p] = (unsigned char)p;
    unsigned j = 0;
    for (unsigned p = 0; p < 256; ++p) {
        j = (j + state[p] + key[p % KEY_LEN]) & 255;
        unsigned char t = state[p]; state[p] = state[j]; state[j] = t;
    }
    unsigned i = 0; j = 0;
    for (unsigned p = 0; p < DATA_LEN; ++p) {
        i = (i + 1) & 255; j = (j + state[i]) & 255;
        unsigned char t = state[i]; state[i] = state[j]; state[j] = t;
        out[p] = fixture_data[p] ^ state[(state[i] + state[j]) & 255];
    }
}
int main(void) {
    unsigned char out[DATA_LEN]; fixture_rc4_encoded(out);
    return fwrite(out, 1, sizeof out, stdout) != sizeof out;
}
```

`tests/crypto_fixtures/near_miss.c`:

```c
#include "negative_cases.h"
#include "fixture_values.h"

void opaque_mutator(unsigned char *key, size_t n);

#define TRANSFORM(NAME, MIX, STEP) \
KEEP void NAME(unsigned char *out, const unsigned char *key, size_t width) { \
    unsigned char s[256]; \
    for (unsigned p = 0; p < 256; p++) s[p] = p; \
    unsigned j = 0; \
    for (unsigned p = 0; p < 256; p++) { \
        j = (MIX) & 255; \
        unsigned char t = s[p]; s[p] = s[j]; s[j] = t; \
    } \
    unsigned i = 0; j = 0; \
    const unsigned char data[] = {DATA_BYTES}; \
    for (unsigned p = 0; p < DATA_LEN; p++) { \
        i = (i + STEP) & 255; j = (j + s[i]) & 255; \
        unsigned char t = s[i]; s[i] = s[j]; s[j] = t; \
        out[p] = data[p] ^ s[(s[i] + s[j]) & 255]; \
    } \
}

TRANSFORM(negative_ksa_xor, ((j + s[p]) ^ key[p % width]), 1)
TRANSFORM(negative_prga_step_two, (j + s[p] + key[p % width]), 2)
TRANSFORM(fixture_rc4_unknown_transform, (j + s[p] + key[p % width]), 1)

KEEP void fixture_unknown_write(unsigned char *out) {
    unsigned char key[] = {KEY_BYTES};
    opaque_mutator(key, sizeof key);
    fixture_rc4_unknown_transform(out, key, sizeof key);
}

int main(void) {
    unsigned char out[DATA_LEN];
    const unsigned char key[] = {KEY_BYTES};
    negative_ksa_xor(out, key, sizeof key);
    if (fwrite(out, 1, sizeof out, stdout) != sizeof out) return 1;
    negative_prga_step_two(out, key, sizeof key);
    if (fwrite(out, 1, sizeof out, stdout) != sizeof out) return 1;
    fixture_unknown_write(out);
    return fwrite(out, 1, sizeof out, stdout) != sizeof out;
}

```

`tests/crypto_fixtures/negative_cases.h`:

```h
#include <stddef.h>
#include <stdint.h>
#define KEEP __attribute__((noinline, used))

/* Intentionally close structural negatives, each with a different missing RC4 invariant. */
KEEP void negative_identity(unsigned char *table) {
    for (unsigned i = 0; i != 256; ++i) table[i] = (unsigned char)i;
}
KEEP void negative_shuffle(unsigned char *table, unsigned seed) {
    for (unsigned i = 0; i < 256; i++) table[i] = (unsigned char)i;
    for (unsigned i = 255; i > 0; --i) {
        seed = seed * 1664525U + 1013904223U;
        unsigned j = seed % (i + 1);
        unsigned char t = table[i]; table[i] = table[j]; table[j] = t;
    }
}
KEEP void negative_xor(unsigned char *buf, size_t n, const unsigned char *key, size_t k) {
    for (size_t p = 0; p < n; ++p) buf[p] ^= key[p % k];
}
KEEP void negative_modulo(unsigned char *table, unsigned char *buf, size_t n) {
    for (unsigned i = 0; i < 256; i++) table[i] = (unsigned char)i;
    unsigned j = 0;
    for (unsigned i = 0; i < 256; i++) {
        j = (j + table[i] + 13) & 255;
        table[i] ^= table[j];
    }
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; i++) buf[i] ^= table[(i + j) & 255];
}

```

```c
/* Separate translation unit: callers cannot establish the post-call key bytes. */
void opaque_mutator(unsigned char *key, size_t n) {
    for (size_t p = 0; p < n; ++p) key[p] ^= 0x5b;
```

`tests/crypto_fixtures/split.c`:

```c
#include "negative_cases.h"
#include "fixture_values.h"
#include <string.h>

const uint8_t fixture_key[] = {KEY_BYTES};
const uint8_t fixture_data[] = {DATA_BYTES};

KEEP void fixture_rc4_schedule(uint8_t *state, const uint8_t *password, unsigned width) {
    unsigned a = 0;
    do { state[a] = a; } while (++a != 256);
    uint8_t b = 0;
    a = 0;
    do {
        b += state[a] + password[a % width];
        uint8_t saved = state[b]; state[b] = state[a]; state[a] = saved;
    } while (++a < 256);
}
KEEP void fixture_rc4_stream(uint8_t *state, const uint8_t *src, uint8_t *dst, unsigned n) {
    uint8_t x = 0, y = 0;
    while (n--) {
        ++x; y += state[x];
        uint8_t saved = state[y]; state[y] = state[x]; state[x] = saved;
        uint8_t slot = state[x] + state[y];
        *dst++ = *src++ ^ state[slot];
    }
}
KEEP void fixture_rc4_caller(unsigned char *out) {
    uint8_t state[256];
    fixture_rc4_schedule(state, fixture_key, sizeof fixture_key);
#ifdef COPY_STATE
    uint8_t duplicate[256];
    memcpy(duplicate, state, sizeof duplicate);
    fixture_rc4_stream(duplicate, fixture_data, out, DATA_LEN);
#else
    fixture_rc4_stream(state, fixture_data, out, DATA_LEN);
#endif
}
int main(void) {
    unsigned char out[DATA_LEN];
    fixture_rc4_caller(out);
    return fwrite(out, 1, sizeof out, stdout) != sizeof out;
}

```

`tests/crypto_fixtures/stack.c`:

```c
#include "negative_cases.h"
#include "fixture_values.h"

KEEP void fixture_rc4_stack(unsigned char *destination) {
    unsigned char password[] = {KEY_BYTES};
    unsigned char encoded[] = {DATA_BYTES};
    unsigned char permutation[256];
    int cursor, accumulator = 0;
    for (cursor = 0; cursor <= 255; cursor++) permutation[cursor] = cursor;
    for (cursor = 0; cursor <= 255; cursor++) {
        accumulator = (accumulator + permutation[cursor] + password[cursor % sizeof password]) % 256;
        unsigned char old = permutation[cursor];
        permutation[cursor] = permutation[accumulator]; permutation[accumulator] = old;
    }
    cursor = 0; accumulator = 0;
    for (unsigned p = 0; p < sizeof encoded; p++) {
        cursor = (cursor + 1) % 256;
        accumulator = (accumulator + permutation[cursor]) % 256;
        unsigned char old = permutation[accumulator];
        permutation[accumulator] = permutation[cursor]; permutation[cursor] = old;
        destination[p] = encoded[p] ^ permutation[(permutation[cursor] + permutation[accumulator]) % 256];
    }
}
int main(void) {
    unsigned char out[DATA_LEN]; fixture_rc4_stack(out);
    return fwrite(out, 1, sizeof out, stdout) != sizeof out;
}
```

`tests/fixtures/PROVENANCE.md`:

```md
# Test fixture provenance

`check.c`, `echo.c`, `loop.c` and `long_argv_compare.c` are small harmless programs written for
this project. The pytest suite compiles them with the local C compiler; no prebuilt binary is
shipped or executed.

`fauxware.c` is angr's teaching crackme and is not redistributed here. Run
`tests/fixtures/fetch_fauxware.sh` to download it from the pinned angr-doc commit
(`bf380700f2baa092c2970a2dceb0eb2793bd9837`) and verify its SHA-256
(`a2a96e7abd1abd2f213665192661878fd7bc1d61490a66114d440d26bfb7070e`). Without it, the
fauxware test is skipped. The reviewed source reads fixed eight-byte username and password
fields; its `SOSNEAKY` password branch authenticates without opening a file. The test asserts
the recovered password equals `SOSNEAKY` before replaying the input natively in a temporary
directory with a three-second timeout.

```

```c
__attribute__((noinline)) int check(const unsigned char *p) {
    return p[0] == 'I' && p[1] == 'D' && (p[2] ^ 0x33) == 0x72 &&
        p[3] + p[4] == 155 && p[3] - p[4] == 11 && p[5] == '!';
}
__attribute__((noinline)) void accepted(void) { puts("ACCEPTED"); }
__attribute__((noinline)) void rejected(void) { puts("REJECTED"); }
int main(int argc, char **argv) {
    unsigned char p[7] = {0};
    if (argc > 1) { if (strlen(argv[1]) != 6) return 2; memcpy(p, argv[1], 6); }
    else if (read(0, p, 6) != 6) return 2;
    if (check(p)) { accepted(); return 0; }
    rejected(); return 1;
}
```

`tests/libc_differential_fixture.c`:

```c
/* Native libc oracle. The fixed output buffer makes untouched bytes observable. */
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <strings.h>

int oracle_fgets(const unsigned char *data, size_t length, int size,
                 unsigned char *output, long *position) {
    FILE *stream = tmpfile();
    if (!stream) return -1;
    if (fwrite(data, 1, length, stream) != length || fseek(stream, 0, SEEK_SET)) {
        fclose(stream);
        return -1;
    }
    memset(output, 0xa5, 32);
    char *result = fgets((char *)output, size, stream);
    *position = ftell(stream);
    int kind = !result ? 0 : result == (char *)output ? 1 : 2;
    fclose(stream);
    return kind;
}

size_t oracle_span(const char *string, const char *reject) { return strcspn(string, reject); }
int oracle_strcmp(const char *left, const char *right) { return strcmp(left, right); }
int oracle_strncmp(const char *left, const char *right, size_t n) { return strncmp(left, right, n); }
int oracle_memcmp(const void *left, const void *right, size_t n) { return memcmp(left, right, n); }
int oracle_bcmp(const void *left, const void *right, size_t n) { return bcmp(left, right, n); }
```
