`tests/multi_input_fixture.c`:

```c
/* Harmless teaching fixture: only this local source is run natively in tests. */
#include <stdio.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <unistd.h>
__attribute__((noinline)) int multi_check(const unsigned char *a, const unsigned char *b) {
    return a[0]=='A' && a[1]>= 'B' && a[1]<= 'M' && a[2]=='!' &&
           b[0]==a[0]+3 && b[1]==a[1]+1 && b[2]=='?';
}
__attribute__((noinline)) int multi_count(const unsigned char *b, int n, const unsigned char *a) {
    return n==3 && multi_check(a,b);
}
__attribute__((noinline)) int byte_at_least_200(const unsigned char *a) {
    return a[0] >= 200;
}
__attribute__((noinline)) void accepted(void) { puts("ACCEPTED"); }
__attribute__((noinline)) void rejected(void) { puts("REJECTED"); }
int main(int argc, char **argv) {
    unsigned char a[5]={0}, b[5]={0};
    if(argc==3) { memcpy(a,argv[1],3); memcpy(b,argv[2],3); }
    else if(argc==2 && strcmp(argv[1],"--lines")==0) {
        if(!fgets((char *)a,5,stdin) || !fgets((char *)b,5,stdin)) return 2;
    }
    else if(read(0,a,3)!=3 || read(0,b,3)!=3) return 2;
    if(multi_check(a,b)) { accepted(); return 0; }
    rejected(); return 1;
}

```

`tests/multi_input_test.py`:

```py
"""Joint-model solver regression; native execution uses only our reviewed fixture."""
import importlib.util
import json
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import sys

import pytest

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('multi_solve', ROOT/'backend/solve.py')
worker = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
spec.loader.exec_module(worker)


@pytest.fixture(scope='module')
def binary(tmp_path_factory):
    path = tmp_path_factory.mktemp('multi_input')/'fixture'
    subprocess.run(['cc', '-O0', '-g', '-fno-builtin', '-no-pie',
                    str(ROOT/'tests/multi_input_fixture.c'), '-o', str(path)], check=True)
    return path


def symbols(binary):
    return {parts[2]: int(parts[0], 16) for line in
            subprocess.check_output(['nm', '-n', str(binary)], text=True).splitlines()
            if len(parts := line.split()) == 3}


def request(binary, mode):
    syms = symbols(binary)
    return dict(binary=str(binary), mode=mode, timeout=30,
                inputs=[dict(name='input1', length=3, prefix='A', suffix='!',
                             byte_constraints=[dict(offset=1, min=66, max=77)]),
                        dict(name='input2', length=3, suffix='?')],
                **(dict(start_addr=syms['multi_check'], return_value=1) if mode=='call'
                   else dict(find=[syms['accepted']], avoid=[syms['rejected']])))


@pytest.mark.parametrize('mode', ['stdin','argv','call','call_selected'])
def test_joint_solutions(binary, mode):
    req = request(binary, 'call' if mode=='call_selected' else mode)
    if mode == 'call_selected':
        req.update(start_addr=symbols(binary)['multi_count'], call_args=[
            dict(kind='buffer', input='input2'), dict(kind='int', value=3),
            dict(kind='buffer', input='input1')])
    result = worker.solve(req)
    assert result['status']=='found', result
    assert [item['name'] for item in result['inputs']]==['input1','input2']
    a,b = [bytes.fromhex(item['input_hex']) for item in result['inputs']]
    assert a[0]==65 and b[0]==a[0]+3 and b[1]==a[1]+1
    assert a[2:]==b'!' and b[2:]==b'?'
    if mode=='stdin':
        assert bytes.fromhex(result['input_hex'])==a+b
        native = subprocess.run([str(binary)], input=a+b, capture_output=True, timeout=3)
    else:
        assert 'input_hex' not in result
        native = subprocess.run([str(binary),a.decode(),b.decode()], capture_output=True, timeout=3)
    assert native.returncode==0 and b'ACCEPTED' in native.stdout


def test_legacy_single(binary):
    req = dict(binary=str(binary), mode='stdin', length=6, prefix='AB!DC?',
               find=[symbols(binary)['accepted']], timeout=30)
    result=worker.solve(req)
    assert result['status']=='found', result
    assert bytes.fromhex(result['input_hex'])==b'AB!DC?'
    assert len(result['inputs'])==1 and result['inputs'][0]['name']=='input1'


def test_unsigned_byte_minimum_crossing_signed_boundary(binary):
    req = dict(binary=str(binary), mode='call', start_addr=symbols(binary)['byte_at_least_200'],
               length=1, printable=False, return_value=1,
               byte_constraints=[dict(offset=0, min=200, max=255)], timeout=30)
    result = worker.solve(req)
    assert result['status'] == 'found', result
    assert bytes.fromhex(result['input_hex'])[0] >= 200


@pytest.mark.parametrize('inputs', [[], None, {}, [1], [dict(name='a',length=1)]*2,
    [dict(name='bad name',length=1)], [dict(name='a',length=0)],
    [dict(name='a',length=65537)], [dict(name='a',length=1.5)],
    [dict(name='a',length=True)], [dict(name='a',length=1,prefix='é')],
    [dict(name='a',length=1,printable='yes')],
    [dict(name='a',length=1,byte_constraints={})],
    [dict(name=f'a{i}',length=1) for i in range(33)],
    [dict(name='a',length=40000),dict(name='b',length=40000)]])
def test_invalid_input_config(inputs):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        worker.input_specs(dict(inputs=inputs))


@pytest.mark.parametrize('selector', [None,'missing',1,[]])
def test_ambiguous_unknown_buffer(selector):
    specs=worker.input_specs(dict(inputs=[dict(name='a',length=1),dict(name='b',length=1)]))
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        worker.call_input_name(dict(kind='buffer',input=selector),specs)


def test_one_buffer_default_and_boundary():
    specs=worker.input_specs(dict(inputs=[dict(name='a',length=65536)]))
    assert worker.call_input_name(dict(kind='buffer'),specs)=='a'


def test_stdin_lines_and_per_input_options(binary):
    req=request(binary,'stdin')
    req.update(printable=False, argv_before=['--lines'])
    req['inputs'][0].update(length=4, suffix='!\n')
    req['inputs'][1].update(length=4, suffix='?\n',
                            byte_constraints=[dict(offset=1,value=67)])
    result=worker.solve(req)
    assert result['status']=='found',result
    assert [item['input_text'] for item in result['inputs']]==['AB!\n','DC?\n']
    native=subprocess.run([str(binary),'--lines'],input=bytes.fromhex(result['input_hex']),
                          capture_output=True,timeout=3)
    assert native.returncode==0 and b'ACCEPTED' in native.stdout


@pytest.mark.parametrize('constraint',[{},dict(offset=-1),dict(offset=1),dict(offset=True),
    dict(offset=0,value=256),dict(offset=0,min=-1),dict(offset=0,max=1.2)])
def test_invalid_byte_constraints(constraint):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        worker.input_specs(dict(inputs=[dict(name='a',length=1,byte_constraints=[constraint])]))
```

`tests/predicate_fixture.c`:

```c
/* Defined-width branch counts over every byte, used as an independent oracle. */
#include <stdint.h>
#include <stdio.h>

int main(void) {
    unsigned parity = 0, signed_growth = 0, unsigned_growth = 0;
    for (unsigned x = 0; x < 256; ++x) {
        uint8_t next = (uint8_t)(x + 1);
        int signed_x = x < 128 ? (int)x : (int)x - 256;
        int signed_next = next < 128 ? (int)next : (int)next - 256;
        parity += ((uint8_t)(x * (x + 1)) & 1) == 0;
        signed_growth += signed_next > signed_x;
        unsigned_growth += next > x;
    }
    printf("%u %u %u\n", parity, signed_growth, unsigned_growth);
    return 0;
}

```

```c
    if (scanf("%u", &seed) != 1) {

    }
    srand(seed);
    if (rand() == 1804289383 && rand() == 846930886) {
```

`tests/test_angr_compat.py`:

```py
import pytest

from angr import claripy
from angr.engines.vex.claripy import irop

from backend.angr_compat import install_avx512_permute_models


@pytest.mark.parametrize(('op_name', 'lane_width', 'lane_count'), [
    ('Iop_PermI8x16', 8, 16), ('Iop_PermI8x32', 8, 32), ('Iop_PermI8x64', 8, 64),
    ('Iop_PermI16x8', 16, 8), ('Iop_PermI16x16', 16, 16), ('Iop_PermI16x32', 16, 32),
    ('Iop_PermI32x4', 32, 4), ('Iop_PermI32x8', 32, 8), ('Iop_PermI32x16', 32, 16),
    ('Iop_PermI64x2', 64, 2), ('Iop_PermI64x4', 64, 4), ('Iop_PermI64x8', 64, 8),
])
def test_evex_permute_uses_both_sources_and_low_selector_bits(op_name, lane_width, lane_count):
    installed = install_avx512_permute_models()
    assert op_name in installed or op_name in irop.operations
    model = irop.vexop_to_simop(op_name)
    lane_mask = (1 << lane_width) - 1
    destination = [(i * 17 + 3) & lane_mask for i in range(lane_count)]
    source = [(i * 23 + 11) & lane_mask for i in range(lane_count)]
    indices = [(i * 7 + lane_count) & (2 * lane_count - 1) for i in range(lane_count)]
    bv = lambda values: claripy.BVV(sum(value << (i * lane_width)
                                         for i, value in enumerate(values)),
                                    lane_count * lane_width)

    result = model.calculate(bv(destination), bv(indices), bv(source))
    actual = claripy.Solver().eval(result, 1)[0]
    expected = [((source if index >= lane_count else destination)[index % lane_count])
                for index in indices]
    actual_lanes = [(actual >> (i * lane_width)) & lane_mask for i in range(lane_count)]
    assert actual_lanes == expected


def test_avx512_byte_permute_keeps_symbolic_indices():
    install_avx512_permute_models()
    model = irop.vexop_to_simop('Iop_PermI8x64')
    destination = claripy.BVV(int.from_bytes(bytes(range(64)), 'little'), 512)
    source = claripy.BVV(int.from_bytes(bytes(range(128, 192)), 'little'), 512)
    symbolic_index = claripy.BVS('permute_index', 8)
    indices = claripy.Concat(claripy.BVV(0, 504), symbolic_index)

    result = model.calculate(destination, indices, source)
    solver = claripy.Solver()
    solver.add(symbolic_index == 64)
    assert solver.eval(result[7:0], 1)[0] == 128


def test_avx512_permute_simplifies_expanded_mask_indices():
    install_avx512_permute_models()
    model = irop.vexop_to_simop('Iop_PermI8x64')
    destination = claripy.BVV(int.from_bytes(bytes(range(64)), 'little'), 512)
    source = claripy.BVV(int.from_bytes(bytes(range(128, 192)), 'little'), 512)
    mask_bit = claripy.BVS('mask_bit', 1)
    expanded_byte = claripy.If(mask_bit == 1, claripy.BVV(0xff, 8), claripy.BVV(0, 8))
    indices = claripy.Concat(*([expanded_byte] * 64))

    result = model.calculate(destination, indices, source)
    solver = claripy.Solver()
    solver.add(mask_bit == 1)
    assert solver.eval(result[7:0], 1)[0] == 191


def test_vpermb_uses_index_then_source_and_ignores_high_selector_bits():
    install_avx512_permute_models()
    model = irop.vexop_to_simop('Iop_Perm8x64')
    source = bytes(range(64))
    indices = bytes(((i * 13) | 0x80) & 0xff for i in range(64))
    source_bv = claripy.BVV(int.from_bytes(source, 'little'), 512)
    index_bv = claripy.BVV(int.from_bytes(indices, 'little'), 512)

    result = model.calculate(index_bv, source_bv)
    actual = claripy.Solver().eval(result, 1)[0].to_bytes(64, 'little')
    expected = bytes(source[index & 0x3f] for index in indices)
    assert actual == expected


def test_vpermb_keeps_symbolic_selector_bit_seven_irrelevant():
    install_avx512_permute_models()
    model = irop.vexop_to_simop('Iop_Perm8x64')
    source = claripy.BVV(int.from_bytes(bytes(range(64)), 'little'), 512)
    symbolic_index = claripy.BVS('vpermb_index', 8)
    indices = claripy.Concat(claripy.BVV(0, 504), symbolic_index)

    result = model.calculate(indices, source)
    solver = claripy.Solver()
    solver.add(symbolic_index == 0xc0)
    assert solver.eval(result[7:0], 1)[0] == 0
```

`tests/test_compact_input_domains.py`:

```py
from angr import claripy

from backend.solve import symbolic_input


def test_binary_byte_domains_use_one_symbolic_bit_per_byte():
    spec = dict(length=4, printable=False, prefix='', suffix='', byte_constraints=[
        {'offset': index, 'min': 48, 'max': 49} for index in range(4)
    ])
    value = symbolic_input(claripy, spec, 'arsenal_input')

    assert value.size() == 32
    assert len(value.variables) == 4
    assert all(variable.endswith('_1') for variable in value.variables)
    solver = claripy.Solver()
    for byte in value.chop(8):
        solver.add(claripy.And(claripy.UGE(byte, claripy.BVV(48, 8)),
                               claripy.ULE(byte, claripy.BVV(49, 8))))
    assert solver.satisfiable()
    for boundary in (48, 49):
        check = claripy.Solver()
        check.add(value.get_byte(0) == boundary)
        assert check.satisfiable()
    for outside in (47, 50):
        check = claripy.Solver()
        check.add(value.get_byte(0) == outside)
        assert not check.satisfiable()


def test_argv_nul_exclusion_is_folded_into_compact_domain():
    spec = dict(length=2, printable=False, prefix='', suffix='', byte_constraints=[
        {'offset': 0, 'min': 0, 'max': 1}, {'offset': 1, 'value': 65}
    ])
    value = symbolic_input(claripy, spec, 'arsenal_input', argv=True)

    solver = claripy.Solver()
    solver.add(claripy.And(value.get_byte(0) == 1, value.get_byte(1) == 65))
    assert solver.satisfiable()
    solver.add(value.get_byte(0) == 0)
    assert not solver.satisfiable()


def test_printable_ascii_uses_seven_symbolic_bits_per_byte():
    spec = dict(length=4, printable=True, prefix='', suffix='', byte_constraints=[])
    value = symbolic_input(claripy, spec, 'arsenal_input')

    assert value.size() == 32
    assert len(value.variables) == 4
    assert all(variable.endswith('_7') for variable in value.variables)
    solver = claripy.Solver()
    for byte in value.chop(8):
        solver.add(claripy.And(claripy.UGE(byte, claripy.BVV(32, 8)),
                               claripy.ULE(byte, claripy.BVV(126, 8))))
    assert solver.satisfiable()
    for boundary in (32, 126):
        check = claripy.Solver()
        check.add(claripy.And(claripy.UGE(value.get_byte(0), claripy.BVV(32, 8)),
                              claripy.ULE(value.get_byte(0), claripy.BVV(126, 8))))
        check.add(value.get_byte(0) == boundary)
        assert check.satisfiable()
    for outside in (31, 127):
        check = claripy.Solver()
        check.add(claripy.And(claripy.UGE(value.get_byte(0), claripy.BVV(32, 8)),
                              claripy.ULE(value.get_byte(0), claripy.BVV(126, 8))))
        check.add(value.get_byte(0) == outside)
        assert not check.satisfiable()


def test_nonzero_offset_range_preserves_endpoints_without_byte_wraparound():
    spec = dict(length=1, printable=False, prefix='', suffix='', byte_constraints=[
        {'offset': 0, 'min': 100, 'max': 200}
    ])
    value = symbolic_input(claripy, spec, 'arsenal_input')

    for allowed in (100, 150, 200):
        solver = claripy.Solver()
        solver.add(claripy.And(claripy.UGE(value.get_byte(0), claripy.BVV(100, 8)),
                               claripy.ULE(value.get_byte(0), claripy.BVV(200, 8))))
        solver.add(value.get_byte(0) == allowed)
        assert solver.satisfiable()
    for outside in (99, 201):
        solver = claripy.Solver()
        solver.add(claripy.And(claripy.UGE(value.get_byte(0), claripy.BVV(100, 8)),
                               claripy.ULE(value.get_byte(0), claripy.BVV(200, 8))))
        solver.add(value.get_byte(0) == outside)
        assert not solver.satisfiable()
```

`tests/test_crypto_algorithms.py`:

```py
"""Native fingerprint positives and deliberately misleading observations."""
import sys
from pathlib import Path
import pytest
sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / 'plugin'))
from crypto_algorithms import AES_SBOX, SHA256_K, MD5_K, classify, referenced_tables


def features(**kw):
    return dict(function=0x1000, **kw)


def test_sbox_is_permutation():
    assert len(AES_SBOX) == len(set(AES_SBOX)) == 256


@pytest.mark.parametrize('order', ['little', 'big'])
def test_exact_referenced_tables(order):
    data = b''.join(n.to_bytes(4, order) for n in SHA256_K)
    assert 'SHA-256' in referenced_tables(data, 0x2000, 0x1000)
    assert not referenced_tables(b'x' + data, 0x2000, 0x1000)
    assert not referenced_tables(data[:-1], 0x2000, 0x1000)
    altered = bytearray(data); altered[3] ^= 1
    assert not referenced_tables(bytes(altered), 0x2000, 0x1000)


def test_constants_alone_do_not_claim_crypto():
    assert not classify(features(constants=list(SHA256_K) + list(MD5_K) + [0x9e3779b9]))
    assert not classify(features(tables=referenced_tables(AES_SBOX, 0x2000, 0x1000)))


def test_aes_requires_reads_and_mixing():
    f = features(tables=referenced_tables(AES_SBOX, 0x2000, 0x1000), operations={'and': 4}, memory_reads=4)
    assert classify(f)[0]['algorithm'] == 'AES'
    f['memory_reads'] = 0
    assert not classify(f)


@pytest.mark.parametrize('name,rotations', [('ChaCha', [16, 12, 8, 7]), ('Salsa20', [7, 9, 13, 18])])
def test_arx_candidates_need_full_rotation_set(name, rotations):
    f = features(rotl32=rotations, operations={'add': 4, 'xor': 4})
    result = classify(f)
    assert [x['algorithm'] for x in result] == [name]
    assert result[0]['materials'] == []
    assert 'candidate' in result[0]['status']
    f['rotl32'] = rotations[:-1]
    assert not classify(f)


def test_tea_delta_is_not_enough():
    f = features(constants=[0x9e3779b9], shifts=[4, 5], operations={'add': 6, 'xor': 4}, backedges=1)
    assert classify(f)[0]['algorithm'] == 'TEA/XTEA family'
    f['backedges'] = 0
    assert not classify(f)


def test_sha256_rotations_and_table_required():
    f = features(rotl32=[26, 21, 7], operations={'add': 3, 'xor': 2, 'and': 1}, constants=SHA256_K)
    assert classify(f)[0]['algorithm'] == 'SHA-256/SHA-224'
    f['constants'] = SHA256_K[:5]
    assert not classify(f)


def test_hardware_does_not_infer_key_or_variant():
    result = classify(features(hardware={'AES': [(0x1234, 'aesenc')]}))[0]
    assert result['status'] == 'hardware instruction evidence'
    assert result['evidence'][0]['site'] == 0x1234
    assert result['materials'] == []
```

`tests/test_crypto_constant_references.py`:

```py
"""Address proof guards for ctree table references, independent of names/bytes."""
from pathlib import Path
import sys
from types import SimpleNamespace

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / 'plugin'))
from crypto_algorithms import ctree_constant_references


class Type:
    def __init__(self, size=8, unsigned=False, pointer=False, array=False):
        self.size, self.unsigned, self.pointer, self.array = size, unsigned, pointer, array

    def get_size(self): return self.size
    def is_unsigned(self): return self.unsigned
    def is_ptr(self): return self.pointer
    def is_array(self): return self.array


HX = SimpleNamespace(CV_FAST=0)
for number, name in enumerate(('cot_num', 'cot_var', 'cot_cast', 'cot_add', 'cot_sub', 'cot_mul',
                               'cot_ref', 'cot_obj', 'cot_ptr', 'cot_idx', 'cot_call', 'cot_helper')):
    setattr(HX, name, number)
HX.cot_asg, HX.cot_asgumod = 50, 60
for number, name in enumerate(('cot_preinc', 'cot_predec', 'cot_postinc', 'cot_postdec',
                               'cit_block', 'cit_expr', 'cit_do', 'cit_return', 'cit_goto',
                               'cit_break', 'cit_continue', 'cit_if'), 61):
    setattr(HX, name, number)


def expr(op, **kwargs):
    return SimpleNamespace(op=getattr(HX, op), type=kwargs.pop('type', Type()), ea=0x1000, **kwargs)


def num(value): return expr('cot_num', numval=lambda: value)
def var(index): return expr('cot_var', v=SimpleNamespace(idx=index))
def assign(index, value): return expr('cot_asg', x=var(index), y=value)
def obj(address): return expr('cot_obj', obj_ea=address, type=Type(256, array=True))
def ref(value): return expr('cot_ref', x=value, type=Type(pointer=True))
def load_index(address, index): return expr('cot_idx', x=obj(address), y=index, type=Type(1, unsigned=True))


def stmt(op, **kwargs):
    return SimpleNamespace(op=getattr(HX, op), label_num=-1, **kwargs)


def block(*expressions):
    return stmt('cit_block', cblock=[stmt('cit_expr', cexpr=item) for item in expressions])


class Visitor:
    def __init__(self, flags=None): self.parents = [None]
    def apply_to(self, node, parent):
        if self.visit_expr(node):
            return 1
        for child in [getattr(node, name) for name in ('x', 'y') if hasattr(node, name)] + list(getattr(node, 'a', [])):
            self.parents.append(node)
            if self.apply_to(child, node):
                self.parents.pop()
                return 1
            self.parents.pop()
        return 0


HX.ctree_visitor_t = Visitor
HX.ctree_parentee_t = Visitor


def addresses(body):
    return {item['address'] for item in ctree_constant_references(SimpleNamespace(body=body), HX)}


def test_negative_index_recovers_exact_start_after_pure_rotation():
    rotate = expr('cot_call', x=expr('cot_helper', helper='__ROL8__'), a=[var(7), num(13)])
    body = block(assign(0, num(-192)), assign(1, var(0)), assign(7, rotate),
                 load_index(0x4000 + 192, var(1)))
    assert addresses(body) == {0x4000}


def test_unknown_calls_and_overwrites_discard_index_knowledge():
    call = expr('cot_call', x=obj(0x5000), a=[])
    assert addresses(block(assign(0, num(-192)), call, load_index(0x40c0, var(0)))) == set()
    assert addresses(block(assign(0, num(-192)), assign(0, var(8)), load_index(0x40c0, var(0)))) == set()


def test_alias_store_and_unknown_control_flow_discard_knowledge():
    store = expr('cot_asg', x=load_index(0x6000, num(0)), y=num(4))
    assert addresses(block(assign(0, num(-192)), store, load_index(0x40c0, var(0)))) == set()
    body = block(assign(0, num(-192)))
    body.cblock.extend([stmt('cit_if'), stmt('cit_expr', cexpr=load_index(0x40c0, var(0)))])
    assert addresses(body) == set()


def test_first_do_iteration_is_known_but_exit_value_is_not_guessed():
    body = block(assign(0, num(-192)))
    body.cblock.extend([stmt('cit_do', cdo=SimpleNamespace(body=block(load_index(0x40c0, var(0))))),
                       stmt('cit_expr', cexpr=load_index(0x50c0, var(0)))])
    assert addresses(body) == {0x4000}


def test_pointer_cast_must_preserve_address_bits():
    original = ref(obj(0x140002000))
    same_width = expr('cot_cast', x=original, type=Type(8, pointer=True))
    assert addresses(block(expr('cot_ptr', x=same_width))) == {0x140002000}
    truncated = expr('cot_cast', x=original, type=Type(4, unsigned=True))
    widened = expr('cot_cast', x=truncated, type=Type(8, pointer=True))
    assert addresses(block(expr('cot_ptr', x=widened))) == set()


def test_labels_invalidate_prior_assignments():
    body = block(assign(0, num(-192)), load_index(0x40c0, var(0)))
    body.cblock[-1].label_num = 1
    assert addresses(body) == set()


def test_address_computation_under_truncation_is_not_a_table_read():
    indexed = ref(load_index(0x1400020c0, num(-192)))
    truncated = expr('cot_cast', x=indexed, type=Type(4, unsigned=True))
    widened = expr('cot_cast', x=truncated, type=Type(8, pointer=True))
    assert addresses(block(expr('cot_ptr', x=widened))) == set()
    assert addresses(block(expr('cot_ptr', x=indexed))) == {0x140002000}


def test_multidimensional_array_decay_is_not_a_load():
    row = expr('cot_idx', x=obj(0x140002000), y=num(0), type=Type(8, array=True))
    cell = expr('cot_idx', x=row, y=num(0), type=Type(1))
    truncated = expr('cot_cast', x=ref(cell), type=Type(4, unsigned=True))
    widened = expr('cot_cast', x=truncated, type=Type(8, pointer=True))
    assert addresses(block(expr('cot_ptr', x=widened))) == set()

```

`tests/test_crypto_extended.py`:

```py
"""Guard the added families against shared constants, wrong widths and decoys."""
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / 'plugin'))
from crypto_algorithms import (BLAKE2B_IV, BLAKE2S_IV, BLAKE2_SIGMA, KECCAK_RC,
                               KECCAK_RHO, SHA512_K, classify, referenced_tables)


def names(features):
    return {finding['algorithm'] for finding in classify(dict(function=0x1000, **features))}


@pytest.mark.parametrize('order', ['little', 'big'])
@pytest.mark.parametrize('name,words,width', [
    ('SHA-512', SHA512_K, 8), ('BLAKE2s IV', BLAKE2S_IV, 4),
    ('BLAKE2b IV', BLAKE2B_IV, 8), ('Keccak round constants', KECCAK_RC, 8),
    ('Keccak rho offsets', KECCAK_RHO, 4)])
def test_extended_tables_require_exact_start_and_full_signature(name, words, width, order):
    data = b''.join(word.to_bytes(width, order) for word in words)
    assert name in referenced_tables(data, 0x2000, 0x1000)
    assert name not in referenced_tables(data[:-1], 0x2000, 0x1000)
    assert name not in referenced_tables(b'x' + data, 0x2000, 0x1000)
    altered = bytearray(data)
    altered[-1] ^= 1
    assert name not in referenced_tables(altered, 0x2000, 0x1000)


def test_sha512_requires_full_width_constants_and_both_sigma_rotation_sets():
    data = dict(constants64=SHA512_K, rotl64=[36, 30, 25, 50, 46, 23],
                operations64={'add': 4, 'xor': 3, 'and': 2})
    expected = {'SHA-512/SHA-384 family'}
    assert names(data) == expected
    for key, value in [('constants64', [x & 0xffffffff for x in SHA512_K]),
                       ('rotl64', [36, 30, 25]), ('operations64', {})]:
        assert not names(dict(data, **{key: value}))
    data['operations'] = data.pop('operations64')
    assert not names(data)  # 32-bit/unknown-width arithmetic is insufficient.


@pytest.mark.parametrize('name,width,iv,rots', [
    ('BLAKE2s', 32, BLAKE2S_IV, [16, 20, 24, 25]),
    ('BLAKE2b', 64, BLAKE2B_IV, [32, 40, 48, 1])])
def test_blake_requires_correct_width_full_rotations_and_corroboration(name, width, iv, rots):
    data = {'rotl%d' % width: rots, 'operations%d' % width: {'add': 6, 'xor': 4},
            'constants' if width == 32 else 'constants64': iv}
    assert names(data) == {name}
    no_iv = dict(data)
    no_iv.pop('constants' if width == 32 else 'constants64')
    assert not names(no_iv)
    no_iv['tables'] = referenced_tables(BLAKE2_SIGMA, 0x2000, 0x1000)
    assert names(no_iv) == {name}
    no_iv['rotl%d' % width] = rots[:-1]
    assert not names(no_iv)


def test_chacha_left_rotations_are_not_blake2s_right_rotations():
    assert not names(dict(rotl32=[16, 12, 8, 7], constants=BLAKE2S_IV,
                          operations32={'add': 6, 'xor': 4}))


def test_keccak_requires_rounds_boolean_mixing_and_rho_evidence():
    data = dict(constants64=KECCAK_RC, rotl64=KECCAK_RHO,
                operations64={'xor': 5, 'and': 1, 'not': 1})
    expected = {'Keccak-f[1600] / SHA-3 family'}
    assert names(data) == expected
    for key, value in [('constants64', []), ('rotl64', [1]),
                       ('operations64', {'xor': 5, 'and': 1})]:
        assert not names(dict(data, **{key: value}))
    data['rotl64'] = [1]
    data['variable_rotate64'] = True
    assert not names(data)
    data['tables'] = referenced_tables(bytes(KECCAK_RHO), 0x2000, 0x1000)
    assert names(data) == expected


def test_all_new_tables_without_mixing_are_only_data():
    tables = {}
    for words in (SHA512_K, BLAKE2B_IV, KECCAK_RC):
        tables.update(referenced_tables(b''.join(x.to_bytes(8, 'little') for x in words), 0x2000, 0x1000))
    assert not names(dict(tables=tables))
```

`tests/test_crypto_patterns.py`:

```py
"""Algorithm invariants, independent of sample bytes and symbol names."""
import sys
from pathlib import Path
sys.path.insert(0,str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]/'plugin'))
from crypto_patterns import recognize

S=('var',10); K=('var',11); D=('var',12)
i=('var',1); j=('var',2); x=('var',3); y=('var',4)
def ld(root,idx):return ('load',root,idx)
def add(a,b):return ('add',a,b)
def event(lhs,rhs,loop,width=1,**kw):
 return {'lhs':lhs,'rhs':rhs,'site':len(repr(rhs)),'loop':loop,'lhs_width':width,'byte_wrap':width==1,'lookup_byte_wrap':True,'zero_unit':True,'stepvar':1,**kw}
def valid():
 return [event(ld(S,i),i,1,loop_bound=256),event(j,add(add(j,ld(S,i)),ld(K,('umod',i,('num',13)))),2,loop_bound=256),event(ld(S,i),ld(S,j),2),event(ld(S,j),ld(S,i),2),event(x,add(x,('num',1)),3),event(y,add(y,ld(S,x)),3),event(ld(S,x),ld(S,y),3),event(ld(S,y),ld(S,x),3),event(ld(D,i),('xor',ld(D,i),ld(S,add(ld(S,x),ld(S,y)))),3)]
def test_connected_rc4():
 r=recognize(valid())[S]
 assert all(r[k] for k in ('identity','swap','ksa','prga','xor'))
def test_disconnected_features_do_not_confirm():
 es=valid()
 for n,e in enumerate(es):e['loop']=n
 r=recognize(es)[S]
 assert not r['ksa'] and not r['prga']
def test_wrong_accumulator_not_ksa():
 es=valid();es[1]['lhs']=('var',99)
 assert not recognize(es)[S]['ksa']
def test_xor_key_schedule_is_not_standard_rc4():
 es=valid();es[1]['rhs']=('xor',add(j,ld(S,i)),ld(K,('umod',i,('num',13))))
 assert not recognize(es)[S]['ksa']
def test_step_two_is_not_standard_stream():
 es=valid();es[4]['rhs']=add(x,('num',2))
 assert not recognize(es)[S]['prga']
def test_word_state_is_not_byte_rc4():
 es=valid()
 for e in es:
  e['lhs_width']=4;e['byte_wrap']=False
 assert S not in recognize(es)
def test_wrong_lookup_does_not_confirm():
 es=valid();es[-1]['rhs']=('xor',ld(D,i),ld(S,add(x,y)))
 assert not recognize(es)[S]['xor']
def test_unbounded_key_loop_not_full_schedule():
 es=valid();es[1]['loop_bound']=17
 assert not recognize(es)[S]['ksa']
def test_mask_256_is_not_byte_wrap():
 es=valid();es[1]['byte_wrap']=False;es[1]['rhs']=('band',es[1]['rhs'],('num',256))
 assert not recognize(es)[S]['ksa']
def test_modulo_255_is_not_byte_wrap():
 es=valid();es[1]['byte_wrap']=False;es[1]['rhs']=('umod',es[1]['rhs'],('num',255))
 assert not recognize(es)[S]['ksa']

def test_key_modulo_is_not_accumulator_wrap():
 es=valid();es[1]['byte_wrap']=False
 es[1]['rhs']=add(add(j,ld(S,i)),ld(K,('umod',i,('num',256))))
 assert not recognize(es)[S]['ksa']

def test_identity_must_use_induction_index():
 es=valid();q=('var',99);es[0]['lhs']=ld(S,q);es[0]['rhs']=q
 assert not recognize(es)[S]['identity']

def test_ksa_must_visit_full_induction_range():
 es=valid();es[1]['stepvar']=99
 assert not recognize(es)[S]['ksa']

def test_swaps_in_opposite_branches_do_not_connect():
 es=valid();es[1]['control']=((100,101),)
 es[2]['control']=es[3]['control']=((100,102),)
 assert not recognize(es)[S]['ksa']

def test_typed_unsigned_arithmetic_wraps_before_shift():
 from crypto_scan import _eval
 expression=('typedop','ushr',32,False,('typedop','add',32,False,('num',0xffffffff),('num',1)),('num',31))
 assert _eval(expression,{})==0

def test_typed_narrowing_precedes_shift():
 from crypto_scan import _eval
 assert _eval(('typedop','ushr',32,True,('castint',8,False,('num',260)),('num',1)),{})==2

def test_c_signed_division_truncates_toward_zero():
 from crypto_scan import _eval
 assert _eval(('sdiv',('num',-7),('num',3)),{})==-2
 assert _eval(('smod',('num',-7),('num',3)),{})==-1
```

`tests/test_crypto_preview.py`:

```py
"""Reference UI transform tested independently of recognition or target bytes."""
import json
from pathlib import Path
import sys

import pytest

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / 'plugin'))
from crypto_reference import rc4_transform


def test_rfc6229_vector():
    key = bytes.fromhex('0102030405')
    assert rc4_transform(key, bytes(16)).hex() == 'b2396305f03dc027ccc3524a0a1118a8'
    assert rc4_transform(key, bytes(16), drop=16).hex() == '6982944f18fc82d589c403a47a0d0919'


@pytest.mark.parametrize('case', json.loads((ROOT / 'tests/crypto_fixtures/manifest.json').read_text())['cases'], ids=lambda case: case['id'])
def test_independent_material_transform(case):
    assert rc4_transform(bytes.fromhex(case['key_hex']), bytes.fromhex(case['ciphertext_hex'])).hex() == case['plaintext_hex']


@pytest.mark.parametrize('key,data,drop', [(b'', b'x', 0), (b'k'*257, b'x', 0),
                                         (b'k', b'x', -1), (b'k', b'x', True),
                                         (b'k', b'x'*65537, 0)])
def test_preview_bounds(key, data, drop):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        rc4_transform(key, data, drop)
```

`tests/test_crypto_reference.py`:

```py
"""Independent fixture/native reference tests, without importing detection code."""
import importlib.util
import json
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess

import pytest

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "crypto_fixtures"
SPEC = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("fixture_build", ROOT / "build.py")
BUILD = importlib.util.module_from_spec(SPEC)
SPEC.loader.exec_module(BUILD)


def test_rc4_rfc6229_first_block():
    # RFC 6229 section 2, key length 40 bits, stream offset zero.
    assert BUILD.rc4(bytes.fromhex("0102030405"), bytes(16)).hex() == "b2396305f03dc027ccc3524a0a1118a8"


def test_rc4_empty_key_rejected():
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        BUILD.rc4(b"", b"anything")


@pytest.mark.parametrize("case", json.loads((ROOT / "manifest.json").read_text())["cases"], ids=lambda c: c["id"])
def test_native_fixture_matches_independent_reference(case):
    key = bytes.fromhex(case["key_hex"])
    ciphertext = bytes.fromhex(case["ciphertext_hex"])
    plaintext = bytes.fromhex(case["plaintext_hex"])
    if "raw_encoded_key_hex" in case:
        assert bytes(value ^ case["key_transform"]["mask"]
                     for value in bytes.fromhex(case["raw_encoded_key_hex"])) == key
    assert BUILD.rc4(key, ciphertext) == plaintext
    assert BUILD.rc4(key, plaintext) == ciphertext
    binary = ROOT / case["binary"]
    if not binary.is_file():
        pytest.skip("fixtures not built; run python3 tests/crypto_fixtures/build.py")
    native_expected = bytes.fromhex(case.get("native_output_hex", case["plaintext_hex"]))
    assert subprocess.check_output([str(binary)]) == native_expected
```

`tests/test_goal_validation.py`:

```py
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from types import SimpleNamespace
import pytest
sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / 'backend'))
from solve import validate_target_conditions

ARCH = SimpleNamespace(bits=64, registers={'eax': (16, 4), 'al': (16, 1)})


@pytest.mark.parametrize('value', [0, -1, 0xffffffff, -0x80000000])
def test_representable_goals(value):
    validate_target_conditions([{'register': 'eax', 'value': value}], ARCH, {0x1000})


@pytest.mark.parametrize('condition', [
    {'register': 'eax', 'value': 0x100000000}, {'register': 'al', 'value': 256},
    {'register': 'eax', 'value': -0x80000001}, {'register': 'eaz', 'value': 0},
    {'register': 'eax'}, {'bogus': 0}, {'memory': {'address': 0, 'hex': ''}},
    {'memory': {'address': 0xffffffffffffffff, 'hex': 'aabb'}},
])
def test_bad_goals_rejected(condition):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        validate_target_conditions([condition], ARCH, {0x1000})


def test_goal_without_address_rejected():
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        validate_target_conditions([{'register': 'eax', 'value': 0}], ARCH, set())
```

`tests/test_libc_differential.py`:

```py
"""Compare installed libc models with a compiled C oracle, including memory."""
import ctypes
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import sys

import angr
from angr import claripy
from cle.backends.externs.simdata.io_file import io_file_data_for_arch
import pytest

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))
from backend.solve import install_common_models


@pytest.fixture(scope='module')
def oracle(tmp_path_factory):
    binary = tmp_path_factory.mktemp('libc-differential') / 'oracle.so'
    subprocess.run(['cc', '-O0', '-fPIC', '-shared', '-fno-builtin',
                    str(ROOT / 'tests/libc_differential_fixture.c'), '-o', str(binary)], check=True)
    native = ctypes.CDLL(str(binary))
    native.oracle_fgets.argtypes = [ctypes.c_void_p, ctypes.c_size_t, ctypes.c_int,
                                   ctypes.c_void_p, ctypes.POINTER(ctypes.c_long)]
    native.oracle_fgets.restype = ctypes.c_int
    native.oracle_span.argtypes = [ctypes.c_void_p, ctypes.c_void_p]
    native.oracle_span.restype = ctypes.c_size_t
    for name in ('strcmp', 'strncmp', 'memcmp', 'bcmp'):
        func = getattr(native, 'oracle_' + name)
        func.argtypes = [ctypes.c_void_p, ctypes.c_void_p] + ([] if name == 'strcmp' else [ctypes.c_size_t])
        func.restype = ctypes.c_int
    project = angr.Project(str(binary), auto_load_libs=False)
    install_common_models(project, claripy)
    return native, project


def model(project, name):
    return project.hooked_by(project.loader.find_symbol(name).rebased_addr)


@pytest.mark.parametrize('symbolic', [False, True], ids=['concrete-file', 'fixed-symbolic-file'])
@pytest.mark.parametrize('data,size', [
    (b'', 8), (b'A', 8), (b'ABCD', 3), (b'ABCD', 5),
    (b'A\nB\n', 8), (b'\nABC', 8), (b'A\0B\nC', 8),
    (b'\xff\x80\nrest', 8), (b'abc', 0), (b'abc', -1),
    (b'', 1), (b'abc', 1), (b'a\nb', 2),
])
def test_fgets_matches_native_bytes_return_and_position(oracle, data, size, symbolic):
    native, project = oracle
    original = ctypes.create_string_buffer(data)
    output, position = ctypes.create_string_buffer(32), ctypes.c_long()
    expected = native.oracle_fgets(original, len(data), size, output, ctypes.byref(position))
    assert expected >= 0
    content = claripy.BVS('file_content', len(data) * 8) if symbolic and data else data
    state = project.factory.blank_state(stdin=angr.SimFileStream(name='stdin', content=content, has_end=True))
    if symbolic and data:
        state.solver.add(content == claripy.BVV(data))
    destination, file_pointer = 0x700000, 0x710000
    state.memory.store(destination, b'\xa5' * 32)
    fd_offset = io_file_data_for_arch(state.arch)['fd']
    state.memory.store(file_pointer + fd_offset, claripy.BVV(0, 32), endness=state.arch.memory_endness)
    result = model(project, 'fgets').execute(state, arguments=[
        claripy.BVV(destination, state.arch.bits), claripy.BVV(size, 32),
        claripy.BVV(file_pointer, state.arch.bits)]).ret_expr
    assert state.solver.satisfiable(), (data, size, symbolic, 'model rejected a valid read')
    actual = state.solver.eval(result)
    assert actual == (destination if expected == 1 else 0)
    assert state.solver.eval(state.memory.load(destination, 32), cast_to=bytes) == output.raw
    assert state.solver.eval(state.posix.stdin.pos) == position.value


@pytest.mark.parametrize('string,reject', [
    (b'', b''), (b'abc', b''), (b'abc', b'b'), (b'abc', b'cba'),
    (b'abc\0def', b'd'), (b'\xff\x80a', b'\x80'), (b'a\nb', b'\r\n'),
    (b'a' * 129 + b'z', b'z'),
])
def test_strcspn_matches_native(oracle, string, reject):
    native, project = oracle
    expected = native.oracle_span(ctypes.create_string_buffer(string), ctypes.create_string_buffer(reject))
    state = project.factory.blank_state()
    state.memory.store(0x700000, string + b'\0')
    state.memory.store(0x710000, reject + b'\0')
    result = model(project, 'strcspn').execute(state, arguments=[
        claripy.BVV(0x700000, state.arch.bits), claripy.BVV(0x710000, state.arch.bits)]).ret_expr
    assert state.solver.eval(result) == expected


@pytest.mark.parametrize('name', ['strcmp', 'strncmp', 'memcmp', 'bcmp'])
@pytest.mark.parametrize('left,right,count,alias', [
    (b'\xff\x01', b'\x01\xff', 2, False), (b'\x01\xff', b'\xff\x01', 2, False),
    (b'a\0z', b'a\0a', 3, False), (b'a', b'aa', 2, False),
    (b'abc', b'abz', 2, False), (b'abc', b'abz', 0, False),
    (b'abc', b'abc', 3, True),
])
def test_comparison_sign_or_equality_matches_native(oracle, name, left, right, count, alias):
    native, project = oracle
    lhs, rhs = ctypes.create_string_buffer(left), ctypes.create_string_buffer(right)
    expected = getattr(native, 'oracle_' + name)(lhs, lhs if alias else rhs,
                                               *([] if name == 'strcmp' else [count]))
    state = project.factory.blank_state()
    state.memory.store(0x700000, left + b'\0')
    state.memory.store(0x710000, right + b'\0')
    args = [claripy.BVV(0x700000, state.arch.bits),
            claripy.BVV(0x700000 if alias else 0x710000, state.arch.bits)]
    if name != 'strcmp':
        args.append(claripy.BVV(count, state.arch.bits))
    result = model(project, name).execute(state, arguments=args).ret_expr
    actual = state.solver.eval(result) & 0xffffffff
    actual = actual - 2**32 if actual & 0x80000000 else actual
    if name == 'bcmp':
        assert (actual == 0) == (expected == 0)
    else:
        assert (actual > 0) - (actual < 0) == (expected > 0) - (expected < 0)


def test_symbolic_first_newline_preserves_both_reads_and_alternatives(oracle):
    native, project = oracle
    first = claripy.BVS('first_byte', 8)
    content = claripy.Concat(first, claripy.BVV(b'B\n'))
    state = project.factory.blank_state(stdin=angr.SimFileStream(name='stdin', content=content, has_end=True))
    state.solver.add(claripy.Or(first == 10, first == ord('A')))
    destinations, file_pointer = (0x700000, 0x700100), 0x710000
    fd_offset = io_file_data_for_arch(state.arch)['fd']
    state.memory.store(file_pointer + fd_offset, claripy.BVV(0, 32), endness=state.arch.memory_endness)
    results = []
    for destination in destinations:
        state.memory.store(destination, b'\xa5' * 32)
        results.append(model(project, 'fgets').execute(state, arguments=[
            claripy.BVV(destination, state.arch.bits), claripy.BVV(8, 32),
            claripy.BVV(file_pointer, state.arch.bits)]).ret_expr)
    for choice in (10, ord('A')):
        branch = state.copy()
        branch.solver.add(first == choice)
        assert branch.solver.satisfiable()
        data, consumed = bytes([choice]) + b'B\n', 0
        for destination, result in zip(destinations, results):
            output, position = ctypes.create_string_buffer(32), ctypes.c_long()
            expected = native.oracle_fgets(ctypes.create_string_buffer(data[consumed:]),
                                          len(data) - consumed, 8, output, ctypes.byref(position))
            assert branch.solver.eval(result) == (destination if expected else 0)
            assert branch.solver.eval(branch.memory.load(destination, 32), cast_to=bytes) == output.raw
            consumed += position.value
        assert branch.solver.eval(branch.posix.stdin.pos) == consumed


def test_strcspn_after_mutating_a_tracked_fgets_buffer(oracle):
    native, project = oracle
    state = project.factory.blank_state(stdin=angr.SimFileStream(name='stdin', content=b'A\n', has_end=True))
    dst, file_pointer, reject = 0x700000, 0x710000, 0x720000
    state.memory.store(dst, b'\0' * 32)
    fd_offset = io_file_data_for_arch(state.arch)['fd']
    state.memory.store(file_pointer + fd_offset, claripy.BVV(0, 32), endness=state.arch.memory_endness)
    model(project, 'fgets').execute(state, arguments=[claripy.BVV(dst, state.arch.bits),
        claripy.BVV(8, 32), claripy.BVV(file_pointer, state.arch.bits)])
    state.memory.store(dst, b'ABCD\0')
    state.memory.store(reject, b'D\0')
    result = model(project, 'strcspn').execute(state, arguments=[
        claripy.BVV(dst, state.arch.bits), claripy.BVV(reject, state.arch.bits)]).ret_expr
    expected = native.oracle_span(ctypes.create_string_buffer(b'ABCD'), ctypes.create_string_buffer(b'D'))
    assert state.solver.eval(result) == expected


@pytest.mark.parametrize('name', ['strcmp', 'strncmp'])
def test_string_comparison_stops_at_shorter_terminator_before_unmapped_page(oracle, name):
    native, project = oracle
    left, right = b'a', b'a' * 64
    expected = getattr(native, 'oracle_' + name)(ctypes.create_string_buffer(left),
        ctypes.create_string_buffer(right), *([] if name == 'strcmp' else [65]))
    state = project.factory.blank_state(add_options={angr.options.STRICT_PAGE_ACCESS})
    state.memory.map_region(0x9000000, 4096, 3)
    state.memory.map_region(0x9100000, 4096, 3)
    state.memory.store(0x9000ffe, left + b'\0')
    state.memory.store(0x9100000, right + b'\0')
    args = [claripy.BVV(0x9000ffe, state.arch.bits), claripy.BVV(0x9100000, state.arch.bits)]
    if name == 'strncmp':
        args.append(claripy.BVV(65, state.arch.bits))
    result = model(project, name).execute(state, arguments=args).ret_expr
    actual = ctypes.c_int(state.solver.eval(result)).value
    assert (actual > 0) - (actual < 0) == (expected > 0) - (expected < 0)
```

`tests/test_libc_models.py`:

```py
"""Native libc behavior and branch-merging regressions for common models."""
import ctypes
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import sys
from types import SimpleNamespace

import angr
from angr import claripy
import pytest

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))
from backend.libc_models import GlibcRandomState, glibc_random_import, linux_anonymous_mmap_supported
from backend.solve import install_common_models, solve


@pytest.fixture(scope='module')
def rand_project(tmp_path_factory):
    binary = tmp_path_factory.mktemp('rand-models') / 'fixture'
    subprocess.run(['cc', '-O0', str(ROOT / 'tests/rand_fixture.c'), '-o', str(binary)], check=True)
    project = angr.Project(str(binary), auto_load_libs=False)
    install_common_models(project, claripy)
    return project


@pytest.mark.parametrize('seed', [0, 1, 2, 45483068, 0x7fffffff, 0x80000000, 0xffffffff])
def test_random_sequence_matches_native_glibc(rand_project, seed):
    relocation = rand_project.loader.main_object.imports['rand']
    assert glibc_random_import(rand_project, relocation)
    libc = ctypes.CDLL(None)
    libc.srand.argtypes = [ctypes.c_uint]
    libc.rand.restype = ctypes.c_int
    libc.srand(seed)
    expected = [libc.rand() for _ in range(8)]
    state = GlibcRandomState()
    state.seed(claripy.BVV(seed, 32))
    solver = claripy.Solver()
    assert [solver.eval(state.draw(), 1)[0] for _ in expected] == expected


def test_branch_seed_and_draw_count_survive_merge(rand_project):
    left = rand_project.factory.blank_state()
    right = left.copy()
    left.register_plugin('arsenal_glibc_random', GlibcRandomState())
    right.register_plugin('arsenal_glibc_random', GlibcRandomState())
    right.arsenal_glibc_random.seed(claripy.BVV(2, 32))
    right.arsenal_glibc_random.draw()
    merged, conditions, _ = left.merge(right)
    value = merged.arsenal_glibc_random.draw()
    assert merged.solver.eval(value, extra_constraints=[conditions[0]]) == 1804289383
    assert merged.solver.eval(value, extra_constraints=[conditions[1]]) == 1738766719
    assert set(merged.solver.eval_upto(value, 3)) == {1804289383, 1738766719}
    # Merging must not mutate the predecessor's random sequence.
    assert left.solver.eval(left.arsenal_glibc_random.draw()) == 1804289383


def test_merge_with_no_previous_random_call_uses_default_seed(rand_project):
    unused = rand_project.factory.blank_state()
    seeded = unused.copy()
    seeded.register_plugin('arsenal_glibc_random', GlibcRandomState())
    seeded.arsenal_glibc_random.seed(claripy.BVV(2, 32))
    merged, conditions, _ = unused.merge(seeded)
    value = merged.arsenal_glibc_random.draw()
    assert merged.solver.eval(value, extra_constraints=[conditions[0]]) == 1804289383
    assert merged.solver.eval(value, extra_constraints=[conditions[1]]) == 1505335290


def test_platform_and_libc_evidence_required(rand_project, monkeypatch):
    relocation = rand_project.loader.main_object.imports['rand']
    assert linux_anonymous_mmap_supported(rand_project)
    unknown_version = SimpleNamespace(symbol=SimpleNamespace(version=None))
    assert not glibc_random_import(rand_project, unknown_version)
    windows = SimpleNamespace(loader=rand_project.loader, simos=SimpleNamespace(name='Win32'),
                              arch=rand_project.arch)
    assert not glibc_random_import(windows, relocation)
    assert not linux_anonymous_mmap_supported(windows)
    mips = SimpleNamespace(loader=rand_project.loader, simos=rand_project.simos,
                           arch=SimpleNamespace(name='MIPS32'))
    assert not linux_anonymous_mmap_supported(mips)
    monkeypatch.setattr(rand_project.loader.main_object, 'deps', [])
    assert not glibc_random_import(rand_project, relocation)


@pytest.fixture(scope='module')
def fgets_binary(tmp_path_factory):
    directory = tmp_path_factory.mktemp('fgets-model')
    source = directory / 'fixture.c'
    source.write_text(r'''
#include <stdio.h>
int main(int argc, char **argv) {
    char buffer[4] = {'X', 'Y', 'Z', 0};
    char mode = argc > 1 ? argv[1][0] : 'p';
    FILE *stream = mode == 'e' ? fopen("/empty", "r") : stdin;
    if (!stream) return 2;
    int size = mode == 'z' ? 0 : mode == 'n' ? -1 : mode == 'o' ? 1 : sizeof(buffer);
    char *result = fgets(buffer, size, stream);
    int ok = mode == 'e' || mode == 'z' || mode == 'n'
        ? result == NULL && buffer[0] == 'X'
        : result == buffer && buffer[0] == (mode == 'o' ? 0 : 'A');
    puts(ok ? "FGETS_OK" : "FGETS_BAD");
    return !ok;
}
''')
    binary = directory / 'fixture'
    subprocess.run(['cc', '-O0', str(source), '-o', str(binary)], check=True)
    return binary


@pytest.mark.parametrize('mode', ['p', 'e', 'z', 'n', 'o'])
def test_fgets_pointer_eof_and_size_semantics(fgets_binary, mode):
    request = dict(binary=str(fgets_binary), mode='stdin', length=2,
                   prefix='A\n', printable=False, argv_before=[mode],
                   files=[dict(path='/empty', hex='')], stdout_find='FGETS_OK',
                   stdout_avoid='FGETS_BAD', timeout=30)
    result = solve(request)
    assert result['status'] == 'found', result
    if mode != 'e':
        native = subprocess.run([str(fgets_binary), mode], input=b'A\n', capture_output=True, timeout=5)
        assert native.stdout == b'FGETS_OK\n'
        assert native.returncode == 0


@pytest.fixture(scope='module')
def random_family_binary(tmp_path_factory):
    directory = tmp_path_factory.mktemp('random-family')
    source = directory / 'fixture.c'
    source.write_text(r'''
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
int main(int argc, char **argv) {
    char storage[256] = {0};
    if (argc > 1 && argv[1][0] == 'i') { initstate(1, storage, 8); puts("MIX_OK"); return 0; }
    if (argc > 1 && argv[1][0] == 's') { setstate(storage); puts("MIX_OK"); return 0; }
    srand(1);
    int first = random() == 1804289383 && rand() == 846930886;
    srandom(2);
    int second = rand() == 1505335290 && random() == 1738766719;
    puts(first && second ? "MIX_OK" : "MIX_BAD");
    return !(first && second);
}
''')
    binary = directory / 'fixture'
    subprocess.run(['cc', '-O0', str(source), '-o', str(binary)], check=True)
    return binary


def test_random_and_rand_share_seed_and_draw_state(random_family_binary):
    result = solve(dict(binary=str(random_family_binary), mode='stdin', length=1,
                        stdout_find='MIX_OK', stdout_avoid='MIX_BAD', timeout=30))
    assert result['status'] == 'found', result
    native = subprocess.run([str(random_family_binary)], capture_output=True, timeout=5)
    assert native.stdout == b'MIX_OK\n'
    assert native.returncode == 0


@pytest.mark.parametrize('mode', ['i', 's'])
def test_state_layout_changes_are_explicitly_unsupported(random_family_binary, mode):
    result = solve(dict(binary=str(random_family_binary), mode='stdin', length=1,
                        argv_before=[mode], stdout_find='MIX_OK', timeout=30))
    assert result['status'] == 'incomplete', result
    assert any('state layout' in error for error in result['errors'])
```

`tests/test_outcome_scan.py`:

```py
"""False-positive guards for output suggestions; no IDA or sample execution."""
import sys
from pathlib import Path
import pytest

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / 'plugin'))
from outcome_scan import classify_message, literal_printf, rank_candidates, output_api


@pytest.mark.parametrize('text,kind', [
    ('Correct!', 'success'), ('Congratulations, you win!', 'success'),
    ('Access granted\n', 'success'), ('Welcome, admin!', 'success'),
    ('incorrect', 'failure'), ('not correct', 'failure'),
    ('Flag not accepted', 'failure'), ('Correct! No, wrong.', 'failure'),
    ('Enter the flag: ', 'prompt'), ('Please enter your password', 'prompt'),
    ('Password: ', 'prompt'), ('Enter the correct flag:', 'prompt'),
])
def test_wording(text, kind):
    assert classify_message(text)['kind'] == kind


@pytest.mark.parametrize('text', [
    'correctness', 'success_count', 'Correct syntax: ./challenge input',
    'Success rate: %d', 'The answer should be correct', 'Successfully allocated memory',
    'correct answer is required', '', '\x01Correct', 'x' * 513,
])
def test_not_outcome(text):
    assert classify_message(text) is None


def candidate(message, kind, function=1, literal=True):
    return dict(message=message, kind=kind, function=function, site=10,
                literal_stdout=literal, evidence=[], score=80)


def test_substring_collision():
    rows = [candidate('correct', 'success'), candidate('incorrect', 'failure')]
    assert rank_candidates(rows) is None
    assert rows[0]['stdout_find'] is None


def test_unique_supported_pair():
    rows = [candidate('Correct!', 'success'), candidate('Wrong!', 'failure')]
    assert rank_candidates(rows) == 'Correct!'
    assert all(item['goal_address'] is None for item in rows)


def test_ambiguity_and_reference_only():
    rows = [candidate('Correct!', 'success'), candidate('Accepted!', 'success'), candidate('Wrong!', 'failure')]
    assert rank_candidates(rows) is None
    rows = [candidate('Correct!', 'success', literal=False), candidate('Wrong!', 'failure')]
    assert rank_candidates(rows) is None


def test_no_inferred_branch_for_unrelated_messages():
    assert rank_candidates([candidate('Correct!', 'success'), candidate('Wrong!', 'failure', function=2)]) is None


def test_printf_interpolation():
    assert literal_printf('Correct!') == 'Correct!'
    assert literal_printf('Success! 100%%') == 'Success! 100%'
    assert literal_printf('Correct: %s') is None
    assert literal_printf('Correct: %n') is None


@pytest.mark.parametrize('name,expected', [('puts','puts'), ('_puts','puts'),
    ('__imp__puts','puts'), ('printf@@GLIBC_2.2.5','printf'), ('__printf_chk','__printf_chk'),
    ('_printf@8','printf'), ('my_puts',None), ('puts_fake',None)])
def test_output_import_decorations(name,expected):
    assert output_api(name) == expected
```

`tests/test_protection_scan.py`:

```py
"""Evidence boundaries and real UPX extraction without executing the target."""
import hashlib
from pathlib import Path
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import threading

import pytest

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / 'plugin'))
from protection_scan import (entropy, executable_format, function_observations,
                             sample_ranges, section_observations, unpack_upx)


def section(**updates):
    value = dict(name='.text', start=0x1000, end=0x2000, size=4096, writable=False,
                 executable=True, entropy=5.0, sample_bytes=4096)
    value.update(updates)
    return value


def test_entropy_and_sample_bounds():
    assert entropy(b'') is None
    assert entropy(b'x' * 1000) == 0
    assert entropy(bytes(range(256)) * 8) == 8
    for size in (1, 100, 65536, 65537, 1 << 40):
        ranges = sample_ranges(123, size)
        assert sum(length for _, length in ranges) <= 65536
        assert ranges[0][0] == 123
        assert ranges[-1][0] + ranges[-1][1] <= 123 + size
        assert all(a + length <= b for (a, length), (b, _) in zip(ranges, ranges[1:]))
    assert sample_ranges(1, 1000, budget=0) == []


def test_entropy_alone_never_names_a_packer():
    findings = section_observations([section(entropy=7.99)], 0x1100)
    assert {f['kind'] for f in findings} == {'High byte entropy', 'Entry segment observation'}
    assert all('UPX' not in f['kind'] for f in findings)
    assert not section_observations([section(entropy=7.99, sample_bytes=128)])


def test_named_pair_and_rwx_are_independent_observations():
    findings = section_observations([section(name='UPX0', writable=True),
                                    section(name='UPX1', start=0x3000, end=0x4000)])
    assert {f['kind'] for f in findings} == {'UPX section-name candidate', 'Writable executable segment'}
    assert not section_observations([section(name='UPX1')])
    assert not section_observations([section(name='copy_UPX0'), section(name='UPX1')])


def test_dispatcher_and_indirect_branch_thresholds():
    blocks = [{'start': n, 'successors': [0] if n in (1, 2, 3, 4) else []} for n in range(8)]
    blocks[0]['successors'] = [1, 2, 3]
    findings = function_observations(0, 'dispatcher', blocks, [5, 6, 7])
    assert {f['kind'] for f in findings} == {'Dispatch-shaped control flow', 'Repeated indirect branches'}
    blocks[0]['successors'] = [1, 2]
    assert not function_observations(0, 'ordinary', blocks, [5, 6])
    # Duplicate CFG edges cannot inflate a candidate's predecessor count.
    blocks = [{'start': n, 'successors': [0, 0, 0, 0] if n == 1 else []} for n in range(8)]
    blocks[0]['successors'] = [1, 2, 3]
    assert not function_observations(0, 'ordinary', blocks, [])


@pytest.fixture
def compiled(tmp_path):
    compiler = shutil.which('gcc')
    if not compiler:
        pytest.skip('gcc is unavailable')
    source = tmp_path / 'fixture.c'
    source.write_text('#include <stdio.h>\nint main(void) { puts("owned protection fixture"); return 0; }\n')
    binary = tmp_path / 'fixture'
    subprocess.run([compiler, '-O2', '-o', str(binary), str(source)], check=True, capture_output=True)
    return binary


@pytest.fixture
def packed(compiled):
    tool = shutil.which('upx')
    if not tool:
        pytest.skip('UPX is unavailable')
    packed = compiled.with_name('packed')
    subprocess.run([tool, '-1', '--exact', '--no-progress', '-o', str(packed), str(compiled)],
                   check=True, capture_output=True)
    return compiled, packed


def test_upx_roundtrip_preserves_input_and_validates_output(packed):
    original, compressed = packed
    before = compressed.read_bytes()
    output = compressed.with_name('extracted')
    result = unpack_upx(compressed, output)
    assert compressed.read_bytes() == before
    assert output.read_bytes() == original.read_bytes()
    assert result['source_sha256'] == hashlib.sha256(before).hexdigest()
    assert result['output_sha256'] == hashlib.sha256(output.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
    assert result['format'] == 'ELF64'
    assert output.stat().st_mode & 0o777 == 0o600
    assert not list(output.parent.glob('.arsenal-upx-*'))


def test_existing_output_and_symlink_are_not_overwritten(packed):
    _, compressed = packed
    output = compressed.with_name('existing')
    output.write_bytes(b'analyst notes')
    with pytest.raises(FileExistsError):
        unpack_upx(compressed, output)
    assert output.read_bytes() == b'analyst notes'
    alias = compressed.with_name('alias')
    alias.symlink_to(compressed.with_name('absent'))
    with pytest.raises(FileExistsError):
        unpack_upx(compressed, alias)
    assert not alias.resolve().exists()


def test_unpacked_input_fails_cleanly(compiled):
    if not shutil.which('upx'):
        pytest.skip('UPX is unavailable')
    before = compiled.read_bytes()
    output = compiled.with_name('unpacked-again')
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match='UPX failed'):
        unpack_upx(compiled, output)
    assert compiled.read_bytes() == before
    assert not output.exists()
    assert not list(output.parent.glob('.arsenal-upx-*'))


def test_cancellation_does_not_publish(packed):
    _, compressed = packed
    cancelled = threading.Event()
    cancelled.set()
    output = compressed.with_name('cancelled')
    with pytest.raises(InterruptedError):
        unpack_upx(compressed, output, cancel=cancelled)
    assert not output.exists()
    assert not list(output.parent.glob('.arsenal-upx-*'))


def test_truncated_executable_headers_rejected(tmp_path):
    path = tmp_path / 'bad'
    for data in (b'\x7fELF', b'MZ' + b'\0' * 62, b'not an executable'):
        path.write_bytes(data)
        with pytest.raises(ValueError):
            executable_format(path)

```

`tests/test_smt_predicates.py`:

```py
"""Semantic predicate proofs, wraparound counterexamples, and vacuity guards."""
import json
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import sys

import pytest

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / 'backend'))
from smt_solver import solve_smt

BASE = '(declare-const x (_ BitVec 8))'
EVEN_PRODUCT = '(= (bvand (bvmul x (bvadd x #x01)) #x01) #x00)'


def check(expression, assumptions=BASE):
    return solve_smt({'smtlib': assumptions, 'predicate': expression, 'timeout': 5})


def test_even_product_is_constant_without_restricting_input():
    result = check(EVEN_PRODUCT)
    assert result['classification'] == 'always_true'
    assert result['checks']['assumptions']['status'] == 'sat'
    assert result['checks']['when_false']['status'] == 'unsat'
    assert result['checks']['when_true']['witness']['x']['bits'] == 8
    assert all((x * (x + 1)) & 1 == 0 for x in range(256))


def test_negated_identity_is_always_false():
    result = check('(not ' + EVEN_PRODUCT + ')')
    assert result['classification'] == 'always_false'
    assert result['checks']['when_true']['status'] == 'unsat'


def test_signed_overflow_disproves_apparent_monotonicity():
    result = check('(bvsgt (bvadd x #x01) x)')
    assert result['classification'] == 'both_feasible'
    false_value = int(result['checks']['when_false']['witness']['x']['hex'], 16)
    true_value = int(result['checks']['when_true']['witness']['x']['hex'], 16)
    assert false_value == 127
    signed = lambda x: x if x < 128 else x - 256
    assert signed((true_value + 1) & 255) > signed(true_value)
    assert not signed((false_value + 1) & 255) > signed(false_value)


def test_unsigned_wraparound_and_precondition_change_proof():
    predicate = '(bvugt (bvadd x #x01) x)'
    general = check(predicate)
    assert general['classification'] == 'both_feasible'
    assert general['checks']['when_false']['witness']['x']['hex'] == 'ff'
    bounded = check(predicate, BASE + ' (assert (bvult x #xff))')
    assert bounded['classification'] == 'always_true'


def test_contradictory_assumptions_are_unreachable_not_a_branch_proof():
    result = check(EVEN_PRODUCT, BASE + ' (assert (= x #x00)) (assert (= x #x01))')
    assert result['classification'] == 'unreachable'
    assert result['checks']['when_true']['status'] == 'not_run'
    assert result['checks']['when_false']['status'] == 'not_run'


def test_variables_only_in_predicate_and_quoted_names():
    result = check('flag', '(declare-const flag Bool)')
    assert result['classification'] == 'both_feasible'
    assert result['checks']['when_true']['witness']['flag']['value'] == 'True'
    assert result['checks']['when_false']['witness']['flag']['value'] == 'False'
    assert check('|a(b)|', '(declare-const |a(b)| Bool)')['classification'] == 'both_feasible'


def test_arrays_are_reported_in_branch_witnesses():
    result = check('(= (select a #x00) #x42)', '(declare-const a (Array (_ BitVec 8) (_ BitVec 8)))')
    assert result['classification'] == 'both_feasible'
    assert 'a' in result['checks']['when_true']['other_model_values']
    assert 'a' in result['checks']['when_false']['other_model_values']


@pytest.mark.parametrize('expression', ['', 'true) (assert false', 'true) (check-sat) (assert true',
                                       'true) (set-info :status sat);', 4])
def test_predicate_rejects_commands_and_missing_expression(expression):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        check(expression)


def test_expired_budget_is_inconclusive(monkeypatch):
    import smt_solver
    clock = iter([0.0, 10.0, 10.0])
    monkeypatch.setattr(smt_solver.time, 'monotonic', lambda: next(clock))
    result = check(EVEN_PRODUCT)
    assert result['status'] == 'unknown'
    assert result['classification'] == 'inconclusive'
    assert result['checks']['assumptions']['status'] == 'unknown'
    assert result['checks']['when_false']['status'] == 'not_run'


def test_predicate_supervised_cli(tmp_path):
    request, result = tmp_path / 'request.json', tmp_path / 'result.json'
    request.write_text(json.dumps({'engine': 'z3', 'smtlib': BASE, 'predicate': EVEN_PRODUCT, 'timeout': 5}))
    subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(ROOT / 'backend/solve.py'), str(request), str(result)], check=True, timeout=15)
    assert json.loads(result.read_text())['classification'] == 'always_true'


def test_classifications_match_exhaustive_native_byte_oracle(tmp_path):
    binary = tmp_path / 'predicates'
    subprocess.run(['cc', '-O2', str(ROOT / 'tests/predicate_fixture.c'), '-o', str(binary)], check=True)
    counts = list(map(int, subprocess.check_output([str(binary)], text=True).split()))
    expressions = [EVEN_PRODUCT, '(bvsgt (bvadd x #x01) x)', '(bvugt (bvadd x #x01) x)']
    for expression, true_count in zip(expressions, counts):
        expected = 'always_true' if true_count == 256 else 'always_false' if true_count == 0 else 'both_feasible'
        assert check(expression)['classification'] == expected
    assert counts == [256, 255, 255]
```

`tests/test_smt_solver.py`:

```py
"""SMT assertions, bounded CLI execution and exact angr snapshot interoperability."""
import json
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import sys

import pytest

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / 'backend'))
from smt_solver import export_state, solve_smt, validate_script


def test_joint_models_and_bitvector_wrap():
    result = solve_smt({'smtlib': '''
      (declare-const x (_ BitVec 8)) (declare-const y (_ BitVec 8))
      (assert (= (bvadd x y) #x03))
      (assert (= x #xff)) (assert (= y #x04))''', 'timeout': 5})
    assert result['status'] == 'sat'
    assert result['models'][0]['x']['hex'] == 'ff'
    assert result['models'][0]['y']['hex'] == '04'


def test_unsat_core_contains_contradiction():
    result = solve_smt({'smtlib': '''(declare-const x Int)
      (assert (> x 10)) (assert (< x 5)) (assert (< x 100))'''})
    assert result['status'] == 'unsat'
    assert result['models'] == []
    assert {0, 1} <= set(result['unsat_core_assertions'])


def test_enumeration_is_joint_and_exhaustive_for_small_domain():
    result = solve_smt({'smtlib': '(declare-const x (_ BitVec 1)) (assert (= x x))', 'max_models': 3})
    assert result['status'] == 'sat'
    assert result['enumeration_status'] == 'unsat'
    assert {m['x']['hex'] for m in result['models']} == {'0', '1'}


@pytest.mark.parametrize('command', ['(reset)', '(pop 1)', '(push 1)', '(set-option :timeout 0)',
                                     '(check-sat-using smt)', '(exit)', '(echo "x")'])
def test_state_mutating_and_option_commands_are_rejected(command):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match='Unsupported'):
        validate_script(command)


def test_scanner_handles_quoted_symbols_and_comments():
    result = solve_smt({'smtlib': '''; (reset) ignored comment
      (declare-const |strange(name)| Int)
      (assert (= |strange(name)| 3))
      (check-sat) (get-model)'''})
    assert result['status'] == 'sat'
    assert result['models'][0]['strange(name)']['value'] == '3'


def test_invalid_and_oversize_scripts_fail():
    for script in ['', ')', '(assert true', '(assert "unclosed)', 'x', ';' + 'a' * (4 * 1024 * 1024)]:
        with pytest.raises(ValueError):
            validate_script(script)


def test_snapshot_preserves_correlated_bytes_and_goal_label(tmp_path):
    import angr
    from angr import claripy
    state = angr.load_shellcode(b'\xc3', arch='AMD64').factory.blank_state()
    a, b = claripy.BVS('arsenal_input_a', 8), claripy.BVS('arsenal_input_b', 8)
    state.regs.ip = 0x1234
    state.solver.add(a + b == 5, a == 255)
    path = tmp_path / 'snapshot.smt2'
    snapshot = export_state(state, [a, b], [{'name': 'a', 'length': 1}, {'name': 'b', 'length': 1}], path)
    assert snapshot['reached_goal'] is False
    assert 'goal NOT asserted' in path.read_text()
    result = solve_smt({'smtlib': path.read_text()})
    assert result['status'] == 'sat'
    values = [result['models'][0][item['symbol']]['hex'] for item in snapshot['inputs']]
    assert values == ['ff', '06']
    export_state(state, [a], [{'name': 'a', 'length': 1}], path, reached_goal=True)
    assert 'reached goal' in path.read_text()


def test_z3_through_bounded_supervisor(tmp_path):
    request, output = tmp_path / 'request.json', tmp_path / 'result.json'
    request.write_text(json.dumps({'engine': 'z3', 'smtlib': '(assert false)', 'timeout': 5}))
    subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(ROOT / 'backend/solve.py'), str(request), str(output)], check=True, timeout=15)
    result = json.loads(output.read_text())
    assert result['status'] == 'unsat'
    assert result['unsat_core_assertions'] == [0]


def test_array_model_is_reported_without_scalar_enumeration():
    result = solve_smt({'smtlib': '''(declare-const a (Array (_ BitVec 8) (_ BitVec 8)))
        (assert (= (select a #x00) #x42))''', 'max_models': 3})
    assert result['status'] == 'sat'
    assert result['models'] == [{}]
    assert 'a' in result['other_model_values'][0]
    assert not result['other_model_values'][0]['a']['truncated']


def test_tracking_names_cannot_collide_with_user_symbols_in_fresh_worker(tmp_path):
    request, output = tmp_path / 'tracking-request.json', tmp_path / 'tracking-result.json'
    request.write_text(json.dumps({'engine': 'z3', 'timeout': 5,
        'smtlib': '(declare-const arsenal_assertion!0 Bool) (assert (not arsenal_assertion!0))'}))
    subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(ROOT / 'backend/solve.py'), str(request), str(output)], check=True, timeout=15)
    result = json.loads(output.read_text())
    assert result['status'] == 'sat', result
    assert result['models'][0]['arsenal_assertion!0']['value'] == 'False'


def test_overloaded_symbol_is_explicit_instead_of_losing_a_model_value():
    script = '''(declare-fun x () Int) (declare-fun x () Bool)
        (assert (= (as x Int) 7)) (assert (as x Bool))'''
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match='Overloaded SMT symbol'):
        solve_smt({'smtlib': script})
```

`tests/test_solver_diagnostics.py`:

```py
"""End-to-end solver status and Z3 snapshot regressions."""
import json
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import sys

import pytest

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / 'backend'))
from solve import solve
from smt_solver import solve_smt


@pytest.fixture
def solver_request(tmp_path):
    source, binary = tmp_path / 'check.c', tmp_path / 'check'
    source.write_text('#include <stdio.h>\n#include <unistd.h>\nint main(void){char c; if(read(0,&c,1)==1 && c==71) puts("GOOD"); return 0;}\n')
    subprocess.run(['cc', '-O0', '-fPIE', '-pie', str(source), '-o', str(binary)], check=True)
    return {'binary': str(binary), 'mode': 'stdin', 'length': 1, 'stdout_find': 'GOOD',
            'timeout': 15, 'smt_export': str(tmp_path / 'constraints.smt2'), 'solver_timeout_ms': 1234}


def test_found_snapshot_resolves_same_input(solver_request):
    result = solve(solver_request)
    assert result['status'] == 'found', result
    assert result['input_hex'] == '47'
    snapshot = result['smt_snapshot']
    assert snapshot['reached_goal'] is True
    smt = solve_smt({'smtlib': Path(snapshot['path']).read_text()})
    assert smt['status'] == 'sat'
    assert smt['models'][0][snapshot['inputs'][0]['symbol']]['hex'] == '47'
    assert result['stats']['solver_timeout_ms'] == 1234


def test_interrupted_paths_are_incomplete_and_exportable(solver_request, monkeypatch):
    import angr

    def interrupt(manager, **kwargs):
        state = manager.active[0]
        assert state.solver._solver.timeout == 1234
        manager.stashes['interrupted'] = [state]
        manager.stashes['active'] = []
        return manager

    monkeypatch.setattr(angr.SimulationManager, 'step', interrupt)
    result = solve(solver_request)
    assert result['status'] == 'incomplete', result
    assert result['stats']['interrupted'] == 1
    assert result['smt_snapshot']['reached_goal'] is False
    assert 'goal NOT asserted' in Path(solver_request['smt_export']).read_text()
    assert any('feasibility is unknown' in message for message in result['warnings'])


def test_snapshot_never_overwrites_unrelated_file(solver_request):
    path = Path(solver_request['smt_export'])
    path.write_text('user-owned constraint file')
    result = solve(solver_request)
    assert result['status'] == 'found'
    assert path.read_text() == 'user-owned constraint file'
    assert 'smt_snapshot' not in result
    assert any('Refusing to overwrite' in message for message in result['warnings'])


def test_direct_query_interrupt_is_incomplete(solver_request, monkeypatch):
    import solve as backend
    from angr import claripy

    def interrupted_output(state):
        try:
            raise claripy.ClaripySolverInterruptError('timeout')
        except claripy.ClaripySolverInterruptError as cause:
            raise RuntimeError('wrapper preserves cause') from cause

    monkeypatch.setattr(backend, 'concrete_stdout', interrupted_output)
    result = solve(solver_request)
    assert result['status'] == 'incomplete'
    assert result['reason'] == 'solver_query_interrupted'
    assert 'timeout' in result['error']
    assert 'input_hex' not in result

```

`tests/test_triage_rules.py`:

```py
"""Classifier policy tests independent of IDA, including rejected near matches."""
from pathlib import Path
import sys

import pytest

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "plugin"))
from triage_rules import normalize_api, rules_for


@pytest.mark.parametrize("name,expected", [
    ("read", "read"), ("read@GLIBC_2.2.5", "read"), ("read@@GLIBC_2.2.5", "read"),
    (".read", "read"), ("__imp__ReadFile@20", "ReadFile"), ("_strcmp", "strcmp"),
    ("__isoc99_scanf", "__isoc99_scanf"), ("j_ReadFile", "ReadFile"),
    ("__isoc23_scanf", "__isoc23_scanf"), ("__isoc23_fscanf", "__isoc23_fscanf"),
])
def test_known_decorations(name, expected):
    assert normalize_api(name) == expected
    assert rules_for(expected)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("name", [
    "pthread_create", "connectivity_setup", "read_configuration", "my_read",
    "decrypt_strings", "cryptography_init", "my_strcmp", "fread_wrapper",
    "send_report", "strstr", "ReadFileSomething", "READ", "aes_helper",
])
def test_substrings_not_behavior(name):
    assert not rules_for(normalize_api(name))


def test_debugger_query_requires_correct_request():
    assert rules_for("ptrace")[0]["category"] == "Process tracing"
    assert rules_for("ptrace", {0: 16})[0]["category"] == "Process tracing"
    assert rules_for("ptrace", {0: 0})[0]["category"] == "Debugger checks"
    assert rules_for("NtQueryInformationProcess", {1: 0})[0]["category"] == "Process information"
    assert rules_for("NtQueryInformationProcess", {1: 7})[0]["category"] == "Debugger checks"


def test_comparison_does_not_imply_input_check():
    assert [r["category"] for r in rules_for("strcmp")] == ["Data comparison"]


def test_network_input_keeps_both_roles():
    assert {r["category"] for r in rules_for("recv")} == {"Network", "Input taking"}


@pytest.mark.parametrize("name", ["RC4", "RC4_set_key"])
def test_imported_rc4_crypto_names(name):
    assert [rule["category"] for rule in rules_for(name)] == ["Cryptography"]

```
